FILE: diagnose.js
================================================
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

console.log("--- 🕵️ INVESTIGACIÓN DE DEPENDENCIAS ---");
console.log(`Directorio actual: ${process.cwd()}`);
console.log(`Versión de Node: ${process.version}`);
console.log(`Plataforma: ${process.platform}`);

const pathsToCheck = [
  "node_modules/next/package.json",
  "node_modules/next/dist/compiled/cookie/index.js",
  "node_modules/next/dist/server/api-utils/get-cookie-parser.js",
];

pathsToCheck.forEach((p) => {
  const fullPath = path.join(__dirname, p);
  if (fs.existsSync(fullPath)) {
    console.log(`✅ EXISTE: ${p}`);
  } else {
    console.log(`❌ NO ENCONTRADO: ${p}`);

    const parent = path.dirname(fullPath);
    if (fs.existsSync(parent)) {
      console.log(
        `   📂 Contenido de ${path.dirname(p)}:`,
        fs.readdirSync(parent),
      );
    }
  }
});
console.log("---------------------------------------");

FILE: docker-compose.yml
================================================
frontend:
  build: ./frontend
  volumes:
    - ./frontend:/app
    - /app/node_modules
    - /app/.next
  ports:
    - "3000:3000"
  environment:
    - NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=http://localhost:3000



================================================
FILE: Dockerfile
================================================
FROM node:20-alpine

WORKDIR /app

# Copiamos solo los archivos de paquetes primero
COPY package.json ./

# Instalamos de cero dentro de Linux
RUN npm install

# Luego copiamos el resto
COPY . .

EXPOSE 3000

CMD ["npm", "run", "dev"]

FILE: jest.config.js
================================================
const nextJest = require('next/jest')

const createJestConfig = nextJest({
  // Ruta a tu app Next.js
  dir: './',
})

const customJestConfig = {
  // Archivo que se ejecuta antes de las pruebas para cargar los matchers (toBeInTheDocument, etc.)
  setupFilesAfterEnv: ['<rootDir>/jest.setup.js'],
  // 🛡️ FIX CRÍTICO: Simular un navegador en lugar de Node.js
  testEnvironment: 'jest-environment-jsdom',
  // Mapeo de alias para que Jest entienda las rutas con "@/"
  moduleNameMapper: {
    '^@/(.*)$': '<rootDir>/$1',
  },
}

module.exports = createJestConfig(customJestConfig)

FILE: next.config.ts
================================================
import type { NextConfig } from "next";

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  // @ts-ignore
  allowedDevOrigins: ["develop.reprompt.studio"],
  typescript: {
    ignoreBuildErrors: true,
  },

  output: "standalone",
  transpilePackages: ["framer-motion"],
  images: {
    remotePatterns: [
      {
        protocol: "https",
        hostname: "**pub-**.r2.dev",
      },
      {
        protocol: "https",
        hostname: "cdn.reprompt.studio",
        port: "",
        pathname: "/**",
      },
    ],
    formats: ["image/avif", "image/webp"],
    minimumCacheTTL: 604800,
  },

  async headers() {
    return [
      {
        source: "/(.*)",
        headers: [
          {
            key: "Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy",
            value: "same-origin-allow-popups",
          },
        ],
      },
    ];
  },
};

export default nextConfig;

import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

/**
 * Read environment variables from file.
 * https://github.com/motdotla/dotenv
 */
// import dotenv from 'dotenv';
// import path from 'path';
// dotenv.config({ path: path.resolve(__dirname, '.env') });

/**
 * See https://playwright.dev/docs/test-configuration.
 */
export default defineConfig({
  testDir: './tests',
  fullyParallel: true,
  forbidOnly: !!process.env.CI,
  retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0,
  workers: process.env.CI ? 1 : undefined,
  reporter: 'html',
  use: {
    baseURL: 'http://localhost:3000',
    trace: 'on-first-retry',
  },

  projects: [
    {
      name: 'chromium',
      use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'] },
    },

    // {
    //   name: 'firefox',
    //   use: { ...devices['Desktop Firefox'] },
    // },

    // {
    //   name: 'webkit',
    //   use: { ...devices['Desktop Safari'] },
    // },

    /* Test against mobile viewports. */
    // {
    //   name: 'Mobile Chrome',
    //   use: { ...devices['Pixel 5'] },

================================================
FILE: proxy.ts
================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

const locales = ["es", "en"];
const defaultLocale = "es";

export function proxy(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;

  const pathnameHasLocale = locales.some(
    (locale) => pathname.startsWith(`/${locale}/`) || pathname === `/${locale}`,
  );

  if (!pathnameHasLocale) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(`/${defaultLocale}${pathname}`, request.url));
  }

  const accessToken = request.cookies.get("access_token")?.value;
  const refreshToken = request.cookies.get("refresh_token")?.value;

  console.log(`\n🚦 [PROXY] Interceptando navegación hacia: ${pathname}`);
  console.log(`   👉 Access Token (15m) presente:  ${accessToken ? "✅ SÍ" : "❌ NO"}`);
  console.log(`   👉 Refresh Token (7d) presente: ${refreshToken ? "✅ SÍ" : "❌ NO"}`);
  // ==============================================================

  const currentLocale =
    locales.find((locale) => pathname.startsWith(`/${locale}/`) || pathname === `/${locale}`) ||
    defaultLocale;

  const pathWithoutLocale = pathname.replace(`/${currentLocale}`, "") || "/";

  // 🟢 LA SOLUCIÓN: Definimos rutas públicas exactas y prefijos públicos
  const publicExactPaths = ["/login"]; // Quitamos "/join" porque ya purgramos ese código muerto
  const publicPrefixes = ["/google/callback", "/discord/callback"]; // Vía libre a los OAuth

  // Comprobamos si la ruta actual es pública
  const isPublicPath =
    publicExactPaths.includes(pathWithoutLocale) ||
    publicPrefixes.some((prefix) => pathWithoutLocale.startsWith(prefix));

  const hasAnyAuth = !!accessToken || !!refreshToken;

  if (!hasAnyAuth && !isPublicPath) {
    console.log(`   🛑 [PROXY] Bloqueado. Redirigiendo a /login (Sin sesión)`);
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(`/${currentLocale}/login`, request.url));
  }

  const isCallbackPath = publicPrefixes.some((prefix) => pathWithoutLocale.startsWith(prefix));

  if (hasAnyAuth && isPublicPath && !isCallbackPath) {
    console.log(`   ♻️ [PROXY] Usuario ya logueado o con Refresh vivo. Redirigiendo a la Home.`);
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(`/${currentLocale}`, request.url));
  }

  if (!accessToken && refreshToken && !isPublicPath) {
    console.log(
      `   🔄 [PROXY] Access Token caducado. Permitimos paso para que el cliente haga Silent Refresh.`,
    );
    return NextResponse.next();
  }

  console.log(`   ✅ [PROXY] Paso permitido.`);
  if (pathnameHasLocale) {
    return NextResponse.next();
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    "/((?!api|_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|.*\\.(?:svg|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp)$).*)",
  ],
};

FILE: app/[lang]/error.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";

export default function GlobalError({
  error,
  reset,
}: {
  error: Error & { digest?: string };
  reset: () => void;
}) {
  const [isSending, setIsSending] = useState(false);
  const [isSent, setIsSent] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    console.error("💥 [ERROR BOUNDARY] Anomalía detectada:", error);
  }, [error]);

  const handleReport = async () => {
    setIsSending(true);
    try {
      await api.admin.reportBug({
        message: error.message,
        stack: error.stack,
        digest: error.digest,
        url: window.location.href,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      });
      setIsSent(true);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Error al enviar el reporte", err);
    } finally {
      setIsSending(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-background flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 md:p-8 font-sans">
      <div className="bg-muted/10 p-6 md:p-10 neo-border neo-shadow max-w-2xl w-full text-center space-y-6">
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-black font-mono text-destructive uppercase tracking-widest">
          ¡Fallo en el Sistema! 🛠️
        </h2>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm leading-relaxed">
          Nuestros escudos han detectado una anomalía crítica en esta ruta. 
          Haz clic en el botón de abajo para enviar los registros directamente al administrador (<strong>@trosdesuru</strong>).
        </p>

        {process.env.NODE_ENV === "development" && (
          <div className="bg-black/80 p-4 rounded-md text-left overflow-auto text-xs text-red-400 font-mono mt-4 max-h-40 custom-scrollbar neo-border">
            {error.message}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4 justify-center pt-6">
          {isSent ? (
            <div className="px-6 py-3 bg-green-500/10 text-green-500 neo-border font-bold font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-xs flex items-center justify-center">
              ✅ Reporte Transmitido
            </div>
          ) : (
            <button
              onClick={handleReport}
              disabled={isSending}
              className="px-6 py-3 bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground font-bold font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-xs neo-border hover:neo-shadow transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {isSending ? "Transmitiendo..." : "🚨 Reportar a trosdesuru"}
            </button>
          )}

          <button
            onClick={() => reset()}
            className="px-6 py-3 bg-background text-foreground font-bold font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-xs neo-border hover:neo-shadow transition-all"
          >
            🔄 Intentar de nuevo
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Prompt, JetBrains_Mono } from "next/font/google";
import "../globals.css";

import { AuthProvider } from "@/src/features/auth/AuthProvider";
import { ThemeProvider } from "@/src/dictionaries/ThemeProvider";
import { AppToaster } from "@/src/system/organisms/Layout/AppToaster";
import { GlobalProgress } from "@/src/system/organisms/Layout/GlobalProgress";

import { GlobalModal } from "@/src/system/organisms/Modal/GlobalModal";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { getDictionary, Locale } from "@/src/dictionaries/getDictionary";
import { I18nProvider } from "@/src/dictionaries/I18nProvider";
import { GoogleOAuthProvider } from "@react-oauth/google";

const prompt = Prompt({
  weight: ["400", "700", "900"],
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-prompt",
});

const jetbrainsMono = JetBrains_Mono({
  weight: ["400", "500", "600", "700"],
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-mono",
});

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "reprompt",
  description: "Wallet para guardar tus imágenes y prompts",
};

export default async function RootLayout({
  children,
  params,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = await params;
  const dictionary = await getDictionary(lang as Locale);

  const googleClientId = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID || "";

  return (
    <html
      lang={lang}
      className={cn(prompt.variable, "font-mono", jetbrainsMono.variable)}
      suppressHydrationWarning
    >
      <body className="antialiased selection:bg-(--accent-color) selection:text-white">
        <ThemeProvider
          attribute="class"
          defaultTheme="system"
          enableSystem
          disableTransitionOnChange
        >
          <GoogleOAuthProvider clientId={googleClientId}>
            <I18nProvider dictionary={dictionary}>
              <AuthProvider>
                <GlobalProgress />
                {children}

                <AppToaster />
                <GlobalModal />
              </AuthProvider>
            </I18nProvider>
          </GoogleOAuthProvider>
        </ThemeProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

FILE: app/[lang]/(auth)/discord/callback/page.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { PageLoader } from "@/src/system/atoms/PageLoader";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

export default function DiscordCallbackPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const processed = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (processed.current) return;

    const code = searchParams.get("code");
    if (!code) {
      toast.error("No se recibió código de Discord.");
      router.push("/es/auth/login");
      return;
    }

    processed.current = true;

    const authenticate = async () => {
      try {
        const currentUrl = new URL(window.location.href);
        const redirectUri = `${currentUrl.origin}${currentUrl.pathname}`;

        const response = await api.verifyDiscordCode({ code, redirect_uri: redirectUri });

        if (response.status === "needs_invite") {
          toast.success("Cuenta de Discord vinculada. Introduce tu código de inivitación");
          router.push(`/es/auth/login?discord_email=${response.email}`);
        } else {
          toast.success(`¡Bienvenido de nuevo, ${response.username}!`);
          router.push("/es");
        }
      } catch (error: any) {
        toast.error(error.message || "Fallo al validar Discord");
        router.push("/es/login");
      }
    };

    authenticate();
  }, [searchParams, router]);

  return <PageLoader text="Verificando enlace cifrado con Discord..." />;
}



================================================
FILE: app/[lang]/(auth)/google/callback/page.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/store/authStore";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams, useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { PageLoader } from "@/src/system/atoms/PageLoader";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

export default function GoogleCallbackPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const params = useParams();
  const lang = params?.lang as string;

  const processed = useRef(false);
  const checkAuth = useAuthStore((state) => state.checkAuth);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (processed.current) return;

    const code = searchParams.get("code");
    if (!code) {
      toast.error("No se recibió código de autenticación de Google.");
      router.push(`/${lang}/login`);
      return;
    }

    processed.current = true;

    const authenticate = async () => {
      try {
        const baseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL || window.location.origin;
        const redirectUri = `${baseUrl}${window.location.pathname}`;

        console.log("📤 Canjeando código con Redirect URI oficial:", redirectUri);

        const response = await api.auth.verifyGoogleCode({ code, redirect_uri: redirectUri });

        if (response.status === "needs_invite") {
          toast.success("Cuenta verificada. ¡Solo falta tu código de invitación!");

          sessionStorage.setItem(
            "oauth_pending",
            JSON.stringify({
              email: response.email,
              token: response.provider_token,
              username: response.suggested_username,
            }),
          );

          router.push(`/${lang}/login?step=oauth_invite`);
        } else {
          toast.success(`¡Bienvenido de nuevo, ${response.suggested_username}!`);

          await checkAuth();

          window.location.href = `/${lang}`;
        }
      } catch (error: any) {
        toast.error(error.message || "Fallo al conectar con Google");
        router.push(`/${lang}/login`);
      }
    };

    authenticate();
  }, [searchParams, router, checkAuth, lang]);

  return <PageLoader text="Cifrando conexión segura con Google..." />;
}

"use client";

import { use } from "react"; // 🟢 Importamos el nuevo hook de React
import Link from "next/link";
import { RegisterForm } from "@/src/system/organisms/Auth/RegisterForm";
import { useRedirectIfAuthenticated } from "@/src/features/auth/hooks/useRedirectIfAuthenticated";

// 🟢 Cambiamos el tipo para decirle a TypeScript que params es una Promesa
export default function JoinPage(props: { params: Promise<{ lang: string }> }) {
  // 🟢 Desempaquetamos la promesa con React.use()
  const params = use(props.params);
  const lang = params.lang;

  // Ahora 'lang' ya es un string seguro (ej: "es" o "en")
  const { isChecking, isAuthenticated } = useRedirectIfAuthenticated(`/${lang}`);

  if (isChecking || isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <main className="min-h-screen bg-background flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4">
        <span className="animate-pulse text-(--accent-color) font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-sm">
          Comprobando credenciales...
        </span>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-background flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4">
      <RegisterForm lang={lang} />

      <p className="mt-8 text-center text-sm font-mono text-(--text-secondary)">
        ¿Ya tienes cuenta?{" "}
        <Link
          href={`/${lang}/login`}
          className="text-(--accent-color) font-black hover:underline underline-offset-4"
        >
          Iniciar sesión
        </Link>
      </p>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { use } from "react";
import dynamic from "next/dynamic";

import { useSearch } from "@/src/features/search/hooks/useSearch";
import { usePublicFeed } from "@/src/features/home/hooks/usePublicFeed";
import { useHomeInteraction } from "@/src/features/home/hooks/useHomeInteraction";

import { Hero } from "@/src/system/organisms/Layout/Hero";
import { FeedState } from "@/src/system/molecules/Feedback/FeedState";
import { PublicFeedGrid } from "@/src/system/pages/Feed/PublicFeedGrid";
import { SearchResults } from "@/src/system/organisms/Search/SearchResults/SearchResults";

const ImageViewerModal = dynamic(
  () =>
    import("@/src/system/organisms/Viewer/ImageViewerModal/ImageViewerModal").then(
      (mod) => mod.ImageViewerModal,
    ),
  { ssr: false },
);

export default function Home({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string }> }) {
  const { lang } = use(params);

  const search = useSearch();
  const publicFeed = usePublicFeed(search.query);
  const interactions = useHomeInteraction();

  const globalError = search.error || publicFeed.feedError;

  return (
    <main className="max-h-screen bg-background text-foreground font-sans">
      <Hero />

      {globalError && <FeedState type="error" message={globalError} />}

      <div className="max-w-90rem mx-auto px-4 pb-12">
        {!search.query ? (
          <PublicFeedGrid
            feed={publicFeed.feed}
            isLoading={publicFeed.isFeedLoading}
            lang={lang}
            hasMore={publicFeed.hasMore}
            onLoadMore={publicFeed.loadMore}
          />
        ) : search.isLoading && search.results.length === 0 ? (
          <FeedState type="loading" />
        ) : !search.isLoading && search.results.length === 0 ? (
          <FeedState type="empty" highlight={search.query} />
        ) : (
          <div className="pt-8">
            <SearchResults
              results={search.results}
              onOpenVisor={interactions.handleOpenVisor}
              onDownloadTotal={interactions.handleDownloadTotal}
              hasMore={search.hasMore}
              isLoadingMore={search.isLoadingMore}
              onLoadMore={search.loadMore}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {interactions.viewerSync.isViewerOpen && (
          <ImageViewerModal
            title={interactions.activeTitle}
            pages={interactions.viewerSync.viewerPages}
            isLoading={interactions.viewerSync.isViewerLoading}
            isLoadingMore={interactions.viewerSync.isLoadingMore}
            hasMorePages={interactions.viewerSync.hasMorePages}
            onLoadMore={interactions.viewerSync.loadNextPage}
            onClose={interactions.viewerSync.closeViewer}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { use } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { PageLoader } from "@/src/system/atoms/PageLoader";
import { IconPhotoOff, IconFolder } from "@tabler/icons-react";

import { PromptCard } from "@/src/system/organisms/Studio/PromptCard";
import { usePublicProfile } from "@/src/features/public/hooks/usePublicProfile";
import { Breadcrumbs } from "@/src/system/organisms/Navigation/Breadcrumbs";

export default function PublicProfilePage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; username: string; index: number }>;
}) {
  const { lang, username } = use(params);

  const { data, isLoading, error } = usePublicProfile(username);

  if (isLoading) {
    return <PageLoader text="Sincronizando perfil..." />;
  }

  if (error || !data) {
    return (
      <div className="max-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center font-mono gap-4">
        <p className="text-red-500">
          {error || "Este usuario no existe o se ha perdido en el vacío."}
        </p>
        <Link
          href={`/${lang}`}
          className="text-(--accent-color) hover:underline uppercase text-xs tracking-widest"
        >
          ← Volver al inicio
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const breadcrumbItems = [{ label: `@${data.user.username}` }];

  return (
    <main className="max-h-screen bg-background p-4 md:p-8">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-8">
        <div>
          <Breadcrumbs items={breadcrumbItems} />
        </div>

        <header className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-b border-border pb-8">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-black tracking-tighter">@{data.user.username}</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm uppercase tracking-widest">
            {data.user.role}
          </p>
        </header>

        {data.directories.length > 0 && (
          <section className="space-y-4">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2">
              <IconFolder size={18} className="text-(--accent-color)" /> Colecciones
            </h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              {data.directories.map((col) => (
                <Link
                  key={col.id}
                  href={`/${lang}/${username}/${col.slug}`}
                  className="group p-4 bg-muted/10 neo-border hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors"
                >
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-lg group-hover:text-(--accent-color) transition-colors">
                    {col.name}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
                    {col.description || "Sin descripción"}
                  </p>
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        <section className="space-y-4">
          <h2 className="text-sm text-accent font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">
            Últimas Generaciones
          </h2>
          {data.recent_prompts.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-muted-foreground/50 bg-muted/5 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/50">
              <IconPhotoOff size={48} stroke={1} className="mb-4" />
              <p className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest">No hay prompts públicos</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-6">
              {data.recent_prompts.map((prompt, index) => (
                <PromptCard
                  key={prompt.id}
                  item={prompt}
                  index={index}
                  isPriority={index < 6}
                  lang={lang}
                  authorUsername={username}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: app/[lang]/(main)/[username]/[collection_slug]/page.tsx

"use client";

import { use, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { IconFolder, IconPhotoOff } from "@tabler/icons-react";

import { PromptCard } from "@/src/system/organisms/Studio/PromptCard";
import { usePublicDirectory } from "@/src/features/public/hooks/usePublicDirectory";
import { FadeIn } from "@/src/system/atoms/FadeIn";
import { Breadcrumbs } from "@/src/system/organisms/Navigation/Breadcrumbs";
import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/store/authStore";

export default function PublicDirectoryPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; username: string; directory_slug: string }>;
}) {
  const { lang, username, directory_slug } = use(params);
  const { data, isLoading, error } = usePublicDirectory(username, directory_slug);

  const user = useAuthStore((state) => state.user);
  const [isMounted, setIsMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsMounted(true);
  }, []);

  const isOwner = isMounted && user?.username === username;

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center font-mono animate-pulse">
        Descifrando la colección...
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !data) {
    return (
      <div className="max-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center font-mono gap-4">
        <p className="text-red-500">{error || "Colección no encontrada."}</p>
        <Link
          href={`/${lang}/${username}`}
          className="text-(--accent-color) hover:underline uppercase text-xs tracking-widest"
        >
          ← Volver al perfil
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const breadcrumbItems = isOwner
    ? [{ label: "Mi Studio", href: `/${lang}/studio` }, { label: data.directory.name }]
    : [{ label: `@${username}`, href: `/${lang}/${username}` }, { label: data.directory.name }];

  return (
    <FadeIn duration={0.8}>
      <main className="max-h-screen bg-background p-4 md:p-8">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-8">
          <div>
            <Breadcrumbs items={breadcrumbItems} homeHref={`/${lang}/reprompt`} />
          </div>

          <header className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-b border-border pb-8">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <IconFolder size={32} className="text-(--accent-color)" />
              <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black tracking-tighter uppercase">
                {data.directory.name}
              </h1>
            </div>
            {data.directory.description && (
              <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono mt-2">{data.directory.description}</p>
            )}
          </header>

          <section>
            {data.prompts.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-muted-foreground/50 bg-muted/5 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/50">
                <IconPhotoOff size={48} stroke={1} className="mb-4" />
                <p className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest">
                  Esta colección está vacía
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-6">
                {data.prompts.map((prompt, index) => (
                  <FadeIn key={prompt.id} delay={0.3 + index * 0.05} y={15}>
                    <PromptCard
                      item={prompt}
                      index={index}
                      isPriority={index < 6}
                      lang={lang}
                      isStudio={false}
                      authorUsername={username}
                    />
                  </FadeIn>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>
      </main>
    </FadeIn>
  );
}

FILE: app/[lang]/(main)/[username]/p/[prompt_id]/page.tsx

"use client";

import { use } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePublicPrompt } from "@/src/features/public/hooks/usePublicPrompt";
import { PromptImageViewer } from "@/src/system/molecules/Cards/PromptImageViewer";
import { PromptMetadataPanel } from "@/src/system/molecules/Cards/PromptMetadataPanel";
import { Breadcrumbs } from "@/src/system/organisms/Navigation/Breadcrumbs";
import { PageLoader } from "@/src/system/atoms/PageLoader";

interface PromptDetailPageProps {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; username: string; prompt_id: string }>;
  searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>;
}

export default function PromptDetailPage({ params, searchParams }: PromptDetailPageProps) {
  const { lang, username, prompt_id } = use(params);
  const resolvedSearchParams = use(searchParams);

  const from = resolvedSearchParams?.from;

  const { data, isLoading, error } = usePublicPrompt(prompt_id);

  const isFromStudio = from === "studio";
  const backUrl = isFromStudio ? `/${lang}/studio` : `/${lang}/${username}`;
  const backText = isFromStudio ? "Volver a Mi Studio" : `Volver a @${username}`;

  if (isLoading) {
    return <PageLoader text="Desencriptando datos..." />;
  }

  if (error || !data || !data.prompt) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center font-mono gap-4">
        <p className="text-red-500">{error || "No se pudo cargar el prompt."}</p>
        <Link
          href={backUrl}
          className="text-(--accent-color) hover:underline uppercase text-xs tracking-widest"
        >
          ← {backText}
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const prompt = data.prompt;

  const breadcrumbItems = isFromStudio
    ? [{ label: "Mi Studio", href: `/${lang}/studio` }, { label: prompt.title || "Detalle" }]
    : [
        { label: `@${username}`, href: `/${lang}/${username}` },
        { label: prompt.title || "Detalle" },
      ];

  return (
    <main className="max-h-screen bg-background p-4 md:p-8">
      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-8">
        <div className="mb-4">
          <Breadcrumbs items={breadcrumbItems} />
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-8 lg:gap-12 items-start">
          <section className="sticky top-24">
            <PromptImageViewer
              imageUrl={prompt.image_url || null}
              altText={`Generación por @${username}`}
            />
          </section>

          <section className="space-y-6">
            <header>
              <h1 className="text-2xl font-black uppercase tracking-tighter mb-2">
                {prompt.title || "Prompt Sin Título"}
              </h1>
              <p className="text-sm font-mono text-muted-foreground">
                Creado el {new Date(prompt.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}
              </p>
            </header>

            <PromptMetadataPanel prompt={prompt} />
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useAdminGuard } from "@/src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminGuard";
import { useAdminDashboard } from "@/src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminDashboard";

// Organismos (Módulos Complejos)
import { InviteManager } from "@/src/system/organisms/Admin/InviteManager";
import { SecurityModule } from "@/src/system/organisms/Admin/SecurityModule";
import { AuditTable } from "@/src/system/organisms/Admin/AuditTable";
import { DatabaseModule } from "@/src/system/organisms/Admin/DatabaseModule";
import { StorageModule } from "@/src/system/organisms/Admin/StorageModule";

// Moléculas (Componentes UI puros extraídos)
import { AdminHeader } from "@/src/system/molecules/Admin/AdminHeader";
import { SectionTitle } from "@/src/system/molecules/Admin/SectionTitle";
import { MetricWidget } from "@/src/system/molecules/Admin/MetricWidget";
import { AdminLoadingScreen } from "@/src/system/molecules/Admin/AdminLoadingScreen";
import { GarbageCollectorModule } from "@/src/system/organisms/Admin/GarbageCollectorModule";

export default function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const { user, isLoadingAuth, isAuthorized } = useAdminGuard();

  const {
    logs,
    isLoadingLogs,
    inviteCode,
    isLoadingInvite,
    fetchLogs,
    handleGenerateInvite,
    handleRevokeAll,
  } = useAdminDashboard();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAuthorized) fetchLogs();
  }, [isAuthorized, fetchLogs]);

  if (isLoadingAuth || !isAuthorized || !user) return <AdminLoadingScreen />;

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-background text-foreground p-4 md:p-8 font-sans pb-20">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-12">
        <AdminHeader username={user.username} />

        {/* ZONA 1: Centro de Mando */}
        <section className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
          <InviteManager
            onGenerate={handleGenerateInvite}
            isLoading={isLoadingInvite}
            inviteCode={inviteCode}
          />
          <SecurityModule onRevokeAll={handleRevokeAll} />
          <MetricWidget count={logs.length} label="Eventos en Auditoría" />

          {/* El nuevo Botón Nuclear */}
          <GarbageCollectorModule />
        </section>
        {/* ZONA 2: Infraestructura */}
        <section className="space-y-6">
          <SectionTitle title="Monitor de" highlight="Infraestructura" />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-2 gap-8">
            <DatabaseModule />
            <StorageModule />
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* ZONA 3: Auditoría */}
        <section className="space-y-6">
          <SectionTitle title="Registro de" highlight="Auditoría" />
          <AuditTable logs={logs} isLoading={isLoadingLogs} onRefresh={fetchLogs} />
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { use } from "react";
import { usePromptExtractor } from "@/src/features/extractor/hooks/usePromptExtractor";
import { ExtractorDropzone } from "@/src/system/organisms/Extractor/Dropzone";
import { BatchInspector } from "@/src/system/organisms/Extractor/Inspector/BatchInspector";
import { UpgradeModal } from "@/src/system/organisms/Modal/UpgradeModal"; // Importado correctamente
import { IconScanTraces } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { PageHeader } from "@/src/system/organisms/Navigation/PageHeader";

export default function RepromptPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string }> }) {
  const { lang } = use(params);

  const {
    processFiles,
    isExtracting,
    error,
    batch,
    skippedFiles,
    upgradeModal,
    closeUpgradeModal,
    updateItemStatus,
    updateItemData,
    removeItem,
    clearBatch,
    clearSkipped,
  } = usePromptExtractor();

  return (
    <main className="max-h-screen bg-background p-4 md:p-8">
      <UpgradeModal isOpen={upgradeModal} onClose={closeUpgradeModal} />

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-8">
        <PageHeader
          title="Extractor"
          subtitle="reprompt"
          description="Arrastra múltiples imágenes o archivos ZIP para extraer metadatos en lote."
          icon={<IconScanTraces size={28} stroke={1.5} />}
          breadcrumbItems={[
            { label: "Mi Studio", href: `/${lang}/studio` },
            { label: "Extractor Reprompt" },
          ]}
        />

        <section className="w-full max-w-7xl mx-auto">
          {batch.length === 0 ? (
            <ExtractorDropzone
              onFileDrop={processFiles}
              isExtracting={isExtracting}
              error={error}
            />
          ) : (
            <BatchInspector
              batch={batch}
              skippedFiles={skippedFiles}
              onUpdateStatus={updateItemStatus}
              onUpdateItemData={updateItemData}
              onRemoveItem={removeItem}
              onClearAll={clearBatch}
              onClearSkipped={clearSkipped}
            />
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: components/ui/button.tsx

import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { Slot } from "radix-ui"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "group/button inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-none border border-transparent bg-clip-padding text-xs font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all outline-none select-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 active:not-aria-[haspopup]:translate-y-px disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-1 aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:border-destructive/50 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground [a]:hover:bg-primary/80",
        outline:
          "border-border bg-background hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground aria-expanded:bg-muted aria-expanded:text-foreground dark:border-input dark:bg-input/30 dark:hover:bg-input/50",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-secondary/80 aria-expanded:bg-secondary aria-expanded:text-secondary-foreground",
        ghost:
          "hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground aria-expanded:bg-muted aria-expanded:text-foreground dark:hover:bg-muted/50",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/20 focus-visible:border-destructive/40 focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:hover:bg-destructive/30 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default:
          "h-8 gap-1.5 px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2",
        xs: "h-6 gap-1 rounded-none px-2 text-xs has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",
        sm: "h-7 gap-1 rounded-none px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3.5",
        lg: "h-9 gap-1.5 px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-3 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-3",
        icon: "size-8",
        "icon-xs": "size-6 rounded-none [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",
        "icon-sm": "size-7 rounded-none",
        "icon-lg": "size-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Button({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"button"> &
  VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> & {
    asChild?: boolean
  }) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot.Root : "button"

  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="button"
      data-variant={variant}
      data-size={size}
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Button, buttonVariants }

FILE: components/ui/combobox.tsx
================================================
import * as React from "react"
import { Combobox as ComboboxPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  InputGroup,
  InputGroupAddon,
  InputGroupButton,
  InputGroupInput,
} from "@/components/ui/input-group"
import { CaretDownIcon, XIcon, CheckIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

const Combobox = ComboboxPrimitive.Root

function ComboboxValue({ ...props }: ComboboxPrimitive.Value.Props) {
  return <ComboboxPrimitive.Value data-slot="combobox-value" {...props} />
}

function ComboboxTrigger({
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: ComboboxPrimitive.Trigger.Props) {
  return (
    <ComboboxPrimitive.Trigger
      data-slot="combobox-trigger"
      className={cn("[&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      <CaretDownIcon className="pointer-events-none size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
    </ComboboxPrimitive.Trigger>
  )
}

function ComboboxClear({ className, ...props }: ComboboxPrimitive.Clear.Props) {
  return (
    <ComboboxPrimitive.Clear
      data-slot="combobox-clear"
      render={<InputGroupButton variant="ghost" size="icon-xs" />}
      className={cn(className)}
      {...props}
    >
      <XIcon className="pointer-events-none" />
    </ComboboxPrimitive.Clear>
  )
}

function ComboboxInput({
  className,
  children,
  disabled = false,
  showTrigger = true,
  showClear = false,
  ...props
}: ComboboxPrimitive.Input.Props & {
  showTrigger?: boolean
  showClear?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <InputGroup className={cn("w-auto", className)}>
      <ComboboxPrimitive.Input
        render={<InputGroupInput disabled={disabled} />}
        {...props}
      />
      <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
        {showTrigger && (
          <InputGroupButton
            size="icon-xs"
            variant="ghost"
            asChild
            data-slot="input-group-button"
            className="group-has-data-[slot=combobox-clear]/input-group:hidden data-pressed:bg-transparent"
            disabled={disabled}
          >
            <ComboboxTrigger />
          </InputGroupButton>
        )}
        {showClear && <ComboboxClear disabled={disabled} />}
      </InputGroupAddon>
      {children}
    </InputGroup>
  )
}

function ComboboxContent({
  className,
  side = "bottom",
  sideOffset = 6,
  align = "start",
  alignOffset = 0,
  anchor,
  ...props
}: ComboboxPrimitive.Popup.Props &
  Pick<
    ComboboxPrimitive.Positioner.Props,
    "side" | "align" | "sideOffset" | "alignOffset" | "anchor"
  >) {
  return (
    <ComboboxPrimitive.Portal>
      <ComboboxPrimitive.Positioner
        side={side}
        sideOffset={sideOffset}
        align={align}
        alignOffset={alignOffset}
        anchor={anchor}
        className="isolate z-50"
      >
        <ComboboxPrimitive.Popup
          data-slot="combobox-content"
          data-chips={!!anchor}
          className={cn("group/combobox-content relative max-h-(--available-height) w-(--anchor-width) max-w-(--available-width) min-w-[calc(var(--anchor-width)+--spacing(7))] origin-(--transform-origin) overflow-hidden rounded-none bg-popover text-popover-foreground shadow-md ring-1 ring-foreground/10 duration-100 data-[chips=true]:min-w-(--anchor-width) data-[side=bottom]:slide-in-from-top-2 data-[side=inline-end]:slide-in-from-left-2 data-[side=inline-start]:slide-in-from-right-2 data-[side=left]:slide-in-from-right-2 data-[side=right]:slide-in-from-left-2 data-[side=top]:slide-in-from-bottom-2 *:data-[slot=input-group]:m-1 *:data-[slot=input-group]:mb-0 *:data-[slot=input-group]:h-8 *:data-[slot=input-group]:border-input/30 *:data-[slot=input-group]:bg-input/30 *:data-[slot=input-group]:shadow-none data-open:animate-in data-open:fade-in-0 data-open:zoom-in-95 data-closed:animate-out data-closed:fade-out-0 data-closed:zoom-out-95", className )}
          {...props}
        />
      </ComboboxPrimitive.Positioner>
    </ComboboxPrimitive.Portal>
  )
}

function ComboboxList({ className, ...props }: ComboboxPrimitive.List.Props) {
  return (

"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"

function InputGroup({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="input-group"
      role="group"
      className={cn(
        "group/input-group relative flex h-8 w-full min-w-0 items-center rounded-none border border-input transition-colors outline-none in-data-[slot=combobox-content]:focus-within:border-inherit in-data-[slot=combobox-content]:focus-within:ring-0 has-disabled:bg-input/50 has-disabled:opacity-50 has-[[data-slot=input-group-control]:focus-visible]:border-ring has-[[data-slot=input-group-control]:focus-visible]:ring-1 has-[[data-slot=input-group-control]:focus-visible]:ring-ring/50 has-[[data-slot][aria-invalid=true]]:border-destructive has-[[data-slot][aria-invalid=true]]:ring-1 has-[[data-slot][aria-invalid=true]]:ring-destructive/20 has-[>[data-align=block-end]]:h-auto has-[>[data-align=block-end]]:flex-col has-[>[data-align=block-start]]:h-auto has-[>[data-align=block-start]]:flex-col has-[>textarea]:h-auto dark:bg-input/30 dark:has-disabled:bg-input/80 dark:has-[[data-slot][aria-invalid=true]]:ring-destructive/40 has-[>[data-align=block-end]]:[&>input]:pt-3 has-[>[data-align=block-start]]:[&>input]:pb-3 has-[>[data-align=inline-end]]:[&>input]:pr-1.5 has-[>[data-align=inline-start]]:[&>input]:pl-1.5",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

const inputGroupAddonVariants = cva(
  "flex h-auto cursor-text items-center justify-center gap-2 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground select-none group-data-[disabled=true]/input-group:opacity-50 [&>kbd]:rounded-none [&>svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      align: {
        "inline-start":
          "order-first pl-2 has-[>button]:ml-[-0.3rem] has-[>kbd]:ml-[-0.15rem]",
        "inline-end":
          "order-last pr-2 has-[>button]:mr-[-0.3rem] has-[>kbd]:mr-[-0.15rem]",
        "block-start":
          "order-first w-full justify-start px-2.5 pt-2 group-has-[>input]/input-group:pt-2 [.border-b]:pb-2",
        "block-end":
          "order-last w-full justify-start px-2.5 pb-2 group-has-[>input]/input-group:pb-2 [.border-t]:pt-2",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      align: "inline-start",
    },
  }
)

function InputGroupAddon({
  className,
  align = "inline-start",
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & VariantProps<typeof inputGroupAddonVariants>) {
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      data-slot="input-group-addon"
      data-align={align}
      className={cn(inputGroupAddonVariants({ align }), className)}
      onClick={(e) => {
        if ((e.target as HTMLElement).closest("button")) {
          return
        }
        e.currentTarget.parentElement?.querySelector("input")?.focus()
      }}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

const inputGroupButtonVariants = cva(
  "flex items-center gap-2 text-xs shadow-none",
  {
    variants: {
      size: {
        xs: "h-6 gap-1 rounded-none px-1.5 [&>svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3.5",
        sm: "gap-1",
        "icon-xs": "size-6 rounded-none p-0 has-[>svg]:p-0",
        "icon-sm": "size-7 p-0 has-[>svg]:p-0",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      size: "xs",
    },
  }
)

function InputGroupButton({
  className,
  type = "button",
  variant = "ghost",
  size = "xs",
  ...props
}: Omit<React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>, "size"> &
  VariantProps<typeof inputGroupButtonVariants>) {
  return (
    <Button
      type={type}
      data-size={size}
      variant={variant}
      className={cn(inputGroupButtonVariants({ size }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function InputGroupText({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        "flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function InputGroupInput({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"input">) {
  return (
    <Input
      data-slot="input-group-control"
      className={cn(
        "flex-1 rounded-none border-0 bg-transparent shadow-none ring-0 focus-visible:ring-0 disabled:bg-transparent aria-invalid:ring-0 dark:bg-transparent dark:disabled:bg-transparent",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function InputGroupTextarea({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"textarea">) {
  return (
    <Textarea
      data-slot="input-group-control"
      className={cn(
        "flex-1 resize-none rounded-none border-0 bg-transparent py-2 shadow-none ring-0 focus-visible:ring-0 disabled:bg-transparent aria-invalid:ring-0 dark:bg-transparent dark:disabled:bg-transparent",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  InputGroup,
  InputGroupAddon,
  InputGroupButton,
  InputGroupText,
  InputGroupInput,
  InputGroupTextarea,
}

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx"
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge"

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))
}

FILE: src/dictionaries/getDictionary.ts

import "server-only";

const dictionaries = {
  en: () => import("./en.json").then((module) => module.default),
  es: () => import("./es.json").then((module) => module.default),
};

export type Locale = keyof typeof dictionaries;

export const getDictionary = async (locale: Locale) => {
  return dictionaries[locale]?.() ?? dictionaries.es();
};



================================================
FILE: src/dictionaries/I18nProvider.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { createContext, useContext } from "react";

const I18nContext = createContext<any>(null);

export const I18nProvider = ({
  children,
  dictionary,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  dictionary: any;
}) => {
  return <I18nContext.Provider value={dictionary}>{children}</I18nContext.Provider>;
};

export const useTranslation = () => {
  const dictionary = useContext(I18nContext);

  if (!dictionary) {
    throw new Error("useTranslation debe usarse dentro de un I18nProvider");
  }

  const t = (key: string): string => {
    const keys = key.split(".");
    let value = dictionary;

    for (const k of keys) {
      if (value === undefined) break;
      value = value[k];
    }

    return value || key;
  };

  return { t };
};

FILE: src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminDashboard.ts
================================================
// frontend/features/admin/hooks/useAdminDashboard.ts
import { useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { AuditLog } from "../admin.types";

export function useAdminDashboard() {
  const [inviteCode, setInviteCode] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isLoadingInvite, setIsLoadingInvite] = useState(false);

  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<AuditLog[]>([]);
  const [isLoadingLogs, setIsLoadingLogs] = useState(false);

  const fetchLogs = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsLoadingLogs(true);
    try {
      const data = await api.obtenerLogsAuditoria(20);
      if (data.status === "success") {
        setLogs(data.logs);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Error cargando logs:", err);
    } finally {
      setIsLoadingLogs(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const handleGenerateInvite = async (role: string) => {
    setIsLoadingInvite(true);
    setInviteCode(null);
    try {
      const data = await api.generarInvitacion(role);
      setInviteCode(data.code);
    } catch (error: any) {
      alert(`❌ Error: ${error.message}`);
    } finally {
      setIsLoadingInvite(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRevokeAll = async () => {
    if (
      !confirm(
        "⚠️ ¿Estás seguro? Esto invalidará todas las sesiones en Redis y te expulsará incluso a ti.",
      )
    )
      return;
    try {
      const data = await api.revocarTodasLasSesiones();
      if (data.status === "success") {
        alert("🔒 Sesiones invalidadas con éxito.");
        window.location.href = "/login";
      }
    } catch (err) {
      alert("Error al comunicarse con Redis para revocar sesiones.");
    }
  };

  return {
    logs,
    isLoadingLogs,
    inviteCode,
    isLoadingInvite,
    fetchLogs,
    handleGenerateInvite,
    handleRevokeAll,
  };
}



================================================
FILE: src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminGuard.ts
================================================
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/store/authStore";

export function useAdminGuard() {
  const { user, isLoadingAuth } = useAuthStore();
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isLoadingAuth && (!user || user.role !== "admin")) {
      router.push("/");
    }
  }, [user, isLoadingAuth, router]);

  const isAuthorized = !isLoadingAuth && user?.role === "admin";

  return { user, isLoadingAuth, isAuthorized };
}



================================================
FILE: src/features/auth/AuthProvider.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/store/authStore";

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const { checkAuth, user, hasCheckedInitialAuth } = useAuthStore();
  const pathname = usePathname() || "";

  useEffect(() => {
    const isAuthPage =
      pathname.includes("/login") || pathname.includes("/join") || pathname.includes("/callback");

    if (!isAuthPage) {
      if (!user && !hasCheckedInitialAuth) {
        checkAuth();
      }
    } else {
      useAuthStore.setState({ isLoadingAuth: false });
    }
  }, [pathname, checkAuth, user, hasCheckedInitialAuth]);

  return <>{children}</>;
}



================================================
FILE: src/features/auth/hooks/useAuthForm.tsx
================================================
// src/features/auth/hooks/useAuthForm.ts
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useOAuth } from "./useOAuth";
import { useCredentialsAuth } from "./useCredentialsAuth";

export function useAuthForm() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  // Dependencias
  const oauth = useOAuth();
  const credsAuth = useCredentialsAuth();

  // Estado puro de la UI (Inputs)
  const [isLogin, setIsLogin] = useState(true);
  const [emailOrUser, setEmailOrUser] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [inviteCode, setInviteCode] = useState("");
  const [isInviteLocked, setIsInviteLocked] = useState(false);

  // Manejo de código de invitación por URL
  useEffect(() => {
    const codeFromUrl = searchParams.get("code");
    if (codeFromUrl) {
      setIsLogin(false);
      setInviteCode(codeFromUrl);
      setIsInviteLocked(true);
    }
  }, [searchParams]);

  // Wrappers para inyectar el estado actual en las funciones de los submódulos
  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    credsAuth.handleCredentialsSubmit(isLogin, emailOrUser, password, inviteCode);
  };

  const handleOauthSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    oauth.handleOauthSubmit(inviteCode);
  };

  // Estado derivado para bloquear la UI si ALGO está cargando
  const isAnyLoading = credsAuth.isCredsLoading || oauth.isOauthSubmitting;

  return {
    state: {
      isLogin,
      isLoading: isAnyLoading,
      isGoogleLoading: oauth.isGoogleLoading,
      isDiscordLoading: oauth.isDiscordLoading,
      emailOrUser,
      password,
      inviteCode,
      isInviteLocked,
      oauthPending: oauth.oauthPending,
    },
    actions: {
      setIsLogin,
      setEmailOrUser,
      setPassword,
      setInviteCode,
      handleSubmit,
      handleOauthSubmit,
      handleGoogleLogin: oauth.handleGoogleLogin,
      handleDiscordLogin: oauth.handleDiscordLogin,
      cancelOauthFlow: oauth.cancelOauthFlow,
    },
  };
}

// src/features/auth/hooks/useCredentialsAuth.ts
import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

export function useCredentialsAuth() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isCredsLoading, setIsCredsLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleCredentialsSubmit = async (
    isLogin: boolean,
    emailOrUser: string,
    password: string,
    inviteCode: string,
  ) => {
    setIsCredsLoading(true);
    try {
      if (isLogin) {
        await api.login({ username: emailOrUser, password });
        toast.success("¡Bienvenido de nuevo!");
        router.push("/es/studio");
      } else {
        await api.register({ email: emailOrUser, password, invite_code: inviteCode });
        toast.success("Cuenta creada con éxito");
        await api.login({ username: emailOrUser, password });
        router.push("/es/studio");
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error(error?.response?.data?.detail || "Error en la autenticación");
    } finally {
      setIsCredsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return { handleCredentialsSubmit, isCredsLoading };
}

export function useOAuth() {
  // Inicializamos los micro-hooks
  const googleAuth = useGoogleAuth();
  const discordAuth = useDiscordAuth();
  const pendingAuth = useOAuthPending();

  // Devolvemos el contrato exacto que el useAuthForm espera
  return {
    // Estados
    oauthPending: pendingAuth.oauthPending,
    isGoogleLoading: googleAuth.isGoogleLoading,
    isDiscordLoading: discordAuth.isDiscordLoading,
    isOauthSubmitting: pendingAuth.isOauthSubmitting,

    // Acciones
    handleGoogleLogin: googleAuth.handleGoogleLogin,
    handleDiscordLogin: discordAuth.handleDiscordLogin,
    handleOauthSubmit: pendingAuth.handleOauthSubmit,
    cancelOauthFlow: pendingAuth.cancelOauthFlow,
  };
}

FILE: src/features/auth/hooks/useRedirectIfAuthenticated.ts

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/store/authStore";

export function useRedirectIfAuthenticated(redirectTo: string = "/") {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, isLoadingAuth } = useAuthStore();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isLoadingAuth && user) {
      router.replace(redirectTo);
    }
  }, [user, isLoadingAuth, router, redirectTo]);

  return {
    isChecking: isLoadingAuth,
    isAuthenticated: !!user,
  };
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";

interface RegisterFormData {
  username: string;
  password: string;
  invite_code: string;
}

export const useRegister = (lang: string) => {
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleRegister = async (formData: RegisterFormData) => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      await api.register(formData);

      router.push(`/${lang}/login?registered=true`);
    } catch (err: any) {
      const errorMsg = err.response?.data?.detail || err.message || "Error crítico al registrarse";
      setError(errorMsg);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return { handleRegister, isLoading, error };
};

export function useDiscordAuth() {
  const [isDiscordLoading, setIsDiscordLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleDiscordLogin = async () => {
    setIsDiscordLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await api.auth.getDiscordAuthUrl();
      if (response?.auth_url) {
        window.location.href = response.auth_url;
      } else {
        throw new Error("El servidor no devolvió una URL válida");
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error("❌ Error al iniciar Discord:", error);
      toast.error(error.message || "Servicio de Discord no disponible en este momento.");
    } finally {
      setIsDiscordLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return { isDiscordLoading, handleDiscordLogin };
}

FILE: src/features/auth/hooks/oauth/useOAuthPending.ts
================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

export function useOAuthPending() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  const [oauthPending, setOauthPending] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isOauthSubmitting, setIsOauthSubmitting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const pendingData = sessionStorage.getItem("oauth_pending");
    if (pendingData) {
      setOauthPending(JSON.parse(pendingData));
    }
  }, []);

  const handleOauthSubmit = async (inviteCode: string) => {
    if (!oauthPending) return;
    setIsOauthSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await api.auth.registerGoogleUser({
        token: oauthPending.token,
        invite_code: inviteCode,
        username: oauthPending.username,
      });
      sessionStorage.removeItem("oauth_pending");
      toast.success("¡Cuenta VIP creada con éxito!");
      window.location.href = "/es/studio";
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error(error.message || "Código inválido");
    } finally {
      setIsOauthSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const cancelOauthFlow = () => {
    setOauthPending(null);
    sessionStorage.removeItem("oauth_pending");
    if (searchParams.get("step") === "oauth_invite") {
      router.replace("/es/login");
    }
  };

  return {
    oauthPending,
    isOauthSubmitting,
    handleOauthSubmit,
    cancelOauthFlow,
  };
}

FILE: src/features/extractor/hooks/useFileUploader.ts
================================================
import { usePromptExtractor } from "@/src/features/extractor/hooks/usePromptExtractor";

export function useFileUploader() {
  const { processFiles } = usePromptExtractor();

  const handleUploadClick = () => {
    const input = document.createElement("input");
    input.type = "file";
    input.multiple = true;
    input.onchange = (e) => {
      const files = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).files;
      if (files) processFiles(Array.from(files));
    };
    input.click();
  };

  return { handleUploadClick };
}



================================================
FILE: src/features/extractor/hooks/usePromptExtractor.ts
================================================
import { useState, useCallback, useEffect } from "react";
import { parsePromptData, ExtractedPromptData } from "../utils/promptParser";
import { getRawTextFromFile } from "../utils/fileExtractors";
import { extractFilesFromZip } from "../utils/fileExtractors/zipExtractor";
import { handleError } from "@/src/utils/errors/ErrorHandler";
import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/store/authStore";

export interface BatchItem {
  id: string;
  file: File;
  previewUrl: string | null;
  data: ExtractedPromptData;
  status: "idle" | "saving" | "success" | "error";
}

export interface SkippedFile {
  name: string;
  reason: string;
}

export function usePromptExtractor() {
  const [isExtracting, setIsExtracting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [batch, setBatch] = useState<BatchItem[]>([]);
  const [skippedFiles, setSkippedFiles] = useState<SkippedFile[]>([]);
  const [upgradeModal, setUpgradeModal] = useState(false);

  const { user } = useAuthStore();

  const closeUpgradeModal = useCallback(() => {
    setUpgradeModal(false);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      batch.forEach((item) => {
        if (item.previewUrl) {
          URL.revokeObjectURL(item.previewUrl);
          console.log(`🗑️ RAM liberada: ${item.id}`);
        }
      });
    };
  }, [batch]);

  const processFiles = useCallback(async (files: File[], fileRejections: any[] = []) => {
    setUpgradeModal(false);
    setIsExtracting(true);
    setError(null);

    let allFilesToProcess: File[] = [];
    const currentSkipped: SkippedFile[] = fileRejections.map((r) => ({
      name: r.file.name,
      reason: "Formato no aceptado o archivo inválido.",
    }));

    const maxFilesAllowed = user?.role === "artist" ? 200 : 10;

    try {
      for (const file of files) {
        if (
          file.type === "application/zip" ||
          file.name.endsWith(".zip") ||
          file.name.endsWith(".x-zip-compressed")
        ) {
          const unzipped = await extractFilesFromZip(file, maxFilesAllowed);
          allFilesToProcess = [...allFilesToProcess, ...unzipped.validFiles];
          currentSkipped.push(...unzipped.rejectedFiles);

          if (unzipped.limitReached && user?.role === "voyager") {
            setUpgradeModal(true);
          }
        } else {
          if (allFilesToProcess.length < maxFilesAllowed) {
            allFilesToProcess.push(file);
          } else {
            currentSkipped.push({ name: file.name, reason: "Límite de subida alcanzado" });
            if (user?.role === "voyager") setUpgradeModal(true);
          }
        }
      }

      if (allFilesToProcess.length === 0 && currentSkipped.length === 0) {
        throw new Error("No se recibieron archivos válidos.");
      }

      const newBatchItems: BatchItem[] = [];

      for (const file of allFilesToProcess) {
        try {
          const rawText = await getRawTextFromFile(file);
          let parsedData: ExtractedPromptData;

          if (!rawText) {
            if (file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
              parsedData = {
                prompt: "",
                rawText: "",
                software: "Desconocido",
                negativePrompt: "",
                model: "",
                sampler: "",
                steps: "",
                cfgScale: "",
                seed: "",
                resolution: "",
                loras: [],
                triggerWords: [],
                extractionConfidence: 0,
              };
            } else {
              currentSkipped.push({ name: file.name, reason: "Sin metadatos de IA detectados." });
              continue;
            }
          } else {
            parsedData = parsePromptData(rawText);
          }

          const previewUrl = file.type.startsWith("image/") ? URL.createObjectURL(file) : null;

          newBatchItems.push({
            id: crypto.randomUUID(),
            file,
            previewUrl,
            data: parsedData,
            status: "idle",
          });
        } catch (e) {
          currentSkipped.push({ name: file.name, reason: "Error al procesar el archivo." });
        }
      }

      setSkippedFiles((prev) => [...prev, ...currentSkipped]);

      if (newBatchItems.length > 0) {
        setBatch((prev) => [...prev, ...newBatchItems]);
      } else if (currentSkipped.length > 0) {
        setError(
          `Se descartaron ${currentSkipped.length} archivos por no ser válidos o no tener metadatos.`,
        );
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setError(handleError(err, "ExtractorBatch"));
    } finally {
      setIsExtracting(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const updateItemStatus = useCallback((id: string, status: BatchItem["status"]) => {
    setBatch((prev) => prev.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, status } : item)));
  }, []);

  const updateItemData = useCallback((id: string, newData: Partial<ExtractedPromptData>) => {
    setBatch((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, data: { ...item.data, ...newData } } : item)),
    );
  }, []);

  const removeItem = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setBatch((prev) => {
      const item = prev.find((p) => p.id === id);
      if (item?.previewUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(item.previewUrl);
      return prev.filter((p) => p.id !== id);
    });
  }, []);

  const clearBatch = useCallback(() => {
    setBatch((prev) => {
      prev.forEach((item) => {
        if (item.previewUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(item.previewUrl);
      });
      return [];
    });
    setSkippedFiles([]);
    setError(null);
  }, []);

  const clearSkipped = useCallback(() => {
    setSkippedFiles([]);
  }, []);

  return {
    isExtracting,
    error,
    batch,
    skippedFiles,
    processFiles,
    upgradeModal,
    updateItemStatus,
    updateItemData,
    removeItem,
    clearBatch,
    clearSkipped,
    closeUpgradeModal,
  };
}



================================================
FILE: src/features/extractor/utils/promptParser.ts
================================================
import { ExtractedPromptData, ExtractedPromptSchema } from "./parsers/validation";
import { cleanNullBytes } from "./parsers/stringUtils";
import { parseA1111 } from "./parsers/a1111Parser";
import { parseComfy } from "./parsers/comfyParser";
import { parseMidjourney } from "./parsers/midjourneyParser";

export type { ExtractedPromptData };

export function parsePromptData(rawText: string): ExtractedPromptData {
  const cleanText = cleanNullBytes(rawText);
  let parsed: Partial<ExtractedPromptData>;

  if (cleanText.trim().startsWith("{")) {
    try {
      const jsonObj = JSON.parse(cleanText);
      parsed = parseComfy(jsonObj, cleanText);
    } catch {
      parsed = parseA1111(cleanText);
    }
  } else if (/--ar|--v|--style|--niji|Job ID:/i.test(cleanText)) {
    parsed = parseMidjourney(cleanText, rawText);
    parsed.software = "Midjourney";
  } else {
    parsed = parseA1111(cleanText);
  }

  const validation = ExtractedPromptSchema.safeParse({
    ...parsed,
    rawText: rawText,
  });

  if (!validation.success) {
    console.error("❌ Fallo en contrato de extracción:", validation.error.format());

    return {
      prompt: cleanText,
      negativePrompt: "",
      resolution: undefined,
      rawText: rawText,
      software: "Desconocido",
      extractionConfidence: 0.1,
    };
  }

  return validation.data;
}

FILE: src/features/extractor/utils/fileExtractors/index.ts

import { extractPngMetadata } from "./pngExtractor";
import { extractJpegMetadata } from "./jpegExtractor";
import { extractRawText } from "./rawExtractor";

export async function getRawTextFromFile(file: File): Promise<string | null> {
  const type = file.type;

  if (type === "image/png") {
    return extractPngMetadata(file);
  }

  if (type === "image/jpeg" || type === "image/jpg") {
    return extractJpegMetadata(file);
  }

  if (type === "text/plain" || type === "application/json") {
    return extractRawText(file);
  }

  return null;
}



================================================
FILE: src/features/extractor/utils/fileExtractors/jpegExtractor.ts
================================================
import exifr from "exifr";

export async function extractJpegMetadata(file: File): Promise<string | null> {
  try {
    // Escaneamos solo lo necesario para no sobrecargar
    const metadata = await exifr.parse(file, {
      userComment: true,
      xmp: true,
      iptc: true,
      // Nota: exifr busca en ImageDescription por defecto si no se filtra
    });

    if (!metadata) return null;

    // 1. Prioridad: UserComment (Comfy/Civitai/A1111)
    if (metadata.userComment) {
      const raw =
        typeof metadata.userComment === "string"
          ? metadata.userComment
          : new TextDecoder("utf-8").decode(metadata.userComment);

      return raw.replace(/^(UNICODE|ASCII)\x00+/, "").trim();
    }

    // 2. Prioridad: ImageDescription (Midjourney/A1111 alternativo)
    // exifr suele mapear 'Description' a 'ImageDescription'
    if (metadata.ImageDescription) {
      return metadata.ImageDescription.trim();
    }

    // 3. Prioridad: description (Caso específico de algunos metadatos XMP)
    if (metadata.description) {
      return metadata.description.trim();
    }

    return null;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("❌ Error en jpegExtractor:", error);
    return null;
  }
}

FILE: src/features/extractor/utils/fileExtractors/pngExtractor.ts
================================================
import extract from "png-chunks-extract";
import text from "png-chunk-text";

export async function extractPngMetadata(file: File): Promise<string | null> {
  const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
  const chunks = extract(new Uint8Array(buffer));
  const textChunks = chunks.filter((c: any) => c.name === "tEXt" || c.name === "iTXt");

  for (const chunk of textChunks) {
    const decoded = text.decode(chunk.data);
    if (decoded.keyword === "parameters" || decoded.keyword === "Comment") {
      return decoded.text;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export async function extractRawText(file: File): Promise<string | null> {
  return await file.text();
}


================================================
FILE: src/features/extractor/utils/fileExtractors/zipExtractor.ts
================================================
import JSZip from "jszip";


export async function extractFilesFromZip(
  zipFile: File,
  maxFilesAllowed: number, // 🟢 Usaremos este parámetro dinámico (10 o 200)
): Promise<ZipExtractionResult> {
  const zip = new JSZip();
  const loadedZip = await zip.loadAsync(zipFile);

  const validFiles: File[] = [];
  const rejectedFiles: { name: string; reason: string }[] = [];
  let limitReached = false;

  const supportedRegex = /\.(png|jpe?g|txt|json)$/i;
  let fileCount = 0;

  for (const [filename, zipEntry] of Object.entries(loadedZip.files)) {
    if (
      zipEntry.dir ||
      filename.includes("__MACOSX") ||
      filename.split("/").pop()?.startsWith(".")
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    // 🟢 CORRECCIÓN 1 Y 2: Usamos maxFilesAllowed y activamos la bandera
    if (fileCount >= maxFilesAllowed) {
      rejectedFiles.push({
        name: filename,
        reason: `Requiere plan superior. Límite: ${maxFilesAllowed} archivos.`,
      });
      limitReached = true; // 🟢 ¡Esto dispara el Pop-up de ventas!
      continue;
    }

    if (!supportedRegex.test(filename)) {
      rejectedFiles.push({
        name: filename.split("/").pop() || filename,
        reason: "Formato no soportado",
      });
      continue;
    }

    try {
      const blob = await zipEntry.async("blob");

      if (blob.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) {
        rejectedFiles.push({ name: filename, reason: "Archivo demasiado grande (>50MB)." });
        continue;
      }

      const mimeType = getMimeType(filename);
      const file = new File([blob], filename.split("/").pop() || filename, { type: mimeType });
      validFiles.push(file);
      fileCount++;
    } catch (e) {
      rejectedFiles.push({ name: filename, reason: "Archivo corrupto dentro del ZIP" });
    }
  }

  // 🟢 CORRECCIÓN 3: Devolvemos las 3 cosas que pide la interfaz
  return { validFiles, rejectedFiles, limitReached };
}

function getMimeType(filename: string): string {
  const lower = filename.toLowerCase();
  if (lower.endsWith(".png")) return "image/png";
  if (lower.endsWith(".jpg") || lower.endsWith(".jpeg")) return "image/jpeg";
  if (lower.endsWith(".json")) return "application/json";
  return "text/plain";
}



================================================
FILE: src/features/extractor/utils/parsers/a1111Parser.ts
================================================
import { ExtractedPromptData } from "./types";
import { cleanA1111Prefixes } from "./stringUtils";

export function parseA1111(rawText: string): ExtractedPromptData {
  const result: ExtractedPromptData = {
    prompt: "",
    rawText,
    software: "A1111",
  };

  const cleanText = cleanA1111Prefixes(rawText);

  const negativePromptRegex = /Negative prompt:\s*([\s\S]*?)(?=\nSteps:|$)/i;
  const negativeMatch = cleanText.match(negativePromptRegex);

  if (negativeMatch) {
    result.negativePrompt = negativeMatch[1].trim();
    result.prompt = cleanText.split(/Negative prompt:/i)[0].trim();
  } else {
    result.prompt = cleanText.split(/\nSteps:/i)[0].trim();
  }

  const matchers = {
    steps: /Steps:\s*([^,]+)/i,
    sampler: /Sampler:\s*([^,]+)/i,
    cfgScale: /CFG scale:\s*([^,]+)/i,
    seed: /Seed:\s*([^,]+)/i,
    resolution: /Size:\s*([^,]+)/i,
    model: /Model:\s*([^,]+)/i,
  };

  Object.entries(matchers).forEach(([key, regex]) => {
    const match = cleanText.match(regex);
    if (match) (result as any)[key] = match[1].trim();
  });

  return result;
}



================================================
FILE: src/features/extractor/utils/parsers/comfyParser.ts
================================================
import { ExtractedPromptData } from "./types";
import { extractFileName } from "./stringUtils";

export function parseComfy(jsonObj: any, rawText: string): ExtractedPromptData {
  const result: ExtractedPromptData = { prompt: "", rawText, software: "ComfyUI" };

  // 1. Intentar por extraMetadata (Civitai)
  if (jsonObj.extraMetadata) {
    const meta =
      typeof jsonObj.extraMetadata === "string"
        ? JSON.parse(jsonObj.extraMetadata)
        : jsonObj.extraMetadata;

    result.prompt = meta.prompt || result.prompt;
    result.negativePrompt = meta.negativePrompt || "";
    result.steps = meta.steps?.toString();
    result.sampler = meta.sampler;
    result.cfgScale = meta.cfgScale?.toString();
    result.seed = meta.seed?.toString();

    if (meta.images?.[0]) result.resolution = `${meta.images[0].width}x${meta.images[0].height}`;

    if (meta.resources) {
      result.model = meta.resources
        .find((r: any) => r.type === "Checkpoint")
        ?.modelVersionId?.toString();
      result.loras = meta.resources
        .filter((r: any) => r.type === "LORA")
        .map((l: any) => ({ name: l.modelVersionId?.toString(), strength: l.strength }));
    }

    if (meta.triggerWords) {
      result.triggerWords = Array.isArray(meta.triggerWords)
        ? meta.triggerWords
            .flatMap((w: string) => w.split(","))
            .map((w: string) => w.trim()) // 👈 Agregamos : string aquí
            .filter(Boolean)
        : [];
    }

    if (result.prompt && result.steps) return result;
  }

  // 2. Fallback: Rastreo de nodos manual
  if (jsonObj && typeof jsonObj === "object" && !Array.isArray(jsonObj)) {
    Object.values(jsonObj).forEach((node: any) => {
      if (typeof node !== "object" || !node) return;

      if (node.class_type?.includes("KSampler")) {
        result.steps = node.inputs?.steps?.toString() || result.steps;
        result.seed = node.inputs?.seed?.toString() || result.seed;
        result.cfgScale = node.inputs?.cfg?.toString() || result.cfgScale;
        result.sampler = node.inputs?.sampler_name || result.sampler;
      }
      if (node.class_type?.includes("CLIPTextEncode")) {
        const text = node.inputs?.text;
        const isNeg = node._meta?.title?.toLowerCase().includes("negative");
        if (isNeg) result.negativePrompt = text;
        else if (!result.prompt) result.prompt = text;
      }
      if (node.class_type === "LoraLoader") {
        if (!result.loras) result.loras = [];
        result.loras.push({
          name: extractFileName(node.inputs?.lora_name),
          strength: node.inputs?.strength_model,
        });
      }
    });
  }

  return result;
}



================================================
FILE: src/features/extractor/utils/parsers/midjourneyParser.ts
================================================
import { ExtractedPromptData } from "./types";

export function parseMidjourney(description: string, rawText: string): ExtractedPromptData {
  const result: ExtractedPromptData = {
    prompt: description,
    rawText,
    software: "Desconocido",
  };

  if (description.includes("--") || description.includes("Job ID:")) {
    result.software = "Desconocido";

    // Extraer resolución de --ar
    const arMatch = description.match(/--ar\s+([0-9:]+)/);
    if (arMatch) result.resolution = arMatch[1];

    const noMatch = description.match(/--no\s+([^--]+)/);
    if (noMatch) {
      result.negativePrompt = noMatch[1].trim();
    }
  }

  return result;
}
