export const cleanNullBytes = (text: string): string => {
  return text.replace(/\0/g, "").trim();
};

export const cleanA1111Prefixes = (text: string): string => {
  return text
    .replace(/^UNICODE\x00/, "")
    .replace(/^ASCII\x00\x00\x00/, "")
    .trim();
};

export const extractFileName = (path: string): string => {
  return path.split("/").pop() || path;
};

FILE: src/features/extractor/utils/parsers/validation.ts

import { z } from "zod";

export const ExtractedPromptSchema = z.object({
  prompt: z
    .string()
    .min(1, "El prompt es obligatorio")
    .transform((t) => t.trim()),
  negativePrompt: z.string().default(""),
  steps: z.string().optional(),
  sampler: z.string().optional(),
  cfgScale: z.string().optional(),
  seed: z.string().optional(),
  model: z.string().optional(),
  resolution: z
    .string()
    .optional()
    .transform((r) => r?.replace(/\s+/g, "")),
  software: z.enum(["A1111", "ComfyUI", "Midjourney", "NovelAI", "Desconocido"]),
  rawText: z.string(),
  extractionConfidence: z.number().min(0).max(1).default(1),

  loras: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        name: z.string(),
        strength: z.union([z.string(), z.number()]),
      }),
    )
    .optional(),
  triggerWords: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
});

export type ExtractedPromptData = z.infer<typeof ExtractedPromptSchema>;

import { useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { useNotificationStore } from "@/src/store/notificationStore";
import { useViewerSync } from "@/src/features/image-viewer/hooks/useViewerSync";
import { GalleryItem } from "@/src/types/models";

export function useHomeInteraction() {
  const { notify } = useNotificationStore();
  const [activeUrl, setActiveUrl] = useState("");
  const [activeTitle, setActiveTitle] = useState("");

  const viewerSync = useViewerSync(activeUrl);

  const handleOpenVisor = useCallback((item: GalleryItem) => {
    setActiveUrl(item.url);
    setActiveTitle(item.title);
    viewerSync.openViewer(item.url);
    
    // Disparamos la métrica en background sin esperar (Fire and Forget)
    api.registerInteraction(item, "view").catch((err) => 
      console.error("Fallo al registrar vista:", err)
    );
  }, [viewerSync]);

  const handleDownloadTotal = useCallback(async (item: GalleryItem) => {
    try {
      notify("Descarga", `⬇️ Preparando ${item.title}...`, "info");
      await api.downloadAll(item.url, item.title);
      api.registerInteraction(item, "download").catch(console.error);
      notify("Éxito", `✅ Descarga encolada: ${item.title}`, "success");
    } catch (err) {
      notify("Error", "❌ Falla crítica al encolar la descarga.", "error");
    }
  }, [notify]);

  return {
    activeTitle,
    viewerSync,
    handleOpenVisor,
    handleDownloadTotal,
  };
}

FILE: src/features/home/hooks/usePublicFeed.ts
================================================
import { FeedItem } from "@/src/types/models";
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";

export function usePublicFeed(query: string) {
  const [feed, setFeed] = useState<FeedItem[]>([]);
  const [isFeedLoading, setIsFeedLoading] = useState(false);
  const [feedError, setFeedError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [nextCursor, setNextCursor] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [hasMore, setHasMore] = useState(true);

  const loadFeed = useCallback(
    async (isInitial: boolean = false) => {
      if (isFeedLoading || (!isInitial && !hasMore)) return;

      setIsFeedLoading(true);
      setFeedError(null);

      try {
        const currentCursor = isInitial ? null : nextCursor;
        const res = await api.public.getFeed({
          include_self: false,
          cursor: currentCursor,
        });

        const data = await res.json();

        if (data.status === "success") {
          setFeed((prev) => (isInitial ? data.feed : [...prev, ...data.feed]));
          setNextCursor(data.next_cursor);
          setHasMore(!!data.next_cursor);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Error cargando feed:", err);
        setFeedError("Hubo un problema al cargar el arte.");
      } finally {
        setIsFeedLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [nextCursor, hasMore, isFeedLoading],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (query) return;
    loadFeed(true);
  }, [query]);

  return {
    feed,
    isFeedLoading,
    feedError,
    hasMore,
    loadMore: () => loadFeed(false),
  };
}

FILE: src/features/image-viewer/hooks/useViewerSync.ts
================================================
// frontend/features/image-viewer/hooks/useViewerSync.ts
import { useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { ViewerPageItem } from "@/src/types/models";

export function useViewerSync(activeUrl: string) {
  const [isViewerOpen, setIsViewerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [viewerPages, setViewerPages] = useState<ViewerPageItem[]>([]);
  const [isViewerLoading, setIsViewerLoading] = useState(false);
  const [isLoadingMore, setIsLoadingMore] = useState(false);
  const [hasMorePages, setHasMorePages] = useState(false);
  const [currentViewerPage, setCurrentViewerPage] = useState(0);

  const openViewer = async (targetUrl?: string) => {
    const urlToFetch = targetUrl || activeUrl;

    if (!urlToFetch) {
      console.warn("⚠️ [QA DEBUG] openViewer abortado: targetUrl es undefined");
      return;
    }

    setIsViewerOpen(true);
    setIsViewerLoading(true);
    setViewerPages([]);

    try {
      const res = await api.leer(urlToFetch, 0);
      const dataArray = res.data || res.paginas || res.enlaces;

      if (res.status === "success" || res.status === "ok" || (dataArray && dataArray.length > 0)) {
        if (Array.isArray(dataArray) && dataArray.length > 0) {
          console.log(`✅ [QA DEBUG] Guardando ${dataArray.length} imágenes en el estado.`);
          setViewerPages(dataArray);
          setHasMorePages(!!res.has_next);
          setCurrentViewerPage(1);
        } else {
          console.warn(
            "⚠️ [QA DEBUG] El Backend devolvió SUCCESS, pero el array de imágenes está VACÍO o no es un array válido.",
          );
          setViewerPages([]);
        }
      } else {
        console.error(
          "❌ [QA DEBUG] El Backend devolvió un status de ERROR o estructura irreconocible:",
          res,
        );
        setViewerPages([]);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("💥 [QA DEBUG - CAPA 2] Crash durante la petición de red:", err);
    } finally {
      setIsViewerLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const closeViewer = () => {
    setIsViewerOpen(false);
    setViewerPages([]);
  };

  const loadNextPage = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!hasMorePages || isLoadingMore) return;
    setIsLoadingMore(true);

    try {
      const res = await api.leer(activeUrl, currentViewerPage);
      const dataArray = res.data || res.paginas || res.enlaces;

      if (Array.isArray(dataArray)) {
        setViewerPages((prev) => [...prev, ...dataArray]);
        setHasMorePages(!!res.has_next);

        if (res.has_next) setCurrentViewerPage((prev) => prev + 1);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    } finally {
      setIsLoadingMore(false);
    }
  }, [activeUrl, currentViewerPage, hasMorePages, isLoadingMore]);

  return {
    viewerPages,
    isViewerLoading,
    isLoadingMore,
    isViewerOpen,
    openViewer,
    closeViewer,
    hasMorePages,
    loadNextPage,
  };
}

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { SavedPrompt } from "@/src/types/models";

interface DirectoryDetail {
  directory: {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    description: string;
    slug: string;
    created_at: string;
  };
  prompts: SavedPrompt[];
}

export function usePublicDirectory(username: string, directory_slug: string) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<DirectoryDetail | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    api.public
      .getDirectory(username, directory_slug)
      .then(async (res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Error en la petición");
        const jsonData = await res.json();
        setData(jsonData);
      })
      .finally(() => setIsLoading(false));
  }, [username, directory_slug]);

  return { data, isLoading, error };
}

FILE: src/features/public/hooks/usePublicPrompt.ts

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";

export function usePublicPrompt(promptId: string) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsLoading(true);

    api.public
      .getPromptDetail(promptId)
      .then(async (res: Response) => {
        if (!res.ok) {
          throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${res.status}`);
        }
        const jsonData = await res.json();
        
        setData(jsonData);
      })
      .catch((err: any) => {
        console.error("Error fetching prompt:", err);
        setError("El prompt no existe o es privado.");
        setData(null);
      })
      .finally(() => setIsLoading(false));
  }, [promptId]);

  return { data, isLoading, error };
}

FILE: src/features/search/useVectorSearch.ts
================================================
import { useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { fetchSeguro } from "@/src/services/api/client";

interface SearchOptions {
  scope: "global" | "studio";
  directoryId?: string;
}

export function useVectorSearch({ scope, directoryId }: SearchOptions) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [results, setResults] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleExecute = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!query.trim()) return;

    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({
        q: query,
        scope,
        ...(directoryId && { directory_id: directoryId }),
      });

      const res = await fetchSeguro(`/search/vector?${params.toString()}`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setResults(data.results || []);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`[VectorSearch:${scope}] Error:`, err);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [query, scope, directoryId]);

  return {
    query,
    setQuery,
    results,
    isLoading,
    handleExecute,
    clear: () => {
      setQuery("");
      setResults([]);
    },
  };
}

FILE: src/features/search/hooks/useSearch.ts
================================================
"use client";

import { useProgressStore } from "@/src/store/progressStore";
import { useSearchStore } from "@/src/store/searchStore";

export function useSearch() {
  const store = useSearchStore();

  const handleSearch = async (input?: string | React.FormEvent) => {
    console.trace("🚨 DETECTIVE: Alguien disparó la búsqueda");
    if (input && typeof input === "object" && "preventDefault" in input) {
      input.preventDefault();
    } else if (typeof input === "string") {
      store.setQuery(input);
    }

    useProgressStore.getState().start();
    try {
      await store.executeSearch();
    } finally {
      useProgressStore.getState().done();
    }
  };

  return {
    query: store.query,
    setQuery: store.setQuery,
    results: store.results,
    isLoading: store.isLoading,
    isLoadingMore: store.isLoadingMore,
    hasMore: store.hasMore,
    error: store.error,
    activeCategories: store.activeCategories,
    toggleCategory: store.toggleCategory,
    handleSearch,
    loadMore: store.loadMore,
  };
}



================================================
FILE: src/features/studio/hooks/useDirectoryActions.ts
================================================
import { useCallback } from "react";
import { useStudioUI } from "@/src/store/useStudioUI";
import { fetchSeguro } from "@/src/services/api/client";

const API_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "http://localhost:8000/api";

export function useDirectoryActions() {
  const { directories, setDirectories, activeDirectoryId, setActiveDirectory } = useStudioUI();

  const createDirectory = useCallback(
    async (name: string, parentId: string | null) => {
      try {
        const payload: Record<string, string> = { name };
        if (parentId) {
          payload.parent_id = parentId;
        }

        const res = await fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/directories/`, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload),
          credentials: "include",
        });

        if (res.ok) {
          const newDir = await res.json();
          setDirectories([...directories, newDir]);
        } else {
          throw new Error("Fallo al crear el directorio");
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("[DirectoryActions] Error creando:", error);
      }
    },
    [directories, setDirectories],
  );

  // En src/features/studio/hooks/useDirectoryActions.ts

  const deleteDirectory = useCallback(
    async (id: string) => {
      try {
        const res = await fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/directories/${id}`, {
          method: "DELETE",
          credentials: "include",
        });

        if (res.ok) {
          setDirectories(directories.filter((d) => d.id !== id));
          if (activeDirectoryId === id) setActiveDirectory(null);
        } else {
          // 🟢 FRONTEND INTELIGENTE: Leemos el error del backend
          const errorData = await res.json();

          if (res.status === 409 && errorData.detail === "DIRECTORY_NOT_EMPTY") {
            alert(
              "⚠️ Esta carpeta contiene subcarpetas o prompts.\n\nPor seguridad, debes vaciarla o mover sus elementos antes de eliminarla.",
            );
            return;
          }

          throw new Error(errorData.detail || "Fallo al borrar el directorio");
        }
      } catch (error: any) {
        console.error("[DirectoryActions] Error borrando:", error.message);
      }
    },
    [directories, setDirectories, activeDirectoryId, setActiveDirectory],
  );

  const renameDirectory = useCallback(
    async (id: string, newName: string) => {
      try {
        const res = await fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/directories/${id}`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ name: newName }),
        });

        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("No se pudo renombrar");

        setDirectories(directories.map((dir) => (dir.id === id ? { ...dir, name: newName } : dir)));
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("[DirectoryActions] Error renombrando:", error);
        throw error;
      }
    },
    [directories, setDirectories],
  );

  const moveDirectory = useCallback(
    async (directoryId: string, newParentId: string | null) => {
      const previousDirectories = [...directories];
      setDirectories(
        directories.map((d) => (d.id === directoryId ? { ...d, parent_id: newParentId } : d)),
      );

      try {
        const res = await fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/directories/${directoryId}/move`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ parent_id: newParentId }),
          credentials: "include",
        });

        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Fallo al mover en el servidor");
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("[DirectoryActions] Error moviendo, haciendo rollback:", error);

        setDirectories(previousDirectories);
      }
    },
    [directories, setDirectories],
  );

  return {
    createDirectory,
    deleteDirectory,
    renameDirectory,
    moveDirectory,
  };
}

import { useDraggable, useDroppable } from "@dnd-kit/core";
import { Directory } from "@/src/store/useStudioUI";

export function useDirectoryDnd(directory: Directory) {
  const {
    attributes,
    listeners,
    setNodeRef: setDraggableRef,
    isDragging,
  } = useDraggable({
    id: `dir-${directory.id}`,
    data: { type: "directory", directory },
  });

  const { setNodeRef: setDroppableRef, isOver } = useDroppable({
    id: directory.id,
    data: { type: "directory", directory },
  });

  const setBothRefs = (node: HTMLElement | null) => {
    setDraggableRef(node);
    setDroppableRef(node);
  };

  return { attributes, listeners, setBothRefs, isDragging, isOver };
}

FILE: src/features/studio/hooks/useStudioDnd.ts
================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { useSensor, useSensors, PointerSensor, DragStartEvent, DragEndEvent } from "@dnd-kit/core";
import { Directory } from "@/src/store/useStudioUI";
import { useDirectoryActions } from "./useDirectoryActions";

export function useStudioDnd() {
  const { moveDirectory } = useDirectoryActions();
  const [activeDragDir, setActiveDragDir] = useState<Directory | null>(null);

  const sensors = useSensors(
    useSensor(PointerSensor, {
      activationConstraint: { distance: 8 },
    }),
  );

  const handleDragStart = (event: DragStartEvent) => {
    const { active } = event;
    if (active.data.current?.type === "directory") {
      setActiveDragDir(active.data.current.directory);
    }
  };

  const handleDragEnd = (event: DragEndEvent) => {
    setActiveDragDir(null);
    const { active, over } = event;

    if (!over || active.id === `dir-${over.id}`) return;

    const draggedDirId = String(active.id).replace("dir-", "");
    const targetDirId = String(over.id);

    if (draggedDirId !== targetDirId) {
      moveDirectory(draggedDirId, targetDirId);
    }
  };

  return { sensors, activeDragDir, handleDragStart, handleDragEnd };
}

FILE: src/features/studio/hooks/useStudioPrompts.ts
================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useStudioUI } from "@/src/store/useStudioUI";
import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/store/authStore";
import { fetchSeguro } from "@/src/services/api/client";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

const API_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "http://localhost:8000/api";

export function useStudioPrompts() {
  const { activeDirectoryId, searchQuery } = useStudioUI();
  const user = useAuthStore((state) => state.user);

  const [debouncedQuery, setDebouncedQuery] = useState(searchQuery);
  const [prompts, setPrompts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = setTimeout(() => setDebouncedQuery(searchQuery), 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(handler);
  }, [searchQuery]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchPrompts = async () => {
      setIsLoading(true);
      try {
        const API_BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "http://localhost:8000/api";

        const endpoint = debouncedQuery
          ? `${API_BASE}/search/studio?q=${debouncedQuery}`
          : `${API_BASE}/prompts/studio?directory_id=${activeDirectoryId || ""}`;

        const res = await fetchSeguro(endpoint, { credentials: "include" });

        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          setPrompts(data.results || data.data || []);
        } else {
          console.error("Fallo del servidor:", res.status);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Error en Studio Grid:", err);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };

    fetchPrompts();
  }, [activeDirectoryId, debouncedQuery]);

  const deletePrompt = async (promptId: string) => {
    const toastId = toast.loading("Exterminando rastro digital...");
    try {
      const res = await fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/prompts/${promptId}`, {
        method: "DELETE",
        credentials: "include",
      });

      if (res.ok) {
        setPrompts((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== promptId));
        toast.success("Eliminado del Studio", { id: toastId });
      } else {
        const errorData = await res.json();
        throw new Error(errorData.detail || "Fallo de autorización");
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error("Fallo al borrar:", error);
      toast.error(error.message || "Error al eliminar", { id: toastId });
    }
  };

  return {
    prompts,
    isLoading,
    user,
    deletePrompt,
  };
}

}

import { API_URL, fetchSeguro } from "./client";

export interface AdminUserDto {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string | null;
  role: string;
  auth_provider: string;
  created_at: string;
  is_active: boolean;
}

export interface DatabaseStatsResponse {
  total_users: number;
  total_prompts: number;
  total_mb: number;

  users: AdminUserDto[];
}

// src/services/api/auth.ts
import { UserLogin, UserRegister } from "@/src/types/auth.types";
import { fetchSeguro } from "./client";
import { ENV } from "@/src/services/config/env";

const API_URL = ENV.API_URL;
console.log("🕵️‍♂️ [DEBUG] Valor real de API_URL inyectado por Next:", API_URL);

const JSON_DATA = async (res: Response) => {
  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorData = await res
      .clone()
      .json()
      .catch(() => ({}));

    throw new Error(errorData.detail || errorData.message || `Error del servidor (${res.status})`);
  }
  if (res.status === 204) return null;
  return res.json().catch(() => ({}));
};

export const authApi = {
  register: (data: UserRegister) =>
    fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/register`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    }).then(JSON_DATA),

  login: (credentials: UserLogin) =>
    fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/login`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    }).then(JSON_DATA),

  logout: async () => {
    await fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/logout`, { method: "POST" });
    window.location.href = "/login";
  },

  cambiarPassword: (data: any) =>
    fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/change-password`, {
      method: "PUT",
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    }).then(JSON_DATA),

  me: () => fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/me`, { method: "GET" }).then(JSON_DATA),

  generarInvitacion: (role: string = "voyager") =>
    fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/invite-codes?role_requested=${role}`, { method: "POST" }).then(
      JSON_DATA,
    ),

  obtenerLogsAuditoria: (limit: number = 50) =>
    fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/admin/logs?limit=${limit}`).then(JSON_DATA),

  revocarTodasLasSesiones: () =>
    fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/admin/sessions/revoke-all`, { method: "POST" }).then(JSON_DATA),

  refrescarToken: () => fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/refresh`, { method: "POST" }).then(JSON_DATA),

  getGoogleAuthUrl: () => fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/google/url`, { method: "GET" }).then(JSON_DATA),

  verifyGoogleCode: (data: { code: string; redirect_uri: string }) =>
    fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/auth/google/callback`, {
      method: "POST",

    }).then(JSON_DATA),

FILE: src/services/api/client.ts
================================================
import { ENV } from "@/src/services/config/env";

let isRefreshing = false;
let refreshPromise: Promise<boolean> | null = null;

export const API_URL = ENV.API_URL;
interface ExtendedRequestInit extends RequestInit {
  next?: {
    revalidate?: number;
    tags?: string[];
  };
}

export async function fetchSeguro(
  endpoint: string,
  options: ExtendedRequestInit = {},
): Promise<Response> {
  const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});
  if (!headers.has("Content-Type") && !(options.body instanceof FormData)) {
    headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  }

  const config: ExtendedRequestInit = { ...options, headers, credentials: "include" };
  let response: Response;

  try {
    response = await fetch(endpoint, config);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error("No hay conexión con el servidor.");
  }

  const isCriticalAuthPath =
    endpoint.includes("/auth/login") ||
    endpoint.includes("/auth/register") ||
    endpoint.includes("/auth/refresh");

  if (response.status === 401 && !isCriticalAuthPath) {
    console.warn(`🚑 [RESCATE] 401 en ${endpoint}. Iniciando Silent Refresh...`);

    if (!isRefreshing) {
      isRefreshing = true;
      refreshPromise = (async () => {
        try {
          const refreshRes = await fetch(`${API_URL}/auth/refresh`, {
            method: "POST",
            credentials: "include",
          });
          return refreshRes.ok;
        } catch {
          return false;
        } finally {
          isRefreshing = false;
        }
      })();
    }

    const refreshSuccessful = await refreshPromise;

    if (refreshSuccessful) {
      if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event("auth:session_recovered"));
      }

      try {
        return await fetch(endpoint, config);
      } catch (error) {
        throw new Error("Error en el reintento de conexión.");
      }
    } else {
      if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event("auth:unauthorized"));
      }
      return response;
    }
  }

  return response;
}

FILE: src/services/api/public.ts
================================================
import { API_URL, fetchSeguro } from "./client";

const RESERVED_ROUTES = new Set([
  "login",
  "register",
  "join",
  "admin",
  "studio",
  "reprompt",
  "api",
  "_next",
  "favicon.ico",
]);

export const publicApi = {
  getProfile: async (username: string) => {
    if (RESERVED_ROUTES.has(username.toLowerCase())) {
      return Promise.reject(new Error("Ruta reservada del sistema"));
    }
    return fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/public/profile/${username}`);
  },

  getDirectory: async (username: string, slug: string) => {
    if (RESERVED_ROUTES.has(username.toLowerCase())) {
      return Promise.reject(new Error("Ruta reservada del sistema"));
    }
    return fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/public/profile/${username}/${slug}`);
  },

  getPromptDetail: async (promptId: string) => {
    return fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/public/prompt/${promptId}`);
  },

  getFeed: async (options?: { include_self?: boolean; cursor?: string | null }) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    if (options?.include_self) params.append("include_self", "true");
    if (options?.cursor) params.append("cursor", options.cursor);

    const queryString = params.toString() ? `?${params.toString()}` : "";

    return fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/public/feed${queryString}`);
  },
};

    query: string,
    next_id: string | null = null,
    categories: Record<string, boolean> | null = null,
  ) => {
    let endpoint = `${API_URL}/buscar?query=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`;
    if (next_id) endpoint += `&next_id=${next_id}`;
    if (categories) endpoint += `&categories=${encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(categories))}`;
    return fetchSeguro(endpoint);
  },
  leer: (url: string, p: number = 0) =>
    fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/leer?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}&p=${p}`),

  resolverImagen: (url: string) =>
    fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/resolver_imagen?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`),

  getTrends: (limit: number = 5) => fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/tendencias?limit=${limit}`),

  registerInteraction: (
    item: { url: string; title: string; thumb: string },
    type: "view" | "download",
  ) =>
    fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/interact`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        url: item.url,
        title: item.title,
        thumb: item.thumb,
        interaction_type: type,
      }),
    }),
};

FILE: src/store/authStore.ts
================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";

interface User {
  username: string;
  role: string;
}

interface AuthState {
  user: User | null;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  isLoadingAuth: boolean;
  hasCheckedInitialAuth: boolean;

  login: (userData: User) => void;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  checkAuth: (force?: boolean) => Promise<void>;
  logoutSilencioso: () => void;
}

const redirectToLogin = () => {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;

  const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
  if (
    currentPath.includes("/login") ||
    currentPath.includes("/google/callback") ||
    currentPath.includes("/discord/callback")
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const localeMatch = currentPath.match(/^\/([a-z]{2})(?:\/|$)/);
  const localePrefix = localeMatch ? `/${localeMatch[1]}` : "";

  console.warn(`🚷 [AUTH_ROUTER] Redirigiendo al usuario a: ${localePrefix}/login`);
  window.location.href = `${localePrefix}/login`;
};

export const useAuthStore = create<AuthState>((set, get) => ({
  user: null,
  isAuthenticated: false,
  isLoadingAuth: true,
  hasCheckedInitialAuth: false,

  login: (userData) => {
    console.log(`[AUTH_STORE] Sesión iniciada localmente: @${userData.username}`);
    set({
      user: userData,
      isAuthenticated: true,
      isLoadingAuth: false,
      hasCheckedInitialAuth: true,
    });
  },

  logoutSilencioso: () => {
    console.log("🤫 [AUTH_STORE] Limpieza de estado local (Silenciosa).");
    set({ user: null, isAuthenticated: false, isLoadingAuth: false, hasCheckedInitialAuth: true });
  },

  checkAuth: async (force = false) => {
    const { hasCheckedInitialAuth } = get();

    if (hasCheckedInitialAuth && !force) {
      return;
    }

    console.log("🔍 [AUTH_STORE] Verificando estado de sesión...");
    set({ isLoadingAuth: true });

    try {
      const res = await api.auth.me();

      const userData = res?.user || res?.data || res;
      const username = userData?.username;
      const role = userData?.role || "voyager";

      if (username) {
        console.log(`✅ [AUTH_STORE] checkAuth exitoso: @${username}`);
        set({
          user: { username, role },
          isAuthenticated: true,
        });
      } else {
        console.warn("⚠️ [AUTH_STORE] checkAuth respondió OK pero el cuerpo está vacío.");
        set({ user: null, isAuthenticated: false });
      }
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      console.warn("🛑 [AUTH_STORE] checkAuth falló (No hay sesión activa).");
      set({ user: null, isAuthenticated: false });
    } finally {
      set({ isLoadingAuth: false, hasCheckedInitialAuth: true });
    }
  },

  logout: async () => {
    console.log("扫 [AUTH_STORE] Ejecutando orden de desconexión API...");
    try {
      await api.auth.logout();
      console.log("🧹 [AUTH_STORE] Backend confirmó destrucción de sesión.");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("❌ [AUTH_STORE] Falló llamada al backend para logout:", error);
    } finally {
      set({
        user: null,
        isAuthenticated: false,
        isLoadingAuth: false,
        hasCheckedInitialAuth: true,
      });
      redirectToLogin();
    }
  },
}));

if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  window.addEventListener("auth:session_recovered", () => {
    console.log("♻️ [AUTH_STORE] Sesión recuperada. Sincronizando interfaz...");
    useAuthStore.getState().checkAuth(true);
  });

  window.addEventListener("auth:unauthorized", () => {
    const state = useAuthStore.getState();
    if (state.isAuthenticated) {
      state.logoutSilencioso();
      const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
      if (!currentPath.includes("/login")) {
        window.location.href = `/${currentPath.split("/")[1] || "es"}/login`;
      }
    }
  });
}

FILE: src/store/notificationStore.tsx
================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { IconAlertTriangle, IconInfoCircle } from "@tabler/icons-react";

export type NotificationType = "success" | "error" | "info" | "warning";

export interface AppNotification {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  type: NotificationType;
  read: boolean;
  timestamp: Date;
}

interface NotificationState {
  notifications: AppNotification[];
  unreadCount: number;
  notify: (title: string, message: string, type?: NotificationType) => void;
  markAsRead: (id: string) => void;
  markAllAsRead: () => void;
  clearAll: () => void;
}

export const useNotificationStore = create<NotificationState>((set) => ({
  notifications: [],
  unreadCount: 0,

  notify: (title, message, type = "info") => {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID();
    const newNotif: AppNotification = {
      id,
      title,
      message,
      type,
      read: false,
      timestamp: new Date(),
    };

    set((state) => ({
      notifications: [newNotif, ...state.notifications].slice(0, 50),
      unreadCount: state.unreadCount + 1,
    }));

    const toastMessage = `${title}: ${message}`;

    switch (type) {
      case "success":
        toast.success(toastMessage, { id });
        break;
      case "error":
        toast.error(toastMessage, { id });
        break;
      case "warning":
        toast(toastMessage, {
          id,
          icon: <IconAlertTriangle size={20} className="text-yellow-500" />, // 🟢 Icono Tabler inyectado
        });
        break;
      case "info":
      default:
        toast(toastMessage, {
          id,
          icon: <IconInfoCircle size={20} className="text-blue-500" />, // 🟢 Icono Tabler inyectado
        });
        break;
    }
  },

  markAsRead: (id) =>
    set((state) => {
      const notifs = state.notifications.map((n) => (n.id === id ? { ...n, read: true } : n));
      return {
        notifications: notifs,
        unreadCount: notifs.filter((n) => !n.read).length,
      };
    }),

  markAllAsRead: () =>
    set((state) => ({
      notifications: state.notifications.map((n) => ({ ...n, read: true })),
      unreadCount: 0,
    })),

  clearAll: () => set({ notifications: [], unreadCount: 0 }),
}));

FILE: src/store/progressStore.ts
================================================
import { create } from "zustand";

interface ProgressState {
  isAnimating: boolean;
  progress: number;
  start: () => void;
  setProgress: (value: number) => void;
  done: () => void;
}

export const useProgressStore = create<ProgressState>((set) => {
  let timer: NodeJS.Timeout;

  return {
    isAnimating: false,
    progress: 0,
    start: () => {
      set({ isAnimating: true, progress: 5 });
      clearInterval(timer);
      timer = setInterval(() => {
        set((state) => {
          const nextProgress = state.progress >= 90 ? state.progress : state.progress + (10 - state.progress / 10);
          return { progress: nextProgress };
        });
      }, 300);
    },
    setProgress: (value) => {
      set({ isAnimating: true, progress: value });
    },
    done: () => {
      clearInterval(timer);
      set({ isAnimating: true, progress: 100 });
      setTimeout(() => {
        set({ isAnimating: false, progress: 0 });
      }, 400);
    },
  };
});

FILE: src/store/searchStore.ts
================================================
import { create } from "zustand";

interface SearchState {
  query: string;
  results: any[];
  isLoading: boolean;
  nextCursor: string | null;
  hasMore: boolean;
  activeCategories: Record<string, boolean>;

  setQuery: (q: string) => void;
  setResults: (results: any[]) => void;
  appendResults: (newResults: any[], nextCursor: string | null) => void;
  setIsLoading: (loading: boolean) => void;
  toggleCategory: (categoryId: string) => void;
  clearSearch: () => void;
}

export const useSearchStore = create<SearchState>((set) => ({
  query: "",
  results: [],
  isLoading: false,
  nextCursor: null,
  hasMore: false,

  activeCategories: {
    midjourney: false,
    stablediffusion: false,
    comfyui: false,
    dalle: false,
  },

  setQuery: (query) => set({ query }),
  setResults: (results) => set({ results }),

  appendResults: (newResults, nextCursor) =>
    set((state) => {
      const combined = [...state.results, ...newResults];

      const unique = Array.from(new Map(combined.map((item) => [item.id, item])).values());

      return {
        results: unique,
        nextCursor: nextCursor,
        hasMore: !!nextCursor && unique.length > state.results.length,
      };
    }),

  setIsLoading: (isLoading) => set({ isLoading }),

  toggleCategory: (categoryId) =>
    set((state) => ({
      activeCategories: {
        ...state.activeCategories,
        [categoryId]: !state.activeCategories[categoryId],
      },
    })),

  clearSearch: () =>
    set({
      query: "",
      results: [],
      isLoading: false,
      nextCursor: null,
      hasMore: false,
    }),
}));

import { create } from "zustand";

export interface Directory {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  parent_id: string | null;
  slug?: string;
  is_private?: boolean;
}

interface StudioUIState {
  directories: Directory[];
  activeDirectoryId: string | null;

  searchQuery: string;
  isSearching: boolean;

  setDirectories: (dirs: Directory[]) => void;
  setActiveDirectory: (id: string | null) => void;
  setSearchQuery: (query: string) => void;
  setIsSearching: (status: boolean) => void;

  getChildDirectories: (parentId: string | null) => Directory[];
}

export const useStudioUI = create<StudioUIState>((set, get) => ({
  directories: [],
  activeDirectoryId: null,
  searchQuery: "",
  isSearching: false,

  setDirectories: (directories) => set({ directories }),
  setActiveDirectory: (activeDirectoryId) => set({ activeDirectoryId }),
  setSearchQuery: (searchQuery) => set({ searchQuery }),
  setIsSearching: (isSearching) => set({ isSearching }),

  getChildDirectories: (parentId) => {
    return get().directories.filter((d: Directory) => d.parent_id === parentId);
  },
}));

FILE: src/system/atoms/Button.tsx

import { ButtonHTMLAttributes, forwardRef } from "react";

interface ButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: "primary" | "secondary" | "danger" | "ghost";
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  isLoading?: boolean;
}

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  (
    { className = "", variant = "primary", size = "md", isLoading, children, disabled, ...props },
    ref,
  ) => {
    const variants = {
      primary: "bg-[var(--accent-color)] text-white hover:brightness-110",
      secondary:
        "bg-[var(--bg-secondary)] text-[var(--text-primary)] hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800",
      danger: "bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-500",
      ghost:
        "bg-transparent border-transparent shadow-none hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 text-[var(--text-secondary)]",
    };

    const sizes = {
      sm: "px-4 py-2 text-sm",
      md: "px-6 py-3 text-base",
      lg: "px-10 py-4 text-lg font-bold",
    };

    const brutalistClasses = variant === "ghost" ? "" : "neo-border neo-shadow";

    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        disabled={disabled || isLoading}
        className={`
          inline-flex items-center justify-center font-bold uppercase tracking-wide transition-all duration-200
          disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:transform-none disabled:shadow-none
          ${variants[variant]} 
          ${sizes[size]} 
          ${brutalistClasses}
          ${className}
        `}
        {...props}
      >
        {isLoading && (
          <svg
            className="animate-spin -ml-1 mr-3 h-5 w-5 text-current"
            xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
            fill="none"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          >
            <circle
              className="opacity-25"
              cx="12"
              cy="12"
              r="10"
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth="4"
            ></circle>
            <path
              className="opacity-75"
              fill="currentColor"
              d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"
            ></path>
          </svg>
        )}
        {children}
      </button>
    );
  },
);

Button.displayName = "Button";

FILE: src/system/atoms/FadeIn.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { ReactNode } from "react";

interface FadeInProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  duration?: number;
  delay?: number;
  y?: number;
}

export function FadeIn({ children, duration = 0.5, delay = 0, y = 10 }: FadeInProps) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: y }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{
        duration: duration,
        delay: delay,
        ease: [0.21, 0.47, 0.32, 0.98],
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/atoms/Heading.tsx
================================================
import React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/src/utils/lib/cn";

type HeadingLevel = "h1" | "h2" | "h3" | "h4" | "h5" | "h6";

interface HeadingProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLHeadingElement> {
  level?: HeadingLevel;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export function Heading({ level = "h2", children, className, ...props }: HeadingProps) {
  const Component = level;

  // Estilos base: Limpios, legibles, sin decoraciones
  const baseStyles = "font-sans text-foreground tracking-tight";

  // Jerarquía tipográfica minimalista
  const sizeStyles = {
    h1: "text-3xl sm:text-4xl md:text-5xl font-extrabold tracking-tighter",
    h2: "text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold",
    h3: "text-xl sm:tex-xl md:text-2xl font-semibold",
    h4: "text-xl md:text-2xl font-semibold",
    h5: "text-lg md:text-xl font-medium",
    h6: "text-base md:text-lg font-medium",
  };

  return (
    <Component className={cn(baseStyles, sizeStyles[level], className)} {...props}>
      {children}
    </Component>
  );
}

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";

interface MediaCoverProps {
  src: string | null | undefined;
  alt: string;
}

export function MediaCover({ src, alt }: MediaCoverProps) {
  if (!src || src === "" || typeof src !== "string") {
    return (
      <div className="relative w-full h-[50vh] flex items-center justify-center bg-muted/20 animate-pulse rounded-2xl border border-border/50">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border-4 border-muted border-t-var(--accent-color) animate-spin" />
          <span className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground/50">
            Cargando Media...
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Image
      src={src}
      alt={alt || "Media content"}
      width={1600}
      height={1200}
      className={cn(
        "block mx-auto rounded-2xl",
        "w-full h-auto",
        "max-h-[70vh] md:max-h-[80vh]",
        "object-contain md:object-contain",
        "transition-transform duration-700 ease-out group-hover:scale-[1.02]",
      )}
      quality={90}
      decoding="async"
    />
  );
}

FILE: src/system/atoms/Navbar/NavbarActions.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/store/authStore";
import { useTheme } from "@/src/utils/hooks/useTheme";
import { UserDropdown } from "../../molecules/Navbar/UserDropdown";
import { NotificationDropdown } from "../../molecules/Navbar/NotificationDropdown";
import { IconAlbum, IconSun, IconMoon, IconScanTraces } from "@tabler/icons-react";

export function NavbarActions() {
  const user = useAuthStore((state) => state.user);
  const isLoadingAuth = useAuthStore((state) => state.isLoadingAuth);

  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const [isMounted, setIsMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsMounted(true);
  }, []);

  if (!isMounted) {
    return <div className="hidden md:flex w-200px h-10 shrink-0" />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-1.5 lg:gap-1.5 shrink-0">
      {isLoadingAuth ? (
        <div className="w-32 h-9 rounded-lg bg-muted/40 animate-pulse"></div>
      ) : user ? (
        <>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Link
              href="/es/studio"
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-foreground text-background rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity"
            >
              <IconAlbum size={18} />
              Studio
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/es/reprompt"
              className="p-2 rounded-md text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-muted cursor-pointer"
              title="Mi Estudio"
            >
              <IconScanTraces size={22} />
            </Link>
          </div>
          <NotificationDropdown />
          <button
            onClick={toggleTheme}
            className="p-2 rounded-md text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted cursor-pointer transition-colors"
            title="Cambiar tema"
          >
            {isDark ? <IconSun size={24} /> : <IconMoon size={24} />}
          </button>
          <UserDropdown />
        </>
      ) : (
        /* Usuario NO Autenticado */
        <Link
          href="/es/login"
          className="text-sm font-bold px-4 py-2 bg-foreground text-background rounded-lg hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity"
        >
          Entrar
        </Link>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import React from "react";
import { IconWand, IconDownload } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { MediaVariant } from "@/src/types/models";

interface MediaOverlayProps {
  title: string;
  prompt?: string;
  tags?: string[];
  variant?: MediaVariant;
  onDownload?: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
}

export function MediaOverlay({ title, prompt, tags, variant = "gallery", onDownload }: MediaOverlayProps) {
  const isViewer = variant === "viewer";

  return (
    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-linear-to-t from-black/90 via-black/30 to-transparent opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300 flex flex-col justify-end p-4 pointer-events-none">
      
      {/* Botón de descarga interactivo (Top Right) */}
      {onDownload && (
        <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 flex gap-2 translate-y-2.5 opacity-0 group-hover:translate-y-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-all duration-300 delay-75 pointer-events-auto">
          <button 
            onClick={onDownload}
            className="p-2 bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md text-white rounded-lg transition-colors border border-white/10"
            title="Descargar imagen"
          >
            <IconDownload size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Contenido (Bottom) */}
      <div className="translate-y-2.5 opacity-0 group-hover:translate-y-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-all duration-300 delay-100 text-white">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold line-clamp-1 mb-1 shadow-black/50 drop-shadow-md">
          {title}
        </h3>
        
        {prompt && (
          <div className="flex items-start gap-1.5 mt-2 text-xs text-white/80 line-clamp-2 leading-tight">
            <IconWand size={14} className="shrink-0 text-var(--accent-color) mt-0.5" />
            <p className="font-mono">{prompt}</p>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Los tags se esconden en el visor para mayor limpieza */}
        {!isViewer && tags && tags.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mt-3">
            {tags.slice(0, 3).map(tag => (
              <span key={tag} className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider px-2 py-0.5 bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-sm">
                {tag}
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Admin/QuotaBar.tsx
================================================
import { IconAlertTriangle } from "@tabler/icons-react";

interface QuotaBarProps {
  label: string;
  currentMb: number;
  limitMb: number;
}

export function QuotaBar({ label, currentMb, limitMb }: QuotaBarProps) {
  const percentage = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (currentMb / limitMb) * 100));

  // Lógica de alerta visual
  let colorClass = "bg-green-500";
  if (percentage > 70) colorClass = "bg-yellow-500";
  if (percentage > 90) colorClass = "bg-red-500 animate-pulse";

  return (
    <div className="bg-background border border-border p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-2">
        <div>
          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
            {label}
          </p>
          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1 mt-1">
            <span className="text-2xl font-black text-foreground">{currentMb.toFixed(2)}</span>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground mb-1">
              / {limitMb.toLocaleString()} MB
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold">{percentage.toFixed(1)}%</span>
      </div>

      {/* La Barra de Progreso */}
      <div className="h-2 w-full bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden relative">
        <div
          className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-1000 ease-out ${colorClass}`}
          style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}
        />
      </div>

      {percentage > 90 && (
        <p className="text-[10px] text-red-500 mt-2 flex items-center gap-1 font-bold">
          <IconAlertTriangle size={12} /> PELIGRO DE COLAPSO DE CUOTA
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// src/system/molecules/Auth/CredentialsForm.tsx
import { IconMail, IconLock, IconTicket, IconLoader2 } from "@tabler/icons-react";

interface CredentialsFormProps {
  // ... (Mismos props de antes, no cambian)
  isLogin: boolean;
  isLoading: boolean;
  isSocialLoading: boolean;
  emailOrUser: string;
  password: string;
  inviteCode: string;
  isInviteLocked: boolean;
  onEmailChange: (value: string) => void;
  onPasswordChange: (value: string) => void;
  onInviteChange: (value: string) => void;
  onSubmit: (e: React.FormEvent) => void;
}

export function CredentialsForm({
  isLogin, isLoading, isSocialLoading, emailOrUser, password,
  inviteCode, isInviteLocked, onEmailChange, onPasswordChange, onInviteChange, onSubmit,
}: CredentialsFormProps) {
  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <div className="relative">
        <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3 flex items-center pointer-events-none text-(--text-secondary)">
          <IconMail size={18} />
        </div>
        <input
          type="text"
          required
          placeholder={isLogin ? "EMAIL O USUARIO" : "CORREO ELECTRÓNICO"}
          value={emailOrUser}
          onChange={(e) => onEmailChange(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full h-12 bg-background border border-border pl-10 pr-3 text-sm font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-(--accent-color) transition-colors placeholder:text-(--text-secondary)/50"
          disabled={isLoading}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="relative">
        <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3 flex items-center pointer-events-none text-(--text-secondary)">
          <IconLock size={18} />
        </div>
        <input
          type="password"
          required
          placeholder="••••••••"
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => onPasswordChange(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full h-12 bg-background border border-border pl-10 pr-3 text-sm font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-(--accent-color) transition-colors placeholder:text-(--text-secondary)/50"
          disabled={isLoading}
        />
      </div>

      {!isLogin && (
        <div className="relative animate-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-200">
          <div className={`absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3 flex items-center pointer-events-none ${isInviteLocked ? "text-(--accent-color)" : "text-(--text-secondary)"}`}>
            <IconTicket size={18} />
          </div>
          <input
            type="text"
            required
            placeholder="CÓDIGO DE INVITACIÓN"
            value={inviteCode}
            onChange={(e) => onInviteChange(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
            readOnly={isInviteLocked}
            className={`w-full h-12 border pl-10 pr-3 text-sm font-mono uppercase focus:outline-none transition-colors placeholder:text-(--text-secondary)/50 ${
              isInviteLocked
                ? "bg-(--accent-color)/10 border-(--accent-color) text-(--accent-color) font-black"
                : "bg-background border-border focus:border-(--accent-color)"
            }`}
            disabled={isLoading}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={isLoading || isSocialLoading}
        className="w-full justify-center h-12 mt-2 text-sm font-black uppercase font-mono tracking-widest bg-white text-black hover:bg-gray-200 neo-border transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        {isLoading ? (
          <IconLoader2 className="animate-spin mx-auto" size={20} />
        ) : isLogin ? (
          "Entrar"
        ) : (
          "Confirmar Acceso"
        )}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

  onGoogle,

          <IconBrandGoogleFilled size={18} className="text-red-500" />

      </button>

      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onDiscord}
        disabled={isDisabled}
        className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 w-full h-12 bg-[#5865F2] text-white hover:bg-[#4752C4] neo-border font-mono uppercase text-xs font-black tracking-widest transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"

          <IconLoader2 className="animate-spin" size={18} />
        ) : (
          <IconBrandDiscordFilled size={18} />
        )}
        Continuar con Discord
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Cards/CreateCollectionModal.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { IconX, IconLock, IconWorld } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/store/authStore";
import { UpgradeModal } from "../../organisms/Modal/UpgradeModal";

interface Props {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: () => void;
}

export function CreateDirectoryModal({ isOpen, onClose, onSuccess }: Props) {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [isPrivate, setIsPrivate] = useState(false);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

  const user = useAuthStore((state: any) => state.user);
  const [showUpgradeModal, setShowUpgradeModal] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim()) return toast.error("El nombre es obligatorio");

    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      await api.directories.create({ name, description, is_private: isPrivate });
      toast.success("Colección creada");
      setName("");
      setDescription("");
      setIsPrivate(false);
      onSuccess();
      onClose();
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error("Error al crear la colección");
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePrivacyToggle = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();

    const role = user?.role?.toLowerCase() || "voyager";

    console.log("Rol detectado:", role);

    if (!isPrivate && role === "voyager") {
      console.log("Bloqueando acción y abriendo Modal de Ventas...");
      setShowUpgradeModal(true);
    } else {
      console.log("Cambiando estado de privacidad...");
      setIsPrivate(!isPrivate);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-background/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 animate-fade-in">
        <div className="bg-background w-full max-w-md rounded-xl border border-border shadow-2xl overflow-hidden relative">
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
          >
            <IconX size={20} />
          </button>

          <div className="p-6">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-mono font-bold tracking-tight mb-6 text-(--accent-color)">
              Nueva Colección
            </h2>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <label className="text-xs font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
                  Nombre
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  autoFocus
                  placeholder="Ej. Cyberpunk Portraits"
                  value={name}
                  onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-muted/10 border border-border rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-(--accent-color)"
                  disabled={isLoading}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <label className="text-xs font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
                  Descripción (Opcional)
                </label>
                <textarea
                  placeholder="Un breve resumen de esta galería..."
                  value={description}
                  onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-muted/10 border border-border rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-(--accent-color) resize-none h-20"
                  disabled={isLoading}
                />
              </div>

              <div
                className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-muted/20 border border-border rounded-md mt-2 cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-muted/30"
                onClick={handlePrivacyToggle}
              >
                <div
                  className={`p-2 rounded-full transition-colors ${isPrivate ? "bg-red-500/10 text-red-500" : "bg-green-500/10 text-green-500"}`}
                >
                  {isPrivate ? <IconLock size={18} /> : <IconWorld size={18} />}
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 select-none">
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold">
                    {isPrivate ? "Colección Privada" : "Colección Pública"}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {isPrivate
                      ? "Solo tú podrás verla."
                      : "Visible en tu perfil para todo el mundo."}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isLoading}
                className="w-full mt-4 bg-accent/20 text-accent font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-widest py-3 border border-accent rounded-md hover:bg-accent/30 transition-all disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {isLoading ? "Creando..." : "Crear Colección"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style={{ position: "relative", zIndex: 99999 }}>
        <UpgradeModal
          isOpen={showUpgradeModal}
          onClose={() => setShowUpgradeModal(false)}
          title="Mantén tus Prompts en secreto"
          message={
            <>
              Tu plan actual <strong>Voyager</strong> crea colecciones públicas por defecto. Pásate
              al plan <strong className="text-primary">Artist</strong> para ocultar tus galerías de
              miradas indiscretas y proteger tus prompts.
            </>
          }
        />
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { IconStack2, IconUser } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { LazyImage } from "../Media/LazyImage";
import { FeedItem } from "@/src/types/models";

interface FeedItemCardProps {
  item: FeedItem;
  lang: string;
  isPriority?: boolean;
  index?: number;
}

export function FeedItemCard({ item, lang, isPriority = false, index = 0 }: FeedItemCardProps) {
  const href =
    item.type === "prompt"
      ? `/${lang}/${item.username}/p/${item.raw_id || item.id}`
      : `/${lang}/${item.username}/${item.slug}`;

  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      className="group relative cursor-pointer bg-muted/10 neo-border overflow-hidden aspect-4/5 hover:neo-shadow-accent transition-all block"
    >
      {item.image_url ? (
        <LazyImage
          src={item.image_url}
          className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500"
          alt={item.title}
          priority={isPriority}
          index={index}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="w-full h-full group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500">
          <img
            src="/logo-white.png"
            className="w-full h-full object-scale-down! dark:hidden opacity-80"
            alt="Portada por defecto"
          />
          <img
            src="/logo-black.png"
            className="w-full h-full object-scale-down hidden dark:block opacity-80"
            alt="Portada por defecto"
          />
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-20 bg-linear-to-t from-black/90 via-black/20 to-transparent opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300 flex flex-col justify-end p-4">
        <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 flex gap-2">
          {item.type === "directory" && (
            <span className="bg-foreground-600/60 text-blue-200 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 flex items-center gap-1 neo-border rounded-md shadow-sm">
              <IconStack2 size={12} stroke={2} /> {item.title}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-white font-bold text-sm truncate shadow-black">{item.title}</h3>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-1 text-white/80 text-xs font-mono">
          <IconUser size={12} /> @{item.username}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Cards/PromptCardActions.tsx

import { IconDotsVertical, IconFolder, IconTrash } from "@tabler/icons-react";

export function PromptCardActions({
  item,
  directories,
  onAssign,
  onDelete,
  isOpen,
  setIsOpen,
}: any) {
  return (
    <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 z-50">
      <button
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          setIsOpen(!isOpen);
        }}
        className={`p-1.5 rounded-md backdrop-blur-md border shadow-sm transition-colors ${
          isOpen ? "bg-background border-border" : "bg-black/40 border-white/20 text-white"
        }`}
      >
        <IconDotsVertical size={18} />
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="absolute top-full right-0 w-48 bg-background border border-border rounded-xl shadow-xl z-50 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 hover:border-red-500/50">
          {onAssign && directories.length > 0 && (
            <div className="px-3 py-2 border-b border-border/10">
              <label className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-muted-foreground mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <IconFolder size={12} /> Colección
              </label>
              <select
                className="w-full bg-muted/50 text-xs p-1.5 rounded border border-border"
                value={item.directory_id || ""}
                onChange={(e) => onAssign(item.id, e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null)}
              >
                <option value="">Suelto</option>
                {directories.map((d: any) => (
                  <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>
                    {d.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}
          <button
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              e.stopPropagation();
              if (window.confirm("¿Exterminar rastro digital?")) {
                if (typeof onDelete === "function") {
                  onDelete(item.id);
                } else {
                  console.error("❌ Error: onDelete no ha llegado al componente Actions.");
                }
              }
            }}
            className="flex w-full h-full items-center gap-2 px-3 py-3 text-xs font-mono font-normal text-red-400 hover:bg-red-500/10"
          >
            <IconTrash size={14} /> Eliminar
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Cards/PromptMetadataPanel.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { IconCopy, IconCheck, IconScanTraces, IconWand } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { SavedPrompt } from "@/src/types/models";
import { FadeIn } from "@/src/system/atoms/FadeIn";

interface PromptMetadataPanelProps {
  prompt: SavedPrompt;
}

export function PromptMetadataPanel({ prompt }: PromptMetadataPanelProps) {
  const [copiedSection, setCopiedSection] = useState<"prompt" | "negative" | null>(null);

  const handleCopy = (text: string, section: "prompt" | "negative") => {
    if (!text) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopiedSection(section);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedSection(null), 2000);
  };

  // 🛡️ PRAGMATISMO: Mapeamos solo los datos técnicos que existen realmente
  const techSpecs = [
    { label: "Modelo", value: prompt.model },
    { label: "Sampler", value: prompt.sampler },
    { label: "Software", value: prompt.software },
    { label: "Resolución", value: prompt.resolution },
  ].filter((spec) => spec.value); // Filtramos los nulos dinámicamente

  return (
    <FadeIn delay={0.2}>
      {/* 📝 SECCIÓN 1: El Prompt (La joya de la corona) */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="group relative bg-(--bg-secondary) neo-border p-4 transition-all hover:neo-shadow">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 border-b border-border/50 pb-2">
            <h3 className="font-mono text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2 text-(--accent-color)">
              <IconWand size={16} /> Prompt Principal
            </h3>
            {prompt.prompt && (
              <button
                onClick={() => handleCopy(prompt.prompt, "prompt")}
                className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition-colors"
                title="Copiar prompt"
              >
                {copiedSection === "prompt" ? (
                  <IconCheck size={18} className="text-green-500" />
                ) : (
                  <IconCopy size={18} />
                )}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className="font-mono text-sm leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap text-foreground/90">
            {prompt.prompt || <span className="italic opacity-50">No hay prompt registrado.</span>}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Negative Prompt (Opcional, asumiendo que tu modelo lo tenga, si no, se puede omitir) */}
        {/* Si tu modelo actual de SavedPrompt no tiene negative_prompt, puedes ignorar esta caja, pero en la industria suele existir */}
      </div>

      {/* ⚙️ SECCIÓN 2: Especificaciones Técnicas */}
      {techSpecs.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <h3 className="font-mono text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground">
            <IconScanTraces size={16} /> Datos de Generación
          </h3>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            {techSpecs.map((spec, idx) => (
              <div key={idx} className="bg-muted/10 neo-border p-3 flex flex-col gap-1">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest">
                  {spec.label}
                </span>
                <span className="text-sm font-bold truncate" title={spec.value}>
                  {spec.value}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </FadeIn>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Cards/hooks/usePromptCard.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

export function usePromptCard() {
  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false);
  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const handleCopy = (text: string) => {
    if (!text) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopied(true);
    toast.success("Prompt copiado");
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  return {
    isHovered,
    setIsHovered,
    isMenuOpen,
    setIsMenuOpen,
    copied,
    handleCopy,
  };
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Feedback/FeedState.tsx

import { IconDatabaseOff, IconAlertTriangle } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { PageLoader } from "../../atoms/PageLoader";

interface FeedStateProps {
  type: "loading" | "empty" | "error";
  message?: string;
  highlight?: string;
}

export function FeedState({ type, message, highlight }: FeedStateProps) {
  if (type === "loading") {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12">
        <PageLoader text="decodificando metadata ..."/>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (type === "error") {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-md mx-auto mt-8 p-4 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 text-red-500 rounded-lg flex items-start gap-3 shadow-sm animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-4">
        <IconAlertTriangle size={24} className="shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="font-mono text-sm leading-relaxed">{message}</div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-24 text-muted-foreground bg-muted/10 neo-border border-dashed rounded-lg mt-8">
      <IconDatabaseOff size={64} stroke={1} className="mb-4 opacity-50" />
      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground mb-2">Abismo vacío</h3>
      <p className="text-sm max-w-md text-center">
        No encontramos resultados para <span className="text-foreground font-bold">"{highlight}"</span>.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Media/LazyImage.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useState } from "react";

interface LazyImageProps {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  className?: string;
  sizes?: string;
  priority?: boolean;
  index?: number;
}

export function LazyImage({
  src,
  alt,
  className = "",
  priority = false,
  sizes = "(max-width: 768px) 100vw, (max-width: 1200px) 50vw, 33vw",
}: LazyImageProps) {
  const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="relative w-full h-full overflow-hidden bg-muted/10">
      <div
        className={`absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-muted/20 transition-opacity duration-500 z-0 ${
          isLoaded ? "opacity-0" : "opacity-100"
        }`}
      >
        <img
          src="/logo-white.png"
          alt="Cargando..."
          className="w-6 h-6 object-contain opacity-20 block dark:hidden"
        />
        <img
          src="/logo-black.png"
          alt="Cargando..."
          className="w-6 h-6 object-contain opacity-80 hidden dark:block"
        />
      </div>

      <Image
        src={src}
        alt={alt}
        sizes={sizes}
        fill
        priority={priority}
        quality={75}
        loading={priority ? undefined : "lazy"}
        onLoad={() => {
          setIsLoaded(true);
        }}
        className={`
          absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover z-10
          transition-all duration-700 ease-in-out
          ${isLoaded ? "opacity-100 blur-0 scale-100" : "opacity-0 blur-md scale-110"}
          ${className}
        `}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/src/store/authStore';
import { useTheme } from '@/src/utils/hooks/useTheme';
import { NavbarSearch } from './NavbarSearch';
import { IconMenu2, IconX, IconArchive, IconSun, IconMoon, IconScanTraces } from '@tabler/icons-react';

export function NavbarMobile() {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const { user } = useAuthStore();
  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();

  return (
    <div className="md:hidden flex items-center gap-2">
      <button 
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        className="p-2 text-foreground hover:bg-muted rounded-md transition-colors"
      >
        {isOpen ? <IconX size={24} /> : <IconMenu2 size={24} />}
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="absolute top-16 left-0 w-full bg-background border-b border-border shadow-2xl p-4 flex flex-col gap-4 animate-in slide-in-from-top-2 z-50">
          <NavbarSearch />
          
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-border/50">
            {user ? (
              <>
                <Link href="/reprompt" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-md hover:bg-muted font-bold text-sm text-foreground transition-colors">
                  <IconScanTraces size={20} /> Reprompt Studio
                </Link>
                <Link href="/studio" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-md hover:bg-muted font-bold text-sm text-foreground transition-colors">
                  <IconArchive size={20} /> Mi Estudio
                </Link>
              </>
            ) : (
              <Link href="/login" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="p-3 text-center bg-foreground text-background rounded-lg font-bold text-sm">
                Iniciar Sesión
              </Link>
            )}
            
            <button onClick={toggleTheme} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-md hover:bg-muted font-bold text-sm text-foreground transition-colors text-left">
              {isDark ? <IconSun size={20} /> : <IconMoon size={20} />}
              {isDark ? 'Modo Claro' : 'Modo Oscuro'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Navbar/NavbarSearch.test.tsx
================================================
import "@testing-library/jest-dom";

import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { useSearch } from "@/src/features/search/hooks/useSearch";
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from "@testing-library/react";
import { NavbarSearch } from "./NavbarSearch";

jest.mock("@/src/features/search/hooks/useSearch");
const mockUseSearch = useSearch as jest.MockedFunction<typeof useSearch>;

describe("Organism: NavbarSearch", () => {
  const defaultMockState = {
    query: "",
    setQuery: jest.fn(),
    handleSearch: jest.fn((e) => {
      if (e?.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();
      return Promise.resolve();
    }),
    isLoading: false,
    activeCategories: { misc: true },
    toggleCategory: jest.fn(),
    results: [],
    isLoadingMore: false,
    hasMore: false,
    error: null,
    loadMore: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve()),
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    mockUseSearch.mockReturnValue(defaultMockState as any);
  });

  describe("Renderizado Inicial", () => {
    it("debe renderizar el input de búsqueda correctamente", () => {
      render(<NavbarSearch />);
      const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText(/Buscar prompts/i);
      expect(input).toBeInTheDocument();
    });
  });

  describe("Interacción del Usuario (Happy Path)", () => {
    it("debe llamar a setQuery cuando el usuario escribe", () => {
      render(<NavbarSearch />);
      const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText(/Buscar prompts/i);

      // 🛡️ FIX TEST: Como el mock es estático y no re-renderiza con el nuevo 'query',
      // usamos fireEvent para inyectar el valor final de golpe en lugar de userEvent (tecla a tecla)
      fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "Cyberpunk" } });

      expect(defaultMockState.setQuery).toHaveBeenCalledWith("Cyberpunk");
    });

    it("debe llamar a handleSearch EXACTAMENTE UNA VEZ al enviar el formulario", async () => {
      render(<NavbarSearch />);
      const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText(/Buscar prompts/i);

      await userEvent.type(input, "Goku{enter}");

      expect(defaultMockState.handleSearch).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });
  });

  describe("Casos Límite y UX Avanzada (Edge Cases)", () => {
    it("debe desactivar el botón de búsqueda si está cargando (isLoading: true)", () => {
      mockUseSearch.mockReturnValue({ ...defaultMockState, isLoading: true } as any);
      render(<NavbarSearch />);

      const button = screen.getByRole("button");
      expect(button).toBeDisabled();
    });

    it('atajo de teclado "/": debe hacer focus en el input sin escribir la barra', () => {
      render(<NavbarSearch />);
      const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText(/Buscar prompts/i);

      fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: "/" });

      expect(input).toHaveFocus();
      expect(defaultMockState.setQuery).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith("/");
    });
  });
});
