FILE: src/system/molecules/Navbar/NavbarSearch.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useSearch } from "@/src/features/search/hooks/useSearch";
import { SearchCategorySelector } from "./SearchCategorySelector";
import { IconSearch, IconLoader2 } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { EH_CATEGORIES } from "@/src/constants/filters";

export function NavbarSearch() {
  const { query, setQuery, handleSearch, activeCategories, toggleCategory, isLoading } = useSearch();
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const onSubmit = useCallback((e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    handleSearch(e);
  }, [handleSearch]);

  const onInputChange = useCallback((e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    setQuery(e.target.value);
  }, [setQuery]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === '/' && document.activeElement?.tagName !== 'INPUT' && document.activeElement?.tagName !== 'TEXTAREA') {
        e.preventDefault();
        inputRef.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-2xl flex items-center h-10 bg-muted/30 dark:bg-white/0.03 border border-border/50 rounded-lg overflow-hidden focus-within:border-var(--accent-color)/50 focus-within:ring-1 focus-within:ring-var(--accent-color)/20 transition-all">
      <SearchCategorySelector 
        options={EH_CATEGORIES}
        activeCategories={activeCategories} 
        onToggle={toggleCategory} 
      />
      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="flex-1 h-full flex items-center relative">
        <input 
          ref={inputRef}
          value={query}
          onChange={onInputChange}
          className="w-full h-full bg-transparent px-4 text-sm outline-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground/60"
          placeholder="Buscar prompts, artistas... (Pulsa '/')"
        />
        <button 
          type="submit" 
          disabled={isLoading}
          className="absolute right-3 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors"
        >
          {isLoading ? <IconLoader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" /> : <IconSearch size={18} stroke={2} />}
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Navbar/NotificationDropdown.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useState, useRef } from "react";
import { useNotificationStore } from "@/src/store/notificationStore";
import { useClickOutside } from "@/src/utils/hooks/useClickOutside";
import {
  IconInbox,
  IconCheck,
  IconTrash,
  IconInfoCircle,
  IconAlertTriangle,
} from "@tabler/icons-react";

export function NotificationDropdown() {
  const { notifications, unreadCount, markAsRead, markAllAsRead, clearAll } =
    useNotificationStore();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useClickOutside(menuRef, () => setIsOpen(false));

  const getIcon = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case "success":
        return <IconCheck size={16} className="text-green-500" />;
      case "error":
        return <IconAlertTriangle size={16} className="text-red-500" />;
      case "warning":
        return <IconAlertTriangle size={16} className="text-yellow-500" />;
      default:
        return <IconInfoCircle size={16} className="text-blue-500" />;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={menuRef}>
      <button
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        className="relative p-2 rounded-md transition-colors text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-(--bg-secondary) cursor-pointer"
      >
        {unreadCount > 0 ? (
          <IconInbox size={24} className="text-(--accent-color)" />
        ) : (
          <IconInbox size={24} />
        )}
        {unreadCount > 0 && (
          <span className="p-2 absolute top-1 right-1 flex items-center justify-center min-w-4.5 h-4.5 bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] font-black font-mono rounded-full border-2 border-background">
            {unreadCount > 9 ? "9+" : unreadCount}
          </span>
        )}
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-80 sm:w-96 bg-[#1A1B1E] border-2 border-(--border-color) shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgb(0,0,0,0.8)] rounded-lg flex flex-col z-100 animate-fade-in origin-top-right overflow-hidden">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="p-4 border-b-2 border-(--border-color) bg-black/40 flex justify-between items-center">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold font-mono uppercase tracking-wide">Notificaciones</h3>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {unreadCount > 0 && (
                <button
                  onClick={markAllAsRead}
                  className="text-xs text-(--text-secondary) hover:text-foreground transition-colors"
                  title="Marcar todo como leído"
                >
                  <IconCheck size={16} />
                </button>
              )}
              {notifications.length > 0 && (
                <button
                  onClick={clearAll}
                  className="text-xs text-(--text-secondary) hover:text-red-500 transition-colors"
                  title="Limpiar bandeja"
                >
                  <IconTrash size={16} />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Lista de Mensajes */}
          <div className="max-h-96 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar flex flex-col">
            {notifications.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-8 text-center text-sm font-mono text-(--text-secondary)">
                Bandeja vacía.
              </div>
            ) : (
              notifications.map((n) => (
                <div
                  key={n.id}
                  onClick={() => markAsRead(n.id)}
                  className={`p-4 border-b border-(--border-color)/50 cursor-pointer transition-colors flex gap-3 ${n.read ? "bg-background opacity-70" : "bg-(--accent-color)/5"}`}
                >
                  <div className="mt-0.5">{getIcon(n.type)}</div>
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 w-full">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2">
                      <span
                        className={`text-xs font-bold font-mono uppercase truncate ${!n.read ? "text-foreground" : "text-(--text-secondary)"}`}
                      >
                        {n.title}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-(--text-secondary) whitespace-nowrap">
                        {n.timestamp.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-(--text-secondary) leading-snug line-clamp-2">
                      {n.message}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Navbar/SearchCategorySelector.tsx
================================================
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { IconChevronDown } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import {
  Combobox,
  ComboboxChips,
  ComboboxChipsInput,
  ComboboxContent,
  ComboboxEmpty,
  ComboboxItem,
  ComboboxList,
  ComboboxValue,
  useComboboxAnchor,
} from "@/components/ui/combobox"

interface Option {
  id: string;
  label: string;
}

interface Props {
  options: Option[];
  activeCategories: Record<string, boolean>;
  onToggle: (id: string) => void;
}

export function SearchCategorySelector({ options, activeCategories, onToggle }: Props) {
  const anchor = useComboboxAnchor();
  
  const selectedLabels = React.useMemo(() => 
    options.filter(c => activeCategories[c.id]).map(c => c.label)
  , [options, activeCategories]);

  const handleValueChange = React.useCallback((labels: string[]) => {
    options.forEach(c => {
      const isActive = !!activeCategories[c.id];
      const shouldBeActive = labels.includes(c.label);
      if (isActive !== shouldBeActive) onToggle(c.id);
    });
  }, [options, activeCategories, onToggle]);

  return (
    <Combobox
      multiple
      items={options.map(c => c.label)}
      value={selectedLabels}
      onValueChange={handleValueChange}
    >
      <ComboboxChips 
        ref={anchor} 
        className="relative w-140px h-full flex items-center px-3 bg-muted/60 dark:bg-white/5 border-transparent border-r-border/50 cursor-pointer hover:bg-muted transition-colors rounded-none shadow-none"
      >
        <ComboboxValue>
          {(values) => (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full pointer-events-none z-10">
              {values.length === 0 ? (
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-muted-foreground uppercase">Filtros</span>
              ) : (
                <span className="text-[11px] font-black text-var(--accent-color) uppercase tracking-tight">
                  {values.length === options.length ? "Todo" : `${values.length} CAT`}
                </span>
              )}
              <IconChevronDown size={14} className="text-muted-foreground" />
            </div>
          )}
        </ComboboxValue>
        <ComboboxChipsInput className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full opacity-0 cursor-pointer z-20" readOnly />
      </ComboboxChips>

      <ComboboxContent anchor={anchor} className="z-101 min-w-200px bg-background border border-border shadow-xl">
        <ComboboxEmpty className="p-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">No hay categorías.</ComboboxEmpty>
        <ComboboxList className="max-h-300px overflow-y-auto">
          {(item) => {
            const cat = options.find(c => c.label === item);
            const isActive = cat ? activeCategories[cat.id] : false;
            
            return (
              <ComboboxItem key={item} value={item} className="text-xs font-medium cursor-pointer p-2 hover:bg-muted">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full">
                  {item}
                  {isActive && <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-var(--accent-color) rounded-full shadow-[0_0_5px_var(--accent-color)]" />}
                </div>
              </ComboboxItem>
            );
          }}
        </ComboboxList>
      </ComboboxContent>
    </Combobox>
  )
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Navbar/UserDropdown.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, useRef } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useAuthStore } from "@/src/store/authStore";
import { useTheme } from "@/src/utils/hooks/useTheme";
import { useClickOutside } from "@/src/utils/hooks/useClickOutside";
import { ChangePasswordModal } from "@/src/system/organisms/Auth/ChangePasswordModal";

import {
  IconMenu2,
  IconX,
  IconSun,
  IconMoon,
  IconLogout,
  IconDashboard,
  IconKey,
  IconUserPlus,
} from "@tabler/icons-react";

export function UserDropdown() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, logout } = useAuthStore();
  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();

  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [showPwdModal, setShowPwdModal] = useState(false);

  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useClickOutside(menuRef, () => setIsMenuOpen(false));

  if (!user) return null;

  const menuItems = [];

  if (user.role === "admin") {
    menuItems.push(
      {
        label: "Panel de Control",
        icon: <IconDashboard size={18} />,
        action: () => {
          router.push("/admin");
          setIsMenuOpen(false);
        },
      },
      {
        label: "Gestionar Accesos",
        icon: <IconUserPlus size={18} />,
        action: () => {
          router.push("/admin");
          setIsMenuOpen(false);
        },
      },
    );
  }

  menuItems.push({
    label: "Cambiar Clave",
    icon: <IconKey size={18} />,
    action: () => {
      setShowPwdModal(true);
      setIsMenuOpen(false);
    },
  });

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={menuRef}>
      <button
        onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(!isMenuOpen)}
        className={`p-2 neo-border transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${
          isMenuOpen
            ? "bg-(--accent-color) text-white neo-shadow-accent -translate-y-1"
            : "bg-background text-foreground hover:neo-shadow hover:-translate-y-0.5"
        }`}
        aria-label="Menú de usuario"
      >
        {isMenuOpen ? <IconX size={24} /> : <IconMenu2 size={24} />}
      </button>

      {isMenuOpen && (
        <div className="absolute right-0 mt-3 w-64 bg-(--bg-secondary) neo-border neo-shadow flex flex-col z-50 animate-fade-in origin-top-right">
          <div className="p-4 border-b-2 border-(--border-color) bg-background">
            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground truncate">@{user.username}</p>
            <p className="text-xs font-mono text-(--accent-color) uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">
              {user.role}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col p-2 gap-1">
            {menuItems.map((item, index) => (
              <button
                key={index}
                onClick={item.action}
                className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 text-sm font-bold text-foreground hover:bg-background neo-border border-transparent hover:border-(--border-color) transition-all text-left uppercase tracking-wide"
              >
                {item.icon}
                {item.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="border-t-2 border-(--border-color) p-2 flex flex-col gap-1 bg-background">
            <button
              onClick={toggleTheme}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 text-sm font-bold text-foreground hover:bg-(--bg-secondary) neo-border border-transparent hover:border-(--border-color) transition-all text-left uppercase tracking-wide"
            >
              {isDark ? (
                <IconSun size={18} className="text-yellow-500" />
              ) : (
                <IconMoon size={18} className="text-blue-500" />
              )}
              {isDark ? "Modo Claro" : "Modo Oscuro"}
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={logout}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 text-sm font-black text-red-500 hover:bg-red-500/10 neo-border border-transparent hover:border-red-500/50 transition-all text-left uppercase tracking-wide"
            >
              <IconLogout size={18} />
              Cerrar Sesión
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {showPwdModal && <ChangePasswordModal onClose={() => setShowPwdModal(false)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/molecules/Search/UniversalSearchBar.tsx
================================================
// src/system/molecules/Search/UniversalSearchBar.tsx
"use client";

import { useRef, useEffect } from "react";
import { IconSearch, IconLoader2, IconX } from "@tabler/icons-react";

interface UniversalSearchBarProps {
  query: string;
  isLoading: boolean;
  placeholder?: string;
  onQueryChange: (q: string) => void;
  onSubmit: () => void;
  onClear: () => void;
  enableKeyboardShortcut?: boolean;
}

export function UniversalSearchBar({
  query,
  isLoading,
  placeholder = "Buscar...",
  onQueryChange,
  onSubmit,
  onClear,
  enableKeyboardShortcut = false,
}: UniversalSearchBarProps) {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enableKeyboardShortcut) return;
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      const isInput =
        target.tagName === "INPUT" || target.tagName === "TEXTAREA" || target.isContentEditable;
      if (e.key === "/" && !isInput) {
        e.preventDefault();
        inputRef.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, [enableKeyboardShortcut]);

  return (
    <div className="relative flex items-center w-full h-10 bg-muted/20 border border-border/40 rounded-xl focus-within:border-(--accent-color)/50 focus-within:ring-1 focus-within:ring-(--accent-color)/20 transition-all backdrop-blur-sm">
      <form
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          onSubmit();
        }}
        className="flex-1 h-full flex items-center relative pl-3"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-5 h-5">
          {isLoading ? (
            <IconLoader2 size={16} className="animate-spin text-(--accent-color)" />
          ) : (
            <IconSearch size={16} className="text-muted-foreground/60" />
          )}
        </div>

        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => onQueryChange(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full h-full bg-transparent px-3 text-sm outline-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground/40 font-medium"
          placeholder={placeholder}
        />

        {query && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClear}
            className="pr-3 text-muted-foreground/40 hover:text-foreground transition-colors"
          >
            <IconX size={14} />
          </button>
        )}
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import { Directory } from "@/src/store/useStudioUI";
import {
  IconChevronDown,
  IconChevronRight,
  IconFolderOpen,
  IconFolder,
  IconFolderPlus,
  IconTrash,
  IconCloudLock,
} from "@tabler/icons-react";

interface SidebarDirectoryItemProps {
  directory: Directory;
  level: number;
  isOpen: boolean;
  isSelected: boolean;
  isDragging: boolean;
  isOver: boolean;
  isEffectivelyPrivate: boolean;
  hasChildren: boolean;
  setBothRefs: (node: HTMLElement | null) => void;
  attributes: any;
  listeners: any;
  onSelect: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  onToggle: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  onRename: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  onCreateSub: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  onDelete: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
}

export const SidebarDirectoryItem = ({
  directory,
  level,
  isOpen,
  isSelected,
  isDragging,
  isOver,
  isEffectivelyPrivate,
  hasChildren,
  setBothRefs,
  attributes,
  listeners,
  onSelect,
  onToggle,
  onRename,
  onCreateSub,
  onDelete,
}: SidebarDirectoryItemProps) => {
  return (
    <div
      ref={setBothRefs}
      style={{
        opacity: isDragging ? 0.4 : 1,
        cursor: isDragging ? "grabbing" : "pointer",
        paddingLeft: `${level * 12 + 12}px`,
      }}
      {...attributes}
      {...listeners}
      onClick={onSelect}
      onDoubleClick={onRename}
      className={`
        group font-mono flex items-center py-1.5 px-2 mx-2 rounded-md transition-all duration-200 relative
        ${isSelected ? "bg-primary/5 text-foreground font-medium" : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}
        ${isOver ? "bg-primary/10 ring-2 ring-primary/30 border-dashed" : "hover:bg-secondary/40 border border-transparent"}
        ${isDragging ? "bg-background border-dashed border-primary/50" : ""}
      `}
    >
      {isSelected && (
        <div className="absolute left-0 w-1 h-6 bg-primary rounded-r-full shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(var(--primary-rgb),0.4)] top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
      )}

      <span
        className="w-4 h-4 mr-1 flex items-center justify-center z-10 hover:bg-muted/50 rounded transition-colors"
        onClick={onToggle}
      >
        {hasChildren && (isOpen ? <IconChevronDown size={14} /> : <IconChevronRight size={14} />)}
      </span>

      <span className="mr-2 relative flex items-center justify-center">
        {isOpen ? (
          <IconFolderOpen size={16} className={isSelected ? "text-primary/70" : "text-foreground/40"} />
        ) : (
          <IconFolder size={16} className={isSelected ? "text-primary/70" : "text-foreground/40"} />
        )}

        {isEffectivelyPrivate && (
          <div className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1.5 bg-background rounded-full p-px">
            <IconCloudLock size={12} className="text-red-300" stroke={2} />
          </div>
        )}
      </span>

      <span className="text-sm truncate flex-1">{directory.name}</span>

      <div className="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 flex items-center gap-1 transition-opacity pointer-events-auto">
        <button
          className="p-1 hover:bg-primary/20 rounded transition-colors text-primary/70"
          title="Nueva Subcarpeta"
          onClick={onCreateSub}
        >
          <IconFolderPlus size={14} />
        </button>
        <button
          className="p-1 hover:bg-destructive/20 hover:text-destructive/90 text-muted-foreground/40 rounded transition-colors"
          title="Eliminar"
          onClick={onDelete}
        >
          <IconTrash size={14} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface AuthTabsProps {
  isLogin: boolean;
  onToggle: (isLogin: boolean) => void;
}

export function AuthTabs({ isLogin, onToggle }: AuthTabsProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex w-full border-b border-border">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => onToggle(true)}
        className={`flex-1 pb-4 text-xs font-black tracking-widest uppercase font-mono transition-colors ${
          isLogin
            ? "text-(--accent-color) border-b-2 border-(--accent-color)"
            : "text-(--text-secondary) hover:text-foreground"
        }`}
      >
        Iniciar Sesión
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => onToggle(false)}
        className={`flex-1 pb-4 text-xs font-black tracking-widest uppercase font-mono transition-colors ${
          !isLogin
            ? "text-(--accent-color) border-b-2 border-(--accent-color)"
            : "text-(--text-secondary) hover:text-foreground"
        }`}
      >
        Registrarse
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

                    {log.details}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))
            )}

        </table>
      </div>

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { DatabaseStatsResponse } from "@/src/services/api/admin";
import {
  IconUsers,
  IconPhoto,
  IconDatabaseShare,
  IconDotsVertical,
  IconTrash,
  IconKey,
  IconStar,
  IconBan,
  IconExternalLink,
  IconActivity,
} from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { MetricCard } from "../../molecules/Admin/MetricCard";
import { QuotaBar } from "../../molecules/Admin/QuotaBar";
import { UserActivityModal } from "./UserActivityModal";

export function DatabaseModule() {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<DatabaseStatsResponse | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [openMenuId, setOpenMenuId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [activityModalUser, setActivityModalUser] = useState<{
    id: string;
    username: string;
  } | null>(null);

  const fetchStats = async () => {
    try {
      const data = await api.admin.getDatabaseStats();
      setStats(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || "Error de conexión");
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchStats();
  }, []);

  const handleAction = async (
    userId: string,
    action: "upgrade" | "deactivate" | "activate" | "reset_password",
  ) => {
    setOpenMenuId(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.admin.manageUser(userId, action);
      if (action === "reset_password") {
        alert(
          `🚨 NUEVA CONTRASEÑA GENERADA:\n\n${res.new_password}\n\nCópiala antes de cerrar. No se volverá a mostrar.`,
        );
      } else {
        toast.success(res.message);
      }
      fetchStats();
    } catch (err: any) {
      toast.error(err.message || "Fallo al ejecutar acción");
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (userId: string, username: string) => {
    setOpenMenuId(null);
    const code = prompt(
      `¡PELIGRO! Vas a borrar a @${username} y TODAS sus imágenes de R2.\n\nEscribe EXTERMINAR para confirmar:`,
    );

    if (code === "EXTERMINAR") {
      try {
        toast.loading("Ejecutando exterminio en cascada...", { id: "delete" });
        const res = await api.admin.deleteUser(userId);
        toast.success(res.message, { id: "delete" });
        fetchStats();
      } catch (err: any) {
        toast.error(err.message || "Fallo crítico en borrado", { id: "delete" });
      }
    }
  };

  if (error) return <div className="text-red-500 font-mono">{error}</div>;
  if (isLoading || !stats)
    return <div className="animate-pulse h-64 bg-muted/20 rounded-xl border border-border/50" />;

  return (
    <>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <MetricCard
            title="Usuarios Totales"
            value={stats.total_users.toLocaleString()}
            icon={<IconUsers size={24} className="text-blue-500" />}
          />
          <MetricCard
            title="Imágenes Indexadas"
            value={stats.total_prompts.toLocaleString()}
            icon={<IconPhoto size={24} className="text-purple-500" />}
          />
          <QuotaBar
            label="Cuota Disco Neon DB (Free Tier)"
            currentMb={stats.total_mb}
            limitMb={stats.limit_mb}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="bg-background border border-border rounded-xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden pb-32">
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-border flex items-center gap-2 bg-muted/20">
            <IconDatabaseShare size={18} className="text-muted-foreground" />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-sm uppercase tracking-widest text-foreground">
              Últimos Registros (Neon DB)
            </h3>
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-x-auto min-h-300px">
            <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
              <thead className="text-xs text-muted-foreground uppercase bg-muted/30 border-b border-border">
                <tr>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 font-mono">Usuario</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 font-mono">Rol</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 font-mono">Status</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 font-mono text-right">Acciones</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-border">
                {stats.users.map((user) => (
                  <tr
                    key={user.id}
                    className={`transition-colors ${
                      !user.is_active ? "opacity-50 bg-red-500/5" : "hover:bg-muted/10"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-foreground">
                      @{user.username}
                      {user.email && (
                        <span className="block text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">
                          {user.email}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <span
                        className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider ${
                          user.role === "admin"
                            ? "bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 border border-red-500/20"
                            : user.role === "artist"
                              ? "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-500 border border-purple-500/20"
                              : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground border border-border"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {user.role}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      {user.is_active ? (
                        <span className="text-green-500 text-xs font-mono">● Activo</span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-red-500 text-xs font-mono">⊗ BANEADO</span>
                      )}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right relative">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setOpenMenuId(openMenuId === user.id ? null : user.id)}
                        className="p-1.5 hover:bg-muted rounded-md transition-colors"
                      >
                        <IconDotsVertical size={18} />
                      </button>

                      {openMenuId === user.id && (
                        <div className="absolute right-8 top-10 z-50 w-52 bg-background border border-border rounded-lg shadow-xl py-1 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95">
                          <Link
                            href={`/es/${user.username}`}
                            target="_blank"
                            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs hover:bg-muted/50 w-full text-left"
                          >
                            <IconExternalLink size={14} /> Ver Perfil Público
                          </Link>

                          <button
                            onClick={() => {
                              setActivityModalUser({ id: user.id, username: user.username });
                              setOpenMenuId(null);
                            }}
                            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-500/10 w-full text-left"
                          >
                            <IconActivity size={14} /> Ver Rastro de Actividad
                          </button>

                          {user.role === "voyager" && (
                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleAction(user.id, "upgrade")}
                              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-purple-500 hover:bg-purple-500/10 w-full text-left"
                            >
                              <IconStar size={14} /> Ascender a Artist
                            </button>
                          )}

                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleAction(user.id, "reset_password")}
                            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-500/10 w-full text-left border-t border-border/50 mt-1 pt-2"
                          >
                            <IconKey size={14} /> Forzar Nueva Clave
                          </button>

                          {user.is_active ? (
                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleAction(user.id, "deactivate")}
                              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-orange-500 hover:bg-orange-500/10 w-full text-left"
                            >
                              <IconBan size={14} /> Shadowban (Pausar)
                            </button>
                          ) : (
                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleAction(user.id, "activate")}
                              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-green-500 hover:bg-green-500/10 w-full text-left"
                            >
                              <IconBan size={14} /> Quitar Ban
                            </button>
                          )}

                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleDelete(user.id, user.username)}
                            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-red-500 hover:bg-red-500/20 w-full text-left border-t border-red-500/20 mt-1 pt-2"
                          >
                            <IconTrash size={14} /> Exterminar Cuenta
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {activityModalUser && (
        <UserActivityModal
          userId={activityModalUser.id}
          username={activityModalUser.username}
          onClose={() => setActivityModalUser(null)}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Admin/GarbageCollectorModule.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { IconTrashX, IconLoader2, IconAlertTriangle } from "@tabler/icons-react";

export function GarbageCollectorModule() {
  const [isPurging, setIsPurging] = useState(false);
  const [confirmStep, setConfirmStep] = useState(false);

  const handlePurge = async () => {
    if (!confirmStep) {
      setConfirmStep(true);
      setTimeout(() => setConfirmStep(false), 3000);
      return;
    }

    setIsPurging(true);
    try {
      const result = await api.admin.runGarbageCollector();

      toast.success(
        <div>
          <b>¡Purga Exitosa!</b>
          <p className="text-xs mt-1">
            Logs: {result.deleted_logs} | Invites: {result.deleted_invites} | Archivos R2:{" "}
            {result.deleted_temp_files}
          </p>
        </div>,
        { duration: 5000 },
      );
      setConfirmStep(false);
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error(error.message || "Fallo crítico en el recolector de basura");
    } finally {
      setIsPurging(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-red-500/5 border border-red-500/20 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between h-full relative overflow-hidden group">
      {/* Fondo de alerta sutil */}
      <IconAlertTriangle
        size={120}
        className="absolute -right-6 -bottom-6 text-red-500/5 -rotate-12 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-700"
      />

      <div className="relative z-10 mb-6">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-red-500 flex items-center gap-2 uppercase tracking-widest text-sm">
          <IconTrashX size={18} /> Garbage Collector
        </h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-2 font-mono">
          Purga logs de +30 días, invitaciones usadas y vacía la carpeta{" "}
          <code className="text-red-400">/temp</code> de Cloudflare R2 (+24h).
        </p>
      </div>

      <button
        onClick={handlePurge}
        disabled={isPurging}
        className={`relative z-10 w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm uppercase tracking-widest transition-all ${
          isPurging
            ? "bg-red-500/20 text-red-500 cursor-not-allowed"
            : confirmStep
              ? "bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700 animate-pulse"
              : "bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 border border-red-500/20 hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white"
        }`}
      >
        {isPurging ? (
          <span className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
            <IconLoader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" /> Purgando Sistema...
          </span>
        ) : confirmStep ? (
          "¿ESTÁS SEGURO? CLIC PARA PURGAR"
        ) : (
          "Ejecutar Purga del Sistema"
        )}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Admin/StorageModule.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { StorageStatsResponse } from "@/src/services/api/admin";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import {
  IconCloudDataConnection,
  IconFiles,
  IconAlertCircle,
  IconServer,
  IconFlame,
} from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { QuotaBar } from "../../molecules/Admin/QuotaBar";

export function StorageModule() {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<StorageStatsResponse | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [isPurging, setIsPurging] = useState(false);
  const [confirmPurge, setConfirmPurge] = useState(false);

  const fetchStorage = async () => {
    try {
      const data = await api.admin.getStorageStats();
      setStats(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || "Error al conectar con Cloudflare R2");
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const initialFetch = async () => {
      try {
        setIsLoading(true);
        const data = await api.admin.getStorageStats();
        if (isMounted) setStats(data);
      } catch (err: any) {
        if (isMounted) setError(err.message || "Error al conectar con Cloudflare R2");
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };

    initialFetch();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const handlePurge = async () => {
    if (!confirmPurge) {
      setConfirmPurge(true);
      setTimeout(() => setConfirmPurge(false), 4000);
      return;
    }

    const finalConfirm = prompt(
      "ESTO BORRARÁ TODO EL BUCKET R2.\nEscribe DESTRUIR para confirmar:",
    );
    if (finalConfirm !== "DESTRUIR") {
      setConfirmPurge(false);
      return;
    }

    setIsPurging(true);
    try {
      toast.loading("Aniquilando bucket R2...", { id: "purge" });
      const result = await api.admin.purgeStorage();
      toast.success(result.message, { id: "purge", duration: 5000 });
      await fetchStorage();
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error(error.message || "Fallo al purgar R2", { id: "purge" });
    } finally {
      setIsPurging(false);
      setConfirmPurge(false);
    }
  };

  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 rounded-xl flex items-start gap-3 text-red-500">
        <IconAlertCircle className="shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div>
          <h3 className="font-bold">Alerta de Infraestructura (R2)</h3>
          <p className="text-sm opacity-80">{error}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (isLoading || !stats) {
    return <StorageModuleSkeleton />;
  }

  const isStorageHealthy = stats.total_mb < 10000;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-500">
      <QuotaBar
        label="Cuota Mensual Cloudflare R2 (Free Tier)"
        currentMb={stats.total_mb}
        limitMb={stats.limit_mb}
      />

      <div
        className={`p-4 border rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between shadow-sm ${
          isStorageHealthy
            ? "bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/20"
            : "bg-yellow-500/10 border-yellow-500/20"
        }`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <IconCloudDataConnection
            className={isStorageHealthy ? "text-green-500" : "text-yellow-500"}
          />
          <div>
            <h3
              className={`font-bold text-sm uppercase tracking-widest ${isStorageHealthy ? "text-green-600 dark:text-green-400" : "text-yellow-600 dark:text-yellow-400"}`}
            >
              Cloudflare R2 (Object Storage)
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">
              {isStorageHealthy ? "Operativo y Saludable" : "Acercándose a cuota límite"}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono">
          <span className="relative flex h-3 w-3">
            <span
              className={`animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full opacity-75 ${isStorageHealthy ? "bg-green-400" : "bg-yellow-400"}`}
            ></span>
            <span
              className={`relative inline-flex rounded-full h-3 w-3 ${isStorageHealthy ? "bg-green-500" : "bg-yellow-500"}`}
            ></span>
          </span>
          En línea
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-background border border-border p-5 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground mb-1">
              Ancho de Disco
            </p>
            <p className="text-3xl font-black text-foreground">
              {Number(stats.total_mb).toFixed(2)}{" "}
              <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground font-normal">MB</span>
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 bg-muted/30 rounded-lg text-blue-500">
            <IconServer size={24} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-background border border-border p-5 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground mb-1">
              Archivos en R2
            </p>
            <p className="text-3xl font-black text-foreground">
              {stats.total_files.toLocaleString()}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 bg-muted/30 rounded-lg text-purple-500">
            <IconFiles size={24} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <button
        onClick={handlePurge}
        disabled={isPurging}
        className={`w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm uppercase tracking-widest transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${
          isPurging
            ? "bg-red-500/20 text-red-500 cursor-not-allowed"
            : confirmPurge
              ? "bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700 animate-pulse border border-red-500 shadow-lg shadow-red-500/20"
              : "bg-red-500/5 text-red-500 border border-red-500/20 hover:bg-red-500/10"
        }`}
      >
        <IconFlame size={18} />
        {isPurging
          ? "Aniquilando..."
          : confirmPurge
            ? "¿SEGURO? ESTO BORRARÁ TODO EL BUCKET"
            : "Purgar Bucket Completo (Hard Reset)"}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function StorageModuleSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="h-74px bg-muted/40 rounded-xl animate-pulse border border-border/50"></div>
      <div className="h-74px bg-muted/40 rounded-xl animate-pulse border border-border/50"></div>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div className="h-28 bg-muted/40 rounded-xl animate-pulse border border-border/50"></div>
        <div className="h-28 bg-muted/40 rounded-xl animate-pulse border border-border/50"></div>
      </div>
      <div className="h-12 bg-muted/40 rounded-xl animate-pulse border border-border/50"></div>
    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/system/organisms/Admin/UserActivityModal.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { UserActivityResponse } from "@/src/services/api/admin";
import { IconX, IconActivity, IconClock } from "@tabler/icons-react";

interface UserActivityModalProps {
  userId: string;
  username: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function UserActivityModal({ userId, username, onClose }: UserActivityModalProps) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<UserActivityResponse | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchActivity = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.admin.getUserActivity(userId);
        setData(res);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(error);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchActivity();
  }, [userId]);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex justify-end bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md h-full bg-background border-l border-border shadow-2xl flex flex-col animate-in slide-in-from-right-full">
        {/* Cabecera */}
        <div className="p-6 border-b border-border flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black font-mono uppercase italic flex items-center gap-2">
              <IconActivity className="text-(--accent-color)" /> Rastro
            </h2>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
              @{username}
              {data && (
                <span
                  className={`flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                    data.is_online
                      ? "bg-green-500/10 text-green-500"
                      : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"
                  }`}
                >
                  <span
                    className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${data.is_online ? "bg-green-500 animate-pulse" : "bg-muted-foreground"}`}
                  ></span>
                  {data.is_online ? "ONLINE" : "OFFLINE"}
                </span>
              )}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 bg-muted/50 hover:bg-muted rounded-full transition-colors"
          >
            <IconX size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Línea de tiempo (Timeline) */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-6">
          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="animate-pulse space-y-4">
              {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
                <div key={i} className="h-16 bg-muted/20 rounded-xl" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : data?.logs.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-center text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-10">
              No hay registros recientes.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="relative border-l-2 border-border ml-3 space-y-8">
              {data?.logs.map((log) => (
                <div key={log.id} className="relative pl-6">
                  {/* Punto en la línea de tiempo */}
                  <div className="absolute -left-9px top-1 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-background border-2 border-(--accent-color)" />

                  <div className="bg-muted/10 border border-border p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm">
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-(--accent-color) mb-1">
                      {log.action}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-sm text-foreground mb-2">{log.details}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground font-mono flex items-center gap-1">
                      <IconClock size={12} /> {new Date(log.timestamp).toLocaleString()}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useAuthForm } from "@/src/features/auth/hooks/useAuthForm";
import { AuthTabs } from "../../molecules/Tabs/AuthTabs";
import { AuthHeader } from "../../molecules/Auth/AuthHeader";
import { SocialLogins } from "../../molecules/Auth/SocialLogins";
import { AuthSeparator } from "../../atoms/Login/AuthSeparator";
import { CredentialsForm } from "../../molecules/Auth/CredentialsForm";

interface AuthFormProps {
  lang: string;
}

export function AuthForm({lang}: AuthFormProps) {
  const { state, actions } = useAuthForm();

  if (state.oauthPending) {
    return (
      <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto p-8 bg-(--bg-secondary) neo-border neo-shadow animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-300">
        <div className="mb-8 text-center">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black uppercase font-mono tracking-tighter">
            Último <span className="text-(--accent-color)">Paso</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs font-mono text-(--text-secondary) mt-2 uppercase">
            Vinculando: {state.oauthPending.email}
          </p>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={actions.handleOauthSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-6">
          <div className="relative">
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              placeholder="CÓDIGO DE INVITACIÓN"
              value={state.inviteCode}
              onChange={(e) => actions.setInviteCode(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
              className="w-full h-12 bg-background border border-border pl-4 pr-3 text-sm font-mono uppercase focus:outline-none focus:border-(--accent-color) transition-colors"
              disabled={state.isLoading}
              lang={lang}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col-reverse sm:flex-row gap-4">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={actions.cancelOauthFlow}
              disabled={state.isLoading}
              className="w-full sm:w-1/3 justify-center h-12 bg-transparent text-foreground border border-border hover:bg-muted font-mono uppercase text-xs font-black tracking-widest transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
              lang={lang}
            >
              Volver
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={state.isLoading}
              className="w-full sm:w-2/3 justify-center h-12 text-sm font-black uppercase font-mono tracking-widest bg-white text-black hover:bg-gray-200 neo-border transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {state.isLoading ? "Procesando..." : "Completar"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto p-8 bg-(--bg-secondary) neo-border neo-shadow animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-300">
      <AuthTabs isLogin={state.isLogin} onToggle={actions.setIsLogin} />

      <div className="mt-8">
        <AuthHeader isLogin={state.isLogin} />

        <SocialLogins
          isLoading={state.isLoading}
          isGoogleLoading={state.isGoogleLoading}
          isDiscordLoading={state.isDiscordLoading}
          onGoogle={actions.handleGoogleLogin}
          onDiscord={actions.handleDiscordLogin}
        />

        <AuthSeparator />

        <CredentialsForm
          isLogin={state.isLogin}
          isLoading={state.isLoading}
          isSocialLoading={state.isGoogleLoading || state.isDiscordLoading}
          emailOrUser={state.emailOrUser}
          password={state.password}
          inviteCode={state.inviteCode}
          isInviteLocked={state.isInviteLocked}
          onEmailChange={actions.setEmailOrUser}
          onPasswordChange={actions.setPassword}
          onInviteChange={actions.setInviteCode}
          onSubmit={actions.handleSubmit}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Auth/ChangePasswordModal.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { Button } from "@/src/system/atoms/Button";
import { IconKey, IconX } from "@tabler/icons-react";

interface ChangePasswordModalProps {
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function ChangePasswordModal({ onClose }: ChangePasswordModalProps) {
  const [pwdData, setPwdData] = useState({ old_password: "", new_password: "" });
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      await api.cambiarPassword(pwdData);
      alert("✅ Contraseña actualizada con éxito");
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert(`❌ Error: ${err.message}`);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 z-100 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        className="bg-(--bg-secondary) p-8 neo-border neo-shadow flex flex-col gap-5 max-w-sm w-full animate-fade-in relative"
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-(--text-secondary) hover:text-red-500 transition-colors"
        >
          <IconX size={24} />
        </button>

        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold font-mono text-foreground uppercase flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 border-(--border-color) pb-3">
          <IconKey size={24} className="text-(--accent-color)" />
          Cambiar Clave
        </h3>

        <input
          type="password"
          placeholder="Clave Actual"
          required
          className="neo-border p-3 bg-background text-foreground font-mono focus:outline-none focus:neo-shadow-accent transition-all"
          value={pwdData.old_password}
          onChange={(e) => setPwdData({ ...pwdData, old_password: e.target.value })}
        />
        <input
          type="password"
          placeholder="Nueva Clave"
          required
          className="neo-border p-3 bg-background text-foreground font-mono focus:outline-none focus:neo-shadow-accent transition-all"
          value={pwdData.new_password}
          onChange={(e) => setPwdData({ ...pwdData, new_password: e.target.value })}
        />

        <Button type="submit" variant="primary" isLoading={isLoading} className="mt-2 w-full">
          Guardar Cambios
        </Button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRegister } from "@/src/features/auth/hooks/useRegister";

import { FormField } from "@/src/system/molecules/FormField";
import { Button } from "@/src/system/atoms/Button";
import { FormHeader } from "../../atoms/Login/FormHeader";

interface RegisterFormProps {
  lang: string;
}

export function RegisterForm({ lang }: RegisterFormProps) {
  // 🟢 Lógica inyectada sin alterar
  const { handleRegister, isLoading, error } = useRegister(lang);

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    username: "",
    password: "",
    invite_code: "",
  });

  const onSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    await handleRegister(formData);
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto p-8 bg-(--bg-secondary) neo-border neo-shadow">
      <div className="animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-300">
        
        {/* Cabecera idéntica al Login */}
        <FormHeader />

        {error && (
          <div className="bg-red-500 text-white p-3 mb-6 font-bold neo-border text-xs font-mono uppercase text-center animate-in slide-in-from-top-2">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-6">
          
          <FormField
            label="email"
            name="username"
            type="text"
            value={formData.username}
            onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, username: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="reprompt@mail.com"
            autoComplete="username"
            required
          />

          <FormField
            label="Password"
            name="password"
            type="password"
            value={formData.password}
            onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, password: e.target.value })}
            autoComplete="new-password"
            placeholder="••••••••"
            required
          />

          <FormField
            label="Código de invitación"
            name="invite_code"
            type="text"
            value={formData.invite_code}
            onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, invite_code: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })}
            placeholder="ej. TROYA-A1234B56"
            className="uppercase"
            required
          />

          <Button
            type="submit"
            isLoading={isLoading}
            className="w-full justify-center h-12 text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest"
          >
            {isLoading ? "Verificando..." : "Confirmar Acceso"}
          </Button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Extractor/Dropzone/index.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useCallback } from "react";
import { useDropzone } from "react-dropzone";
import { DropzoneLoading } from "./DropzoneLoading";
import { DropzoneContent } from "./DropzoneContent";
import { DropzoneError } from "./DropzoneError";

interface ExtractorDropzoneProps {
  onFileDrop: (acceptedFiles: File[], rejectedFiles: any[]) => void;
  isExtracting: boolean;
  error: string | null;
}

export function ExtractorDropzone({
  onFileDrop,
  isExtracting,
  error: externalError,
}: ExtractorDropzoneProps) {
  const onDrop = useCallback(
    (acceptedFiles: File[], fileRejections: any[]) => {
      if (acceptedFiles.length > 0 || fileRejections.length > 0) {
        onFileDrop(acceptedFiles, fileRejections);
      }
    },
    [onFileDrop],
  );

  // 🟢 Extraemos isDragReject
  const { getRootProps, getInputProps, isDragActive, isDragReject } = useDropzone({
    onDrop,
    accept: {
      "image/png": [".png"],
      "image/jpeg": [".jpg", ".jpeg"],
      "image/webp": [".webp"], // Sugiero añadir webp
      "text/plain": [".txt"],
      "application/json": [".json"],
      "application/zip": [".zip"],
      "application/x-zip-compressed": [".zip"],
    },
    disabled: isExtracting,
  });

  if (isExtracting) return <DropzoneLoading />;

  return (
    <div
      {...getRootProps()}
      className={`group relative w-full border-4 border-dashed transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-400px cursor-pointer neo-border overflow-hidden
        ${
          isDragReject
            ? "border-destructive bg-destructive/5"
            : isDragActive
              ? "border-(--accent-color) bg-(--accent-color)/5 scale-[1.01] neo-shadow-accent"
              : "border-border bg-muted/5 hover:bg-muted/10 hover:border-foreground/30"
        }`}
    >
      <input {...getInputProps()} />
      <DropzoneContent isDragActive={isDragActive} isDragReject={isDragReject} />

      {externalError && <DropzoneError message={externalError} />}
    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/system/organisms/Extractor/Inspector/BatchInspector.tsx
================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { BatchItem, SkippedFile } from "@/src/features/extractor/hooks/usePromptExtractor";
import { ExtractedPromptData } from "@/src/features/extractor/utils/promptParser";
import { Button } from "@/src/system/atoms/Button";
import { IconTrash, IconDeviceFloppy, IconAlertTriangle, IconX } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { api } from "@/src/services/api";
import { handleError } from "@/src/utils/errors/ErrorHandler";
import { useNotificationStore } from "@/src/store/notificationStore";
import { fetchSeguro } from "@/src/services/api/client";

interface Props {
  batch: BatchItem[];
  skippedFiles: SkippedFile[];
  onUpdateStatus: (id: string, status: BatchItem["status"]) => void;
  onUpdateItemData: (id: string, newData: Partial<ExtractedPromptData>) => void;
  onRemoveItem: (id: string) => void;
  onClearAll: () => void;
  onClearSkipped: () => void;
}

export function BatchInspector({
  batch,
  skippedFiles,
  onUpdateStatus,
  onUpdateItemData,
  onRemoveItem,
  onClearAll,
  onClearSkipped,
}: Props) {
  const [isGlobalLoading, setIsGlobalLoading] = useState(false);
  const notify = useNotificationStore((state) => state.notify);

  const handleSaveItem = async (item: BatchItem) => {
    if (item.status === "success") return;
    onUpdateStatus(item.id, "saving");

    try {
      let finalImageUrl = null;

      if (item.previewUrl && item.previewUrl.startsWith("blob:")) {
        const response = await fetch(item.previewUrl);
        const blob = await response.blob();

        const API_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "http://localhost:8000";
        const ticketRes = await fetchSeguro(`${API_URL}/prompts/generate-upload-url`, {
          method: "POST",
          credentials: "include",
          body: JSON.stringify({
            file_name: item.file.name,
            content_type: blob.type,
          }),
        });

        if (!ticketRes.ok) throw new Error("No se pudo obtener el ticket de subida de R2");

        // ✅ MINA 3 DESACTIVADA: Parseamos el JSON plano
        const ticketData = await ticketRes.json();

        const uploadUrl = ticketData.upload_url;
        const contentType = blob.type;
        const fileSize = blob.size;

        // --- 🕵️‍♂️ LOGS DE DIAGNÓSTICO QUIRÚRGICO ANTES DEL FETCH ---
        const fetchOptions = {
          method: "PUT",
          headers: { "Content-Type": contentType },
          body: blob,
        };

        console.log("🔴 === PRE-FLIGHT CHECK (R2 UPLOAD) ===");
        console.log("👉 Archivo:", item.file.name);
        console.log("👉 Origen del Navegador:", window.location.origin);
        console.log("👉 URL Destino (Ticket):", uploadUrl);
        console.log("👉 Objeto Blob - Type:", contentType, "| Size:", fileSize, "bytes");
        console.log("👉 Headers exactos a enviar:", fetchOptions.headers);
        console.log("🔴 ===================================");

        // Subida directa a Cloudflare
        const uploadRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, fetchOptions);

        // --- 🕵️‍♂️ LOGS DE RESPUESTA DE CLOUDFLARE ---
        console.log("🟢 === RESPUESTA DE R2 ===");
        console.log("Status:", uploadRes.status, uploadRes.statusText);
        console.log("Headers recibidos:", Object.fromEntries(uploadRes.headers.entries()));

        if (!uploadRes.ok) {
          const errorText = await uploadRes.text();
          console.error("❌ ERROR RAW DE R2 (XML):", errorText);
          throw new Error("Fallo la subida física a Cloudflare R2");
        }
        console.log("🟢 =======================");

        // ✅ MINA 4 DESACTIVADA: Ahora el backend sí nos entrega file_url
        finalImageUrl = ticketData.file_url;
      }

      const payload = {
        title: `Gen - ${item.data.software || "IA"}`,
        prompt: item.data.prompt,
        negative_prompt: item.data.negativePrompt,
        software: item.data.software,
        model: item.data.model,
        sampler: item.data.sampler,
        steps: item.data.steps,
        cfg_scale: item.data.cfgScale,
        seed: item.data.seed,
        resolution: item.data.resolution,
        image_url: finalImageUrl,
        extra_data: {
          loras: item.data.loras || [],
          triggerWords: item.data.triggerWords || [],
        },
      };

      console.log("👉 Payload a guardar en DB:", payload);

      await api.savePrompt(payload);
      onUpdateStatus(item.id, "success");

      notify("Studio", "Prompt guardado correctamente", "success");

      setTimeout(() => {
        onRemoveItem(item.id);
      }, 1000);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      handleError(err, "SaveBatchItem");
      onUpdateStatus(item.id, "error");
    }
  };

  const handleSaveAll = async () => {
    setIsGlobalLoading(true);
    const pendingItems = batch.filter((i) => i.status !== "success");
    await Promise.allSettled(pendingItems.map((item) => handleSaveItem(item)));
    setIsGlobalLoading(false);
  };

  // ... EL RESTO DEL CÓDIGO (RETURN, HTML, JSX) SIGUE EXACTAMENTE IGUAL ...
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      {/* FEEDBACK DE ARCHIVOS DESCARTADOS INTACTO AQUÍ */}
      {skippedFiles.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-orange-500/10 border-2 border-orange-500 p-4 neo-border relative">
          <button
            onClick={onClearSkipped}
            className="absolute top-2 right-2 text-orange-700 hover:text-orange-900"
          >
            <IconX size={20} />
          </button>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-orange-600 font-black font-mono uppercase mb-2">
            <IconAlertTriangle size={20} /> Se descartaron {skippedFiles.length} archivos
          </div>
          <div className="max-h-32 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
            <ul className="text-xs font-mono text-orange-800 space-y-1">
              {skippedFiles.map((f, i) => (
                <li key={i} className="flex gap-2">
                  <span className="font-bold truncate w-1/3" title={f.name}>
                    {f.name}
                  </span>
                  <span className="opacity-70 truncate w-2/3">{f.reason}</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 🎛️ CONTROLES GLOBALES */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-center bg-(--bg-secondary) p-4 neo-border gap-4">
        <div className="font-mono text-sm font-bold uppercase">
          <span className="text-(--accent-color)">{batch.length}</span> listos para studio
        </div>
        <div className="flex w-full sm:w-auto gap-3">
          <Button
            onClick={onClearAll}
            variant="ghost"
            disabled={isGlobalLoading}
            className="text-red-500 hover:bg-red-500/10"
          >
            Descartar Todos
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={handleSaveAll} isLoading={isGlobalLoading} className="w-full sm:w-auto">
            <IconDeviceFloppy size={18} className="mr-2" /> Guardar Todo
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 🖼️ GRID DE BATCH ITEMS */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
        {batch.map((item) => {
          const isManual = item.data.software === "Desconocido";
          return (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className={`neo-border bg-(--bg-secondary) flex flex-col h-420px relative group overflow-hidden transition-all ${
                item.status === "success" ? "opacity-50 grayscale" : "hover:neo-shadow-accent"
              }`}
            >
              {/* Imagen */}
              <div className="relative h-48 shrink-0 border-b-2 border-(--border-color) bg-black">
                {item.previewUrl ? (
                  <img
                    src={item.previewUrl}
                    alt={item.file.name}
                    className="w-full h-full object-cover opacity-90"
                  />
                ) : (
                  <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground">
                    Sin visual
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="absolute top-2 left-2 flex flex-col gap-1">
                  <span
                    className={`bg-black/80 ${isManual ? "text-yellow-400" : "text-white"} font-mono text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 border border-(--border-color)`}
                  >
                    {item.data.software}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* 🟢 Contenido: Lógica Híbrida (Formulario Manual o Texto Fijo) */}
              <div className="p-4 bg-background grow flex flex-col gap-2 overflow-hidden">
                {isManual ? (
                  <div className="flex flex-col h-full gap-2">
                    <textarea
                      placeholder="Escribe el prompt aquí..."
                      className="w-full grow text-xs font-mono p-2 neo-border bg-muted/10 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-(--accent-color) resize-none custom-scrollbar"
                      value={item.data.prompt}
                      onChange={(e) => onUpdateItemData(item.id, { prompt: e.target.value })}
                    />
                    <div className="flex gap-2 shrink-0">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Modelo (Ej. Midjourney v6)"
                        className="w-1/2 text-xs font-mono p-2 neo-border bg-muted/10 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-(--accent-color)"
                        value={item.data.model || ""}
                        onChange={(e) => onUpdateItemData(item.id, { model: e.target.value })}
                      />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Sampler (Ej. Euler a)"
                        className="w-1/2 text-xs font-mono p-2 neo-border bg-muted/10 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-(--accent-color)"
                        value={item.data.sampler || ""}
                        onChange={(e) => onUpdateItemData(item.id, { sampler: e.target.value })}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="h-full overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar pr-2">
                    <p className="font-mono text-xs text-(--text-secondary) leading-relaxed">
                      {item.data.prompt || (
                        <span className="italic opacity-50 text-red-500">
                          No se detectó un prompt en la metadata.
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Barra de Acciones */}
              <div className="border-t-2 shrink-0 border-(--border-color) flex justify-between bg-muted/20">
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleSaveItem(item)}
                  disabled={item.status === "saving" || item.status === "success"}
                  className={`flex-1 p-3 font-bold font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider transition-colors ${
                    item.status === "success"
                      ? "text-green-500"
                      : item.status === "error"
                        ? "text-red-500 hover:bg-red-500/10"
                        : "text-foreground hover:bg-(--accent-color) hover:text-white"
                  }`}
                >
                  {item.status === "saving"
                    ? "Guardando..."
                    : item.status === "success"
                      ? "¡Guardado!"
                      : item.status === "error"
                        ? "Reintentar"
                        : "Guardar en Mi Studio"}
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => onRemoveItem(item.id)}
                  disabled={item.status === "saving"}
                  className="p-3 bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white transition-colors border-l-2 border-(--border-color)"
                >
                  <IconTrash size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Layout/GlobalProgress.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { usePathname, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useProgressStore } from "@/src/store/progressStore";

export function GlobalProgress() {
  const { isAnimating, progress, start, done } = useProgressStore();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  // 1. Cerrar barra al terminar la navegación de Next.js
  useEffect(() => {
    done();
  }, [pathname, searchParams, done]);

  // 2. Interceptor de enlaces nativos
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleAnchorClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const anchor = (e.target as HTMLElement).closest("a");
      if (!anchor) return;

      const href = anchor.getAttribute("href");
      const target = anchor.getAttribute("target");

      if (href && href.startsWith("/") && target !== "_blank" && href !== pathname) {
        start();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("click", handleAnchorClick, true);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("click", handleAnchorClick, true);
  }, [pathname, start]);

  // 🛡️ FIX ARQUITECTÓNICO: NUNCA DEVOLVEMOS NULL.
  // Dejamos el elemento en el DOM para que el motor CSS pueda calcular las transiciones de salida (Fade out).

  return (
    <div className="fixed top-0 left-0 w-full z-99999 pointer-events-none">
      <div
        className="relative transition-all ease-out"
        style={{
          height: "3px", // El grosor elegante de la industria
          width: `${progress}%`,
          opacity: isAnimating ? 1 : 0,
          // 🛡️ FALLBACK: Si var(--accent-color) no existe, usará un Azul Neón brillante (#0ea5e9)
          background: "var(--accent-color, #0ea5e9)",
          boxShadow: "0 0 10px var(--accent-color, #0ea5e9), 0 0 5px var(--accent-color, #0ea5e9)",
          // Si está desapareciendo, que tarde 500ms en desvanecerse (más suave)
          transitionDuration: isAnimating ? "300ms" : "500ms",
        }}
      >
        {/* LA PUNTA DE LUZ (Puro CSS, sin imágenes ni SVGs) */}
        <div
          className="absolute top-0 right-0 h-full w-70px bg-white shadow-[0_0_15px_white] rounded-full"
          style={{
            filter: "blur(2px)",
            opacity: isAnimating ? 0.8 : 0,
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Media/UniversalMediaCard.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import React from "react";
import { MediaCardData, MediaVariant } from "@/src/types/models";
import { MediaCover } from "@/src/system/atoms/MediaCover";
import { MediaOverlay } from "@/src/system/molecules/MediaOverlay";
import { cn } from "@/src/utils/lib/cn";

interface UniversalMediaCardProps {
  data: MediaCardData;
  variant?: MediaVariant;
  className?: string;
  onClick?: () => void;
  onDownload?: () => void;
}

export function UniversalMediaCard({
  data,
  variant = "gallery",
  className,
  onClick,
  onDownload,
}: UniversalMediaCardProps) {
  if (!data) return null;

  const handleDownload = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    onDownload?.();
  };

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "group relative w-full overflow-hidden rounded-xl cursor-pointer border border-border/10 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-300",
        variant === "viewer" ? "bg-black/20" : "bg-muted/20",
        className,
      )}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      <MediaCover src={data.imageUrl} alt={data.title} />

      {data.imageUrl && (
        <MediaOverlay
          title={data.title}
          prompt={data.prompt}
          tags={data.tags}
          variant={variant}
          onDownload={onDownload ? handleDownload : undefined}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Modal/GlobalModal.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useGlobalModal } from "@/src/store/useGlobalModal";
import { IconAlertTriangle, IconInfoCircle, IconX } from "@tabler/icons-react";

export function GlobalModal() {
  const { isOpen, config, closeModal } = useGlobalModal();

  if (!isOpen || !config) return null;

  const isDanger = config.type === "danger" || config.type === "warning";
  const Icon = isDanger ? IconAlertTriangle : IconInfoCircle;
  const iconColor = isDanger
    ? "text-red-500 bg-red-500/10"
    : "text-(--accent-color) bg-(--accent-color)/10";
  const confirmBtnStyle = isDanger
    ? "bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 text-white"
    : "bg-(--accent-color) hover:bg-(--accent-color)/80 text-foreground";

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-9999 flex items-center justify-center bg-background/80 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="bg-muted/30 border border-border/50 shadow-2xl rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md p-6 relative animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
        <button
          onClick={closeModal}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition-colors"
        >
          <IconX size={20} />
        </button>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className={`p-2 rounded-full ${iconColor}`}>
              <Icon size={24} stroke={1.5} />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold tracking-tight">{config.title}</h2>
          </div>

          <div className="text-muted-foreground text-sm leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
            {config.message}
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-border/30">
            {config.onConfirm && (
              <button
                onClick={closeModal}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium hover:bg-muted transition-colors"
              >
                {config.cancelText || "Cancelar"}
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              onClick={() => {
                if (config.onConfirm) config.onConfirm();
                closeModal();
              }}
              className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold transition-all ${confirmBtnStyle}`}
            >
              {config.confirmText || "Entendido"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Navigation/Breadcrumbs.tsx
================================================
import Link from "next/link";
import { IconChevronRight, IconHome } from "@tabler/icons-react";

interface BreadcrumbItem {
  label: string;
  href?: string;
}

interface BreadcrumbsProps {
  items: BreadcrumbItem[];
  homeHref?: string;
}

export function Breadcrumbs({ items, homeHref = "/" }: BreadcrumbsProps) {
  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Breadcrumb"
      className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-xs md:text-sm font-mono text-muted-foreground overflow-x-auto pb-2 scrollbar-none"
    >
      <Link href={homeHref} className="hover:text-accent-foreground transition-colors flex items-center">
        <IconHome size={16} stroke={1.5} />
      </Link>

      {items.map((item, index) => {
        const isLast = index === items.length - 1;

        return (
          <div key={index} className="flex items-center space-x-1 whitespace-nowrap">
            <IconChevronRight size={14} className="opacity-50" stroke={1.5} />
            {isLast || !item.href ? (
              <span
                className="font-bold text-foreground tracking-widest uppercase truncate max-w-150px sm:max-w-300px"
                title={item.label}
              >
                {item.label}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <Link
                href={item.href}
                className="hover:text-(--accent-color) transition-colors tracking-widest uppercase truncate max-w-150px sm:max-w-200px"
                title={item.label}
              >
                {item.label}
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}
