FILE: src/system/organisms/Navigation/NavbarSearch.tsx
================================================
// src/system/organisms/Navigation/NavbarSearch.tsx
"use client";

import { UniversalSearchBar } from "../../molecules/Search/UniversalSearchBar";
import { useVectorSearch } from "@/src/features/search/useVectorSearch";
import { useSearchStore } from "@/src/store/searchStore"; // Para sincronizar con el feed

export function NavbarSearch() {
  const globalSearchStore = useSearchStore(); // Conectamos con el estado global de la página

  const vector = useVectorSearch({ scope: "global" });

  const onSearchSubmit = async () => {
    await vector.handleExecute();
    // Actualizamos el store global para que el feed principal cambie
    globalSearchStore.setResults(vector.results);
    globalSearchStore.setQuery(vector.query);
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-xl">
      <UniversalSearchBar
        query={vector.query}
        isLoading={vector.isLoading}
        placeholder="Búsqueda semántica de prompts..."
        enableKeyboardShortcut={true}
        onQueryChange={vector.setQuery}
        onSubmit={onSearchSubmit}
        onClear={vector.clear}
      />
    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/system/organisms/Navigation/PageHeader.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Breadcrumbs } from "@/src/system/organisms/Navigation/Breadcrumbs";

export function PageHeader({ title, subtitle, description, icon, breadcrumbItems }: any) {
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(true);
  const [lastScrollY, setLastScrollY] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => {
      const currentScrollY = window.scrollY;
      if (currentScrollY > lastScrollY && currentScrollY > 150) {
        setIsVisible(false);
      } else {
        setIsVisible(true);
      }
      setLastScrollY(currentScrollY);
    };

    window.addEventListener("scroll", handleScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", handleScroll);
  }, [lastScrollY]);

  return (
    <header
      className={`
        hidden md:block 
        sticky top-0 z-40 w-full transition-all duration-500 ease-in-out
        ${isVisible ? "translate-y-0 opacity-100" : "-translate-y-full opacity-0"}
        bg-background/80 backdrop-blur-xl px-4 md:px-0 py-6
      `}
    >
      <div className="w-full mx-auto space-y-4">
        <Breadcrumbs items={breadcrumbItems} />
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-4">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl text-foreground font-black tracking-tighter flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-(--accent-color)">{icon}</span>
              {title} {subtitle && <span className="font-mono font-medium opacity-70">{subtitle}</span>}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-xs tracking-widest">
              {description}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Search/SearchResults/SearchResults.tsx
================================================
"use client";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useInView } from "react-intersection-observer";
import { IconLoader2, IconRefresh } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { UniversalMediaCard } from "@/src/system/organisms/Media/UniversalMediaCard";
import { mapSearchToMediaCard } from "@/src/utils/adapters/mediaAdapters";
import { GalleryItem } from "@/src/types/models";

interface SearchResultsProps {
  results: GalleryItem[];
  onOpenVisor: (item: GalleryItem) => void;
  onDownloadTotal: (item: GalleryItem) => void;
  hasMore: boolean;
  isLoadingMore: boolean;
  onLoadMore: () => void;
}

export function SearchResults({
  results,
  onOpenVisor,
  onDownloadTotal,
  hasMore,
  isLoadingMore,
  onLoadMore,
}: SearchResultsProps) {
  // Margen de 400px para cargar con antelación y que el usuario ni note la carga
  const { ref, inView } = useInView({ rootMargin: "400px" });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (inView && hasMore && !isLoadingMore) {
      onLoadMore();
    }
  }, [inView, hasMore, isLoadingMore, onLoadMore]);

  if (!results || results.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section className="w-full py-8 animate-in fade-in duration-700">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-foreground/80">
          Resultados de la <span className="text-var(--accent-color)">Búsqueda</span>
        </h2>
        <span className="text-sm font-mono text-muted-foreground bg-muted/30 px-3 py-1 neo-border shadow-sm">
          {results.length} recuperados
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-4 min-h-[50vh]">
        {results.map((item, index) => {
          const stableKey = item.gid ? `search-${item.gid}` : `search-idx-${index}`;
          return (
            <UniversalMediaCard
              key={stableKey}
              data={mapSearchToMediaCard(item)}
              variant="search"
              onClick={() => onOpenVisor(item)}
              onDownload={() => onDownloadTotal(item)}
            />
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* 🛡️ CENTINELA HÍBRIDO (Auto Scroll + Fallback Manual + End of Line) */}
      <div className="mt-16 flex justify-center items-center h-24">
        {hasMore ? (
          <div ref={ref} className="w-full flex justify-center">
            {isLoadingMore ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 text-var(--accent-color) animate-pulse">
                <IconLoader2 className="animate-spin" size={32} />
                <span className="font-mono text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
                  Decodificando Red...
                </span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <button
                onClick={onLoadMore}
                className="group flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-muted/20 border-2 border-border text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:border-foreground hover:bg-muted/50 neo-border font-mono text-xs font-bold uppercase transition-all"
              >
                <IconRefresh
                  size={16}
                  className="group-hover:rotate-180 transition-transform duration-500"
                />
                Forzar Carga
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="text-center font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest bg-muted/10 px-6 py-3 neo-border border-dashed">
            Has llegado al final del abismo
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Studio/PromptCard.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { IconCopy, IconCheck, IconPhoto } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { usePromptCard } from "@/src/system/molecules/Cards/hooks/usePromptCard";
import { PromptCardActions } from "../../molecules/Cards/PromptCardActions";
import { LazyImage } from "../../molecules/Media/LazyImage";

export function PromptCard({
  item,
  directories,
  onAssign,
  onDelete,
  lang,
  authorUsername,
  isStudio,
  index,
}: any) {
  const { setIsHovered, isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen, copied, handleCopy } = usePromptCard();
  const href = `/${lang}/${authorUsername}/p/${item.id}${isStudio ? "?from=studio" : ""}`;

  return (
    <div
      className="group relative flex flex-col bg-background rounded-xl border border-border shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg transition-all"
      onMouseEnter={() => setIsHovered(true)}
      onMouseLeave={() => {
        setIsHovered(false);
        setIsMenuOpen(false);
      }}
    >
      <PromptCardActions
        item={item}
        directories={directories}
        onAssign={onAssign}
        onDelete={onDelete}
        isOpen={isMenuOpen}
        setIsOpen={setIsMenuOpen}
      />

      <Link href={href} className="flex flex-col h-full z-10">
        <div className="relative aspect-square sm:aspect-4/5 overflow-hidden rounded-t-xl bg-muted/10">
          {item.image_url ? (
            <LazyImage
              src={item.image_url}
              alt={item.title}
              index={index}
              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground/30">
              <IconPhoto size={40} stroke={1} />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-sm truncate">{item.title}</h3>
          <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground uppercase font-mono">
            {item.model || "Base Model"}
          </p>
        </div>
      </Link>

      <div className="px-4 pb-4 mt-auto">
        <div className="relative group/prompt bg-muted/30 rounded-lg p-2 border border-border/50">
          <p className="text-[11px] font-mono text-foreground/70 line-clamp-2">{item.prompt}</p>
          <button
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              e.stopPropagation();
              handleCopy(item.prompt);
            }}
            className="absolute top-1 right-1 p-1 bg-background border rounded-md opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"
          >
            {copied ? <IconCheck size={12} className="text-green-500" /> : <IconCopy size={12} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { IconPhotoOff } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { PageLoader } from "../../atoms/PageLoader";
import { PromptCard } from "./PromptCard";
import { useStudioPrompts } from "@/src/features/studio/hooks/useStudioPrompts";

export default function PromptGrid() {
  const params = useParams();
  const lang = (params?.lang as string) || "es";

  const { prompts, isLoading, user, deletePrompt } = useStudioPrompts();

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="h-full flex flex-col flex-1 items-center justify-center">
        <PageLoader text="Sincronizando Studio..." />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (prompts.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="h-full flex flex-col flex-1 items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground/40">
        <IconPhotoOff size={48} stroke={1.5} className="mb-4" />
        <p className="text-sm font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-center px-4">
          No se encontraron prompts en este rincón
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="h-full flex flex-col">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-6">
        {prompts.map((p: any, index: number) => (
          <PromptCard
            key={p.id}
            item={p}
            index={index}
            lang={lang}
            isStudio={true}
            authorUsername={user?.username || "unknown"}
            isPriority={index < 8}
            onDelete={deletePrompt}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Studio/SidebarDirectory.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useStudioUI, Directory } from "@/src/store/useStudioUI";
import { SidebarDirectoryItem } from "../../molecules/Studio/SidebarDirectoryItem";
import { useDirectoryDnd } from "@/src/features/studio/hooks/useDirectoryDnd";
import { useDirectoryActions } from "@/src/features/studio/hooks/useDirectoryActions";

interface Props {
  directory: Directory;
  level?: number;
}

export function SidebarDirectory({ directory, level = 0 }: Props) {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const { getChildDirectories, activeDirectoryId, setActiveDirectory } = useStudioUI();

  const { createDirectory, deleteDirectory, renameDirectory } = useDirectoryActions();

  const { setBothRefs, isDragging, isOver, attributes, listeners } = useDirectoryDnd(directory);

  const children = getChildDirectories(directory.id);
  const hasChildren = children.length > 0;
  const isSelected = activeDirectoryId === directory.id;

  const handleCreateSubdirectory = async (onSuccess: () => void) => {
    const name = window.prompt("Nombre de la sub-carpeta:");
    if (name?.trim()) {
      await createDirectory(name.trim(), directory.id);
      onSuccess();
    }
  };

  if (isDragging) return null;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col w-full" ref={setBothRefs}>
      <SidebarDirectoryItem
        directory={directory}
        level={level}
        isOpen={isOpen}
        hasChildren={hasChildren}
        isSelected={isSelected}
        isOver={isOver}
        isDragging={isDragging}
        setBothRefs={setBothRefs}
        attributes={attributes}
        listeners={listeners}
        isEffectivelyPrivate={directory.is_private || false}
        onToggle={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        onSelect={() => setActiveDirectory(directory.id)}
        onCreateSub={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          handleCreateSubdirectory(() => setIsOpen(true));
        }}
        onDelete={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          if (window.confirm(`¿Borrar "${directory.name}"?`)) {
            deleteDirectory(directory.id);
          }
        }}
        onRename={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          const newName = window.prompt("Nuevo nombre:", directory.name);
          if (newName && newName.trim() !== directory.name) {
            renameDirectory(directory.id, newName.trim());
          }
        }}
      />

      {isOpen && hasChildren && (
        <div className="flex flex-col w-full">
          {children.map((child: Directory) => (
            <SidebarDirectory key={child.id} directory={child} level={level + 1} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { UniversalSearchBar } from "../../molecules/Search/UniversalSearchBar";
import { useVectorSearch } from "@/src/features/search/useVectorSearch";
import { useStudioUI } from "@/src/store/useStudioUI";

export function StudioSearch() {
  const { activeDirectoryId } = useStudioUI();

  const vector = useVectorSearch({
    scope: "studio",
    directoryId: activeDirectoryId ?? undefined,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (vector.query) vector.handleExecute();
    }, 500);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [vector.query]);

  return (
    <div className="mb-4">
      <UniversalSearchBar
        query={vector.query}
        isLoading={vector.isLoading}
        placeholder="Busca en tus extracciones..."
        onQueryChange={vector.setQuery}
        onSubmit={vector.handleExecute}
        onClear={vector.clear}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { IconFolderPlus, IconCloudUpload, IconFolder } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { useStudioUI, Directory } from "@/src/store/useStudioUI";
import { SidebarDirectory } from "@/src/system/organisms/Studio/SidebarDirectory";
import { useFileUploader } from "@/src/features/extractor/hooks/useFileUploader";

import { useDirectoryActions } from "@/src/features/studio/hooks/useDirectoryActions";

export function StudioSidebar() {
  const { directories, activeDirectoryId, setActiveDirectory } = useStudioUI();

  const { createDirectory } = useDirectoryActions();

  const { handleUploadClick } = useFileUploader();

  const handleCreateRootFolder = async (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const name = window.prompt("Nombre de la nueva carpeta principal:");
    if (name?.trim()) await createDirectory(name.trim(), null);
  };

  return (
    <aside className="w-72 border-r border-border/30 bg-muted/5 flex-col hidden md:flex">
      <div className="pt-6 flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar flex flex-col gap-1">
        <div className="px-3 mb-1 group relative">
          <div
            onClick={() => setActiveDirectory(null)}
            className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs transition-all border cursor-pointer role="button" ${
              activeDirectoryId === null
                ? "bg-primary/10 text-primary border-primary/20 font-bold"
                : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-secondary/40 border-transparent font-medium"
            }`}
          >
            <IconFolder size={18} stroke={1.5} />
            <span className="tracking-widest flex-1 text-left">
              <span className="font-mono font-medium lowercase text-foreground/70">reprompt</span>{" "}
              root
            </span>

            <div className="flex gap-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
              <button
                onClick={handleCreateRootFolder}
                className="p-1 hover:bg-primary/20 rounded text-primary/80 transition-colors"
                title="Nueva carpeta en Root"
              >
                <IconFolderPlus size={14} stroke={2} />
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleUploadClick}
                className="p-1 hover:bg-primary/20 rounded text-primary/80 transition-colors"
                title="Subir a Root"
              >
                <IconCloudUpload size={14} stroke={2} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mx-6 border-t border-border/10 my-2" />

        <div className="px-3 flex flex-col gap-1">
          {directories
            .filter((d: Directory) => d.parent_id === null)
            .map((dir: Directory) => (
              <SidebarDirectory key={dir.id} directory={dir} />
            ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/organisms/Viewer/ImageViewerModal/ImageViewerModal.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from "react";
import { IconX, IconLoader2 } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { UniversalMediaCard } from "@/src/system/organisms/Media/UniversalMediaCard";
import { mapViewerToMediaCard } from "@/src/utils/adapters/mediaAdapters";
import { PageLoader } from "@/src/system/atoms/PageLoader";

interface ViewerPage {
  image_url: string;
  name: string;
  prompt?: string;
}

interface ImageViewerModalProps {
  title: string;
  pages: ViewerPage[];
  isLoading: boolean;
  isLoadingMore: boolean;
  hasMorePages: boolean;
  onLoadMore: () => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function ImageViewerModal({
  title,
  pages,
  isLoading,
  isLoadingMore,
  hasMorePages,
  onLoadMore,
  onClose,
}: ImageViewerModalProps) {
  const observerRef = useRef<IntersectionObserver | null>(null);

  const lastElementRef = useCallback(
    (node: HTMLDivElement | null) => {
      if (isLoading || isLoadingMore) return;
      if (observerRef.current) observerRef.current.disconnect();

      observerRef.current = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        if (entries[0].isIntersecting && hasMorePages) onLoadMore();
      });

      if (node) observerRef.current.observe(node);
    },
    [isLoading, isLoadingMore, hasMorePages, onLoadMore],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleEsc);
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleEsc);
      document.body.style.overflow = "unset";
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-100 flex flex-col bg-background/95 backdrop-blur-xl animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="flex-none h-16 px-4 md:px-8 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border/50 bg-background/50">
        <h2 className="text-lg md:text-xl font-bold line-clamp-1">{title}</h2>
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition-colors"
        >
          <IconX size={24} />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar p-4 md:p-8">
        {isLoading && pages.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 text-muted-foreground">
            <PageLoader text="Cargando Imagen ..."/>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-12">
            {pages.map((page, index) => (
              <div
                key={`${index}-${page.image_url}`}
                ref={index === pages.length - 1 ? lastElementRef : null}
                className="w-full flex justify-center"
              >
                <div className="w-full max-w-4xl">
                  <UniversalMediaCard data={mapViewerToMediaCard(page, index)} variant="viewer" />
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
            {isLoadingMore && (
              <div className="py-8 flex justify-center">
                <IconLoader2 size={32} className="animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/system/pages/Feed/PublicFeedGrid.tsx
================================================
// frontend/src/system/pages/Feed/PublicFeedGrid.tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { FeedItemCard } from "@/src/system/molecules/Cards/FeedItemCard";
import { FeedState } from "@/src/system/molecules/Feedback/FeedState";
import { FeedItem } from "@/src/types/models";
import { FadeIn } from "../../atoms/FadeIn";

interface PublicFeedGridProps {
  feed: FeedItem[];
  isLoading: boolean;
  lang: string;
  hasMore: boolean;
  onLoadMore: () => void;
}

export function PublicFeedGrid({
  feed,
  isLoading,
  lang,
  hasMore,
  onLoadMore,
}: PublicFeedGridProps) {
  const sentinelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (entries[0].isIntersecting && hasMore && !isLoading) {
          onLoadMore();
        }
      },
      { threshold: 0.5 },
    );

    if (sentinelRef.current) observer.observe(sentinelRef.current);

    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [hasMore, isLoading, onLoadMore]);

  return (
    <div className="pt-8">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-black font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-(--accent-color) mb-6">
        Explora la Comunidad
      </h2>

      <div className="mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-6">
        {feed.map((item, index) => (
          <FadeIn key={item.id} delay={0.1} y={20}>
            <FeedItemCard item={item} lang={lang} index={index} />
          </FadeIn>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div ref={sentinelRef} className="h-20 flex items-center justify-center mt-10">
        {isLoading && <FeedState type="loading" />}
        {!hasMore && feed.length > 0 && (
          <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-xs uppercase">
            — Fin de la transmisión —
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { MediaCardData, GalleryItem } from "@/src/types/models";

export const mapSearchToMediaCard = (item: GalleryItem): MediaCardData => {
  // 🚨 SENSOR DE TRANSFORMACIÓN
  if (!item && process.env.NODE_ENV === "development")
    console.error("🚨 TELEMETRÍA [ADAPTER]: Item recibido es NULO o UNDEFINED");
  if (item && !item.thumb && process.env.NODE_ENV === "development")
    console.warn("🚨 TELEMETRÍA [ADAPTER]: Item no tiene imagen (thumb)", item);

  return {
    id: item?.gid || item?.url || crypto.randomUUID(),
    user_id: item.user_id,
    imageUrl: item?.thumb || "",
    title: item?.title || "Sin título",
    tags: item?.tags || [],
    source: "E-Hentai",
    aspectRatio: "portrait",
  };
};

export const mapViewerToMediaCard = (page: any, index: number): MediaCardData => {
  /* ... */ return {
    user_id: "",
    id: `p-${index}`,
    imageUrl: page?.image_url || "",
    title: page?.name || "",
    aspectRatio: "auto",
  };
};

FILE: src/utils/errors/AppErrors.ts

export class AppError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public message: string,
    public code: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "AppError";
  }
}

export class AuthError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string = "Sesión expirada o inválida") {
    super(message, "AUTH_ERROR");
    this.name = "AuthError";
  }
}

export class ExtractionError extends AppError {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    code: "UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT" | "NO_METADATA" | "PARSE_FAILED" = "PARSE_FAILED",
  ) {
    super(message, code);
    this.name = "ExtractionError";
  }
}

export class NetworkError extends AppError {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public statusCode?: number,
  ) {
    super(message, "NETWORK_ERROR");
    this.name = "NetworkError";
  }
}

FILE: src/utils/errors/ErrorHandler.ts
================================================
import { AuthError, NetworkError } from "./AppErrors";

export const handleError = (err: unknown, context: string): string => {
  console.error(`[${context}] Error capturado:`, err);

  if (err instanceof AuthError) {
    console.warn("🔐 Acceso denegado o sesión caducada.");

    if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
      const currentPath = window.location.pathname;

      if (!currentPath.includes("/login") && !currentPath.includes("/join")) {
        console.warn("Redirigiendo al login...");

        const localeMatch = currentPath.match(/^\/([a-z]{2})\//);
        const localePrefix = localeMatch ? `/${localeMatch[1]}` : "";
        window.location.href = `${localePrefix}/login`;
      }
    }
    return "Sesión expirada. Por favor, inicia sesión de nuevo.";
  }

  if (err instanceof NetworkError) {
    return `Error de conexión (${err.statusCode}): ${err.message}`;
  }

  if (err instanceof Error) {
    return err.message;
  }

  return "Error inesperado en el sistema.";
};



================================================
FILE: src/utils/hooks/useClickOutside.ts
================================================
import { useEffect, RefObject } from "react";
export function useClickOutside(ref: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>, handler: () => void) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const listener = (event: MouseEvent | TouchEvent) => {
      if (!ref.current || ref.current.contains(event.target as Node)) return;
      handler();
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", listener);
    document.addEventListener("touchstart", listener);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", listener);
      document.removeEventListener("touchstart", listener);
    };
  }, [ref, handler]);
}

FILE: src/utils/hooks/useTheme.ts
================================================
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

export function useTheme() {
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState<boolean>(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem("theme");
    const prefersDark = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches;

    if (savedTheme === "dark" || (!savedTheme && prefersDark)) {
      setIsDark(true);
      document.documentElement.classList.add("dark");
    } else {
      setIsDark(false);
      document.documentElement.classList.remove("dark");
    }
  }, []);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    const newValue = !isDark;
    setIsDark(newValue);

    if (newValue) {
      document.documentElement.classList.add("dark");
      localStorage.setItem("theme", "dark");
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove("dark");
      localStorage.setItem("theme", "light");
    }
  };

  return { isDark, toggleTheme };
}

import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";

test.describe("Flujo de Autenticación Crítica (E2E Real)", () => {
  test("El administrador puede hacer login y acceder al Terminal", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("http://localhost:3000/es/login", { waitUntil: "networkidle" });

    await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "EMAIL O USUARIO" }).fill("admin_e2e");
    await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "••••••••" }).fill("E2E_Secure_Pass_123!");

    const loginButton = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Entrar" });
    await expect(loginButton).toBeEnabled();
    await loginButton.click();

    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/.*\/studio/, { timeout: 15000 });
  });

  test("Un usuario no puede entrar con credenciales falsas", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("http://localhost:3000/es/login", { waitUntil: "networkidle" });

    await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "EMAIL O USUARIO" }).fill("admin_e2e");
    await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "••••••••" }).fill("ClaveFalsa123");

    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Entrar" }).click();

    const errorMessage = page.getByRole("status");
    await expect(errorMessage).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 });
  });
});

import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test('has title', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('https://playwright.dev/');

  // Expect a title "to contain" a substring.
  await expect(page).toHaveTitle(/Playwright/);

test('get started link', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('https://playwright.dev/');

  // Click the get started link.
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Get started' }).click();

  // Expects page to have a heading with the name of Installation.
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Installation' })).toBeVisible();
});

FILE: tests/reverse_engineering.spec.ts
================================================
import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";

test.describe("Ingeniería Inversa: Pruebas de Resiliencia", () => {
  test("A. Fallo de UX en Rate Limit (429)", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/es/login");

    // Simulamos un atacante: 6 intentos rápidos
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      await page.getByPlaceholder("EMAIL O USUARIO").fill("hacker_bot");
      await page.getByPlaceholder("••••••••").fill("123456");
      await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Entrar/i }).click();
    }

    // EL TEST FALLARÁ AQUÍ:
    // Actualmente verás un error genérico o nada.
    // Buscamos el mensaje específico que configuramos en Redis (Backend).
    const toast = page.locator("text=La cuenta 'hacker_bot' está bloqueada");
    await expect(toast).toBeVisible({ timeout: 5000 });
  });

  test("B. Fallo de Optimización (Falsa Prioridad)", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/es");

    // Verificamos si la primera imagen del feed es un componente optimizado de Next.js
    const firstImage = page.locator("img").first();

    // EL TEST FALLARÁ AQUÍ:
    // Las imágenes de Next.js usan 'srcset' con tamaños optimizados y atributos data-nimg.
    // Tu img actual fallará esta comprobación de arquitectura profesional.
    const isNextImage = await firstImage.getAttribute("data-nimg");
    expect(isNextImage).not.toBeNull();
  });

  test("C. Consistencia de Estado tras Logout forzado", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/es/login");
    // ... asumimos login exitoso ...

    await page.evaluate(() => {
      // Forzamos un estado de "Sesión Muerta" en el cliente
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("auth:unauthorized"));
    });

    // EL TEST FALLARÁ AQUÍ:
    // Al forzar el error, el sistema debería redirigir a /login y LIMPIAR el storage.
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login/);
    const storage = await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("auth-storage"));
    expect(JSON.parse(storage!).state.user).toBeNull();
  });
});

FILE: tests/security.spec.ts
================================================
import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";

test.describe("🛡️ Batería de Pruebas de Seguridad Defensiva", () => {
  test("Defensa contra Inyección SQL Clásica en el Login", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("http://localhost:3000/es/login", { waitUntil: "networkidle" });

    const sqliPayload = "' OR 1=1 --";

    await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "EMAIL O USUARIO" }).fill(sqliPayload);
    await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "••••••••" }).fill("cualquier_cosa");

    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Entrar" }).click();

    const errorMessage = page.getByRole("status");
    await expect(errorMessage).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 });

    const response = await page.request.get("http://localhost:8000/api/auth/me");
    expect(response.status()).not.toBe(500);
  });

  test("Defensa contra XSS (Cross-Site Scripting) en inputs", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("http://localhost:3000/es/login");

    const xssPayload = "<script>alert('hackeado')</script>";

    await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "EMAIL O USUARIO" }).fill(xssPayload);
    await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "••••••••" }).fill("password");

    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Entrar" }).click();

    page.on("dialog", (dialog) => {
      throw new Error(
        `¡Vulnerabilidad XSS detectada! Dialog apareció con texto: ${dialog.message()}`,
      );
    });

    const errorMessage = page.getByRole("status");
    await expect(errorMessage).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("Prueba de Rate Limiting (Fuerza Bruta a la API)", async ({ request }) => {
    const maxRequests = 15;
    let rateLimitTriggered = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < maxRequests; i++) {
      const response = await request.post("http://localhost:8000/api/auth/login", {
        form: {
          username: `user_${i}`,
          password: "password_falsa",
        },
      });

      if (response.status() === 429) {
        rateLimitTriggered = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    expect(rateLimitTriggered, "El Rate Limiter no detuvo el ataque de fuerza bruta").toBe(true);
  });

  test("Control de Acceso Roto (Bypass de RBAC) hacia el Studio", async ({ page }) => {
    const response = await page.goto("http://localhost:3000/es/studio");
  });
});

FILE: .github/workflows/playwright.yml
================================================
name: Playwright Tests
on:
  push:
    branches: [ main, master ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main, master ]
jobs:
  test:
    timeout-minutes: 60
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
    - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
      with:
        node-version: lts/*
    - name: Install dependencies
      run: npm ci
    - name: Install Playwright Browsers
      run: npx playwright install --with-deps
    - name: Run Playwright tests
      run: npx playwright test
    - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
      if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
      with:
        name: playwright-report
        path: playwright-report/
        retention-days: 30
